  90: #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  91: pub struct TokenClaims {
  92:     pub subject: String,
  93:     pub issued_at_unix: u64,
  94:     pub expires_at_unix: u64,
  95:     pub nonce: String,
  96: }
  97: 
  98: impl TokenClaims {
  99:     pub fn ct_eq(&self, other: &TokenClaims) -> bool {
 100:         use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;
 101:         self.subject
 102:             .as_bytes()
 103:             .ct_eq(other.subject.as_bytes())
 104:             .unwrap_u8()
 105:             == 1
 106:             && self
 107:                 .nonce
 108:                 .as_bytes()
 109:                 .ct_eq(other.nonce.as_bytes())
 110:                 .unwrap_u8()
 111:                 == 1
 112:             && self.issued_at_unix == other.issued_at_unix
 113:             && self.expires_at_unix == other.expires_at_unix
 114:     }
 115: }
 116: 
 117: impl fmt::Debug for TokenClaims {
 118:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 119:         f.debug_struct("TokenClaims")
 120:             .field("subject", &"REDACTED")
 121:             .field("issued_at_unix", &self.issued_at_unix)
 122:             .field("expires_at_unix", &self.expires_at_unix)
 123:             .field("nonce", &"REDACTED")
 124:             .finish()
 125:     }
 126: }
 127: 
 128: #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 129: pub struct SignedTokenClaims {
 130:     pub claims: TokenClaims,
 131:     pub signature_hex: String,
 132: }
 133: 
 134: impl SignedTokenClaims {
 135:     pub fn ct_eq(&self, other: &SignedTokenClaims) -> bool {
 136:         use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;
 137:         self.claims.ct_eq(&other.claims)
 138:             && self
 139:                 .signature_hex
 140:                 .as_bytes()
 141:                 .ct_eq(other.signature_hex.as_bytes())
 142:                 .unwrap_u8()
 143:                 == 1
 144:     }
 145: }
 146: 
 147: impl fmt::Debug for SignedTokenClaims {
 148:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 149:         f.debug_struct("SignedTokenClaims")
 150:             .field("claims", &self.claims)
 151:             .field("signature_hex", &"REDACTED")
 152:             .finish()
 153:     }
 154: }

 159:     Tls13,
 160: }

 162: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 163: pub struct ControlPlaneTransportPolicy {
 164:     pub minimum_tls: ControlPlaneTlsVersion,
 165:     pub require_signed_control_data: bool,
 166: }
 167: 
 168: impl Default for ControlPlaneTransportPolicy {
 169:     fn default() -> Self {
 170:         Self {
 171:             minimum_tls: ControlPlaneTlsVersion::Tls13,
 172:             require_signed_control_data: true,
 173:         }
 174:     }
 175: }
 176: 
 177: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 178: pub enum TransportPolicyError {
 179:     TlsVersionRejected,
 180:     UnsignedControlDataRejected,
 181: }
 182: 
 183: impl fmt::Display for TransportPolicyError {
 184:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 185:         match self {
 186:             TransportPolicyError::TlsVersionRejected => f.write_str("tls version rejected"),
 187:             TransportPolicyError::UnsignedControlDataRejected => {
 188:                 f.write_str("unsigned control data rejected")
 189:             }
 190:         }
 191:     }
 192: }
 193: 
 194: impl std::error::Error for TransportPolicyError {}
 195: 
 196: impl ControlPlaneTransportPolicy {
 197:     pub fn validate_negotiated_tls(
 198:         &self,
 199:         negotiated: ControlPlaneTlsVersion,
 200:     ) -> Result<(), TransportPolicyError> {
 201:         if self.minimum_tls == ControlPlaneTlsVersion::Tls13
 202:             && negotiated != ControlPlaneTlsVersion::Tls13
 203:         {
 204:             return Err(TransportPolicyError::TlsVersionRejected);
 205:         }
 206:         Ok(())
 207:     }
 208: 
 209:     pub fn validate_control_data_signature(
 210:         &self,
 211:         signature_present: bool,
 212:     ) -> Result<(), TransportPolicyError> {
 213:         if self.require_signed_control_data && !signature_present {
 214:             return Err(TransportPolicyError::UnsignedControlDataRejected);
 215:         }
 216:         Ok(())
 217:     }
 218: }
 219: 
 220: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 221: pub struct AbuseAlert {
 222:     pub source_ip: String,
 223:     pub identity: String,
 224:     pub endpoint: String,
 225:     pub reason: String,
 226:     pub timestamp_unix: u64,
 227: }
 228: 
 229: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 230: pub struct AbuseAlertPolicy {
 231:     pub threshold: u32,
 232:     pub window_secs: u64,
 233: }
 234: 
 235: impl Default for AbuseAlertPolicy {
 236:     fn default() -> Self {
 237:         Self {
 238:             threshold: 5,
 239:             window_secs: 60,
 240:         }
 241:     }
 242: }
 243: 
 244: #[derive(Debug, Default)]
 245: pub struct ApiAbuseMonitor {
 246:     policy: AbuseAlertPolicy,
 247:     failures: HashMap<(String, String, String), Vec<u64>>,
 248:     alerts: Mutex<Vec<AbuseAlert>>,
 249: }
 250: 
 251: impl ApiAbuseMonitor {
 252:     pub fn new(policy: AbuseAlertPolicy) -> Self {
 253:         Self {
 254:             policy,
 255:             failures: HashMap::new(),
 256:             alerts: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
 257:         }
 258:     }
 259: 
 260:     pub fn record_failure(
 261:         &mut self,
 262:         source_ip: &str,
 263:         identity: &str,
 264:         endpoint: &str,
 265:         reason: &str,
 266:         now_unix: u64,
 267:     ) -> Result<bool, AuthError> {
 268:         let key = (
 269:             source_ip.to_string(),
 270:             identity.to_string(),
 271:             endpoint.to_string(),
 272:         );
 273:         let history = self.failures.entry(key).or_default();
 274:         let window_start = now_unix.saturating_sub(self.policy.window_secs);
 275:         history.retain(|entry| *entry >= window_start);
 276:         history.push(now_unix);
 277: 
 278:         if history.len() as u32 >= self.policy.threshold {
 279:             let mut guard = self.alerts.lock().map_err(|_| AuthError::Internal)?;
 280:             guard.push(AbuseAlert {
 281:                 source_ip: source_ip.to_string(),
 282:                 identity: identity.to_string(),
 283:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
 284:                 reason: reason.to_string(),
 285:                 timestamp_unix: now_unix,
 286:             });
 287:             return Ok(true);
 288:         }
 289: 
 290:         Ok(false)
 291:     }
 292: 
 293:     pub fn alerts(&self) -> Result<Vec<AbuseAlert>, AuthError> {
 294:         let guard = self.alerts.lock().map_err(|_| AuthError::Internal)?;
 295:         Ok(guard.clone())
 296:     }
 297: }
 298: 
 299: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 300: pub enum AuthError {
 301:     RateLimited,
 302:     LockedOutUntil(u64),
 303:     ReplayDetected,
 304:     InvalidTokenLifetime,
 305:     TokenSignatureInvalid,
 306:     TokenExpired,
 307:     TokenNotYetValid,
 308:     Internal,
 309: }
 310: 
 311: impl fmt::Display for AuthError {
 312:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 313:         match self {
 314:             AuthError::RateLimited => f.write_str("rate limited"),
 315:             AuthError::LockedOutUntil(ts) => write!(f, "identity locked out until {ts}"),
 316:             AuthError::ReplayDetected => f.write_str("replay detected"),
 317:             AuthError::InvalidTokenLifetime => f.write_str("invalid token lifetime"),
 318:             AuthError::TokenSignatureInvalid => f.write_str("token signature invalid"),
 319:             AuthError::TokenExpired => f.write_str("token expired"),
 320:             AuthError::TokenNotYetValid => f.write_str("token not yet valid"),
 321:             AuthError::Internal => f.write_str("internal auth guard error"),
 322:         }
 323:     }
 324: }
 325: 
 326: impl std::error::Error for AuthError {}
 327: 
 328: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
 329: struct Bucket {
 330:     tokens: f64,
 331:     last_refill_unix: u64,
 332: }
 333: 
 334: impl Bucket {
 335:     fn new(capacity: u32, now_unix: u64) -> Self {
 336:         Self {
 337:             tokens: f64::from(capacity),
 338:             last_refill_unix: now_unix,
 339:         }
 340:     }
 341: 
 342:     fn consume(
 343:         &mut self,
 344:         now_unix: u64,
 345:         capacity: u32,
 346:         refill_per_second: f64,
 347:     ) -> Result<(), AuthError> {
 348:         let elapsed = now_unix.saturating_sub(self.last_refill_unix);
 349:         self.last_refill_unix = now_unix;
 350: 
 351:         let refill = (elapsed as f64) * refill_per_second;
 352:         self.tokens = (self.tokens + refill).min(f64::from(capacity));
 353: 
 354:         if self.tokens < 1.0 {
 355:             return Err(AuthError::RateLimited);
 356:         }
 357: 
 358:         self.tokens -= 1.0;
 359:         Ok(())
 360:     }
 361: }
 362: 
 363: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
 364: struct LockoutState {
 365:     consecutive_failures: u32,
 366:     locked_until_unix: u64,
 367: }
 368: 
 369: impl LockoutState {
 370:     fn new() -> Self {
 371:         Self {
 372:             consecutive_failures: 0,
 373:             locked_until_unix: 0,
 374:         }
 375:     }
 376: }
 377: 
 378: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
 379: pub struct SecurityEvent {
 380:     pub endpoint: String,
 381:     pub source_ip: String,
 382:     pub identity: String,
 383:     pub failure_class: String,
 384:     pub limiter_decision: String,
 385:     pub timestamp_unix: u64,
 386: }
 387: 
 388: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
 389: pub struct AuthSurfaceGuard {
 390:     rate_config: AuthRateLimitConfig,
 391:     lockout_config: LockoutConfig,
 392:     replay_policy: ReplayPolicy,
 393:     ip_buckets: HashMap<String, Bucket>,
 394:     identity_buckets: HashMap<String, Bucket>,
 395:     lockouts: HashMap<String, LockoutState>,
 396:     seen_nonces: HashMap<String, u64>,
 397:     event_log: Arc<Mutex<Vec<SecurityEvent>>>,
 398: }
 399: 
 400: impl Default for AuthSurfaceGuard {
 401:     fn default() -> Self {
 402:         Self::new(
 403:             AuthRateLimitConfig::default(),
 404:             LockoutConfig::default(),
 405:             ReplayPolicy::default(),
 406:         )
 407:     }
 408: }
 409: 
 410: impl AuthSurfaceGuard {
 411:     pub fn new(
 412:         rate_config: AuthRateLimitConfig,
 413:         lockout_config: LockoutConfig,
 414:         replay_policy: ReplayPolicy,
 415:     ) -> Self {
 416:         Self {
 417:             rate_config,
 418:             lockout_config,
 419:             replay_policy,
 420:             ip_buckets: HashMap::new(),
 421:             identity_buckets: HashMap::new(),
 422:             lockouts: HashMap::new(),
 423:             seen_nonces: HashMap::new(),
 424:             event_log: Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new())),
 425:         }
 426:     }
 427: 
 428:     pub fn authorize_attempt(
 429:         &mut self,
 430:         endpoint: &str,
 431:         source_ip: &str,
 432:         identity: &str,
 433:         now_unix: u64,
 434:     ) -> Result<(), AuthError> {
 435:         let locked_until = {
 436:             let lockout = self
 437:                 .lockouts
 438:                 .entry(identity.to_string())
 439:                 .or_insert_with(LockoutState::new);
 440:             lockout.locked_until_unix
 441:         };
 442: 
 443:         if now_unix < locked_until {
 444:             self.record_event(SecurityEvent {
 445:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
 446:                 source_ip: source_ip.to_string(),
 447:                 identity: identity.to_string(),
 448:                 failure_class: "lockout".to_string(),
 449:                 limiter_decision: "denied_locked".to_string(),
 450:                 timestamp_unix: now_unix,
 451:             })?;
 452:             return Err(AuthError::LockedOutUntil(locked_until));
 453:         }
 454: 
 455:         let ip_bucket = self
 456:             .ip_buckets
 457:             .entry(source_ip.to_string())
 458:             .or_insert_with(|| Bucket::new(self.rate_config.ip_burst, now_unix));
 459:         ip_bucket.consume(
 460:             now_unix,
 461:             self.rate_config.ip_burst,
 462:             f64::from(self.rate_config.ip_refill_per_minute) / 60.0,
 463:         )?;
 464: 
 465:         let identity_bucket = self
 466:             .identity_buckets
 467:             .entry(identity.to_string())
 468:             .or_insert_with(|| Bucket::new(self.rate_config.identity_burst, now_unix));
 469:         identity_bucket.consume(
 470:             now_unix,
 471:             self.rate_config.identity_burst,
 472:             f64::from(self.rate_config.identity_refill_per_15_min) / (15.0 * 60.0),
 473:         )?;
 474: 
 475:         Ok(())
 476:     }
 477: 
 478:     pub fn register_failure(&mut self, identity: &str, now_unix: u64) {
 479:         let lockout = self
 480:             .lockouts
 481:             .entry(identity.to_string())
 482:             .or_insert_with(LockoutState::new);
 483: 
 484:         lockout.consecutive_failures = lockout.consecutive_failures.saturating_add(1);
 485:         let exponent = lockout.consecutive_failures.saturating_sub(1).min(30);
 486:         let mut backoff = self
 487:             .lockout_config
 488:             .initial_backoff_secs
 489:             .saturating_mul(2_u64.saturating_pow(exponent));
 490:         backoff = backoff.min(self.lockout_config.max_backoff_secs);
 491:         lockout.locked_until_unix = now_unix.saturating_add(backoff);
 492:     }
 493: 
 494:     pub fn register_success(&mut self, identity: &str) {
 495:         let lockout = self
 496:             .lockouts
 497:             .entry(identity.to_string())
 498:             .or_insert_with(LockoutState::new);
 499:         lockout.consecutive_failures = 0;
 500:         lockout.locked_until_unix = 0;
 501:     }
 502: 
 503:     pub fn validate_token_and_nonce(
 504:         &mut self,
 505:         claims: &TokenClaims,
 506:         now_unix: u64,
 507:     ) -> Result<(), AuthError> {
 508:         if claims.expires_at_unix <= claims.issued_at_unix {
 509:             return Err(AuthError::InvalidTokenLifetime);
 510:         }
 511: 
 512:         let lifetime = claims.expires_at_unix.saturating_sub(claims.issued_at_unix);
 513:         if lifetime > self.replay_policy.token_lifetime_secs {
 514:             return Err(AuthError::InvalidTokenLifetime);
 515:         }
 516: 
 517:         if now_unix
 518:             > claims
 519:                 .expires_at_unix
 520:                 .saturating_add(self.replay_policy.clock_skew_tolerance_secs)
 521:         {
 522:             return Err(AuthError::TokenExpired);
 523:         }
 524: 
 525:         if now_unix.saturating_add(self.replay_policy.clock_skew_tolerance_secs)
 526:             < claims.issued_at_unix
 527:         {
 528:             return Err(AuthError::TokenNotYetValid);
 529:         }
 530: 
 531:         self.prune_nonce_store(now_unix);
 532: 
 533:         if self.seen_nonces.contains_key(&claims.nonce) {
 534:             return Err(AuthError::ReplayDetected);
 535:         }
 536: 
 537:         self.seen_nonces
 538:             .insert(claims.nonce.clone(), claims.expires_at_unix);
 539:         Ok(())
 540:     }
 541: 
 542:     pub fn security_events(&self) -> Result<Vec<SecurityEvent>, AuthError> {
 543:         let events = self.event_log.lock().map_err(|_| AuthError::Internal)?;
 544:         Ok(events.clone())
 545:     }
 546: 
 547:     fn record_event(&self, event: SecurityEvent) -> Result<(), AuthError> {
 548:         let mut events = self.event_log.lock().map_err(|_| AuthError::Internal)?;
 549:         events.push(event);
 550:         Ok(())
 551:     }
 552: 
 553:     fn prune_nonce_store(&mut self, now_unix: u64) {
 554:         self.seen_nonces.retain(|_, expires_at| {
 555:             expires_at.saturating_add(self.replay_policy.clock_skew_tolerance_secs) >= now_unix
 556:         });
 557:     }
 558: }
 559: 
 560: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 561: pub enum ThrowawayCredentialState {
 562:     Created,
 563:     Used,
 564:     Expired,
 565:     Revoked,
 566: }
 567: 
 568: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 569: pub enum CredentialKind {
 570:     Throwaway,
 571:     Reusable,
 572: }
 573: 
 574: #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 575: pub struct ThrowawayCredential {
 576:     pub id: String,
 577:     pub creator: String,
 578:     pub scope: String,
 579:     pub kind: CredentialKind,
 580:     pub storage_policy: String,
 581:     pub created_at_unix: u64,
 582:     pub expires_at_unix: u64,
 583:     pub max_uses: u8,
 584:     pub uses: u8,
 585:     pub state: ThrowawayCredentialState,
 586: }
 587: 
 588: impl fmt::Debug for ThrowawayCredential {
 589:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 590:         f.debug_struct("ThrowawayCredential")
 591:             .field("id", &"REDACTED")
 592:             .field("creator", &self.creator)
 593:             .field("scope", &self.scope)
 594:             .field("kind", &self.kind)
 595:             .field("storage_policy", &"REDACTED")
 596:             .field("created_at_unix", &self.created_at_unix)
 597:             .field("expires_at_unix", &self.expires_at_unix)
 598:             .field("max_uses", &self.max_uses)
 599:             .field("uses", &self.uses)
 600:             .field("state", &self.state)
 601:             .finish()
 602:     }
 603: }
 604: 
 605: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 606: pub enum CredentialError {
 607:     AlreadyExists,
 608:     NotFound,
 609:     AlreadyConsumed,
 610:     Expired,
 611:     Revoked,
 612:     InvalidStateTransition,
 613:     ScopeTooBroad,
 614:     TtlTooLong,
 615:     StoragePolicyViolation,
 616:     InvalidMaxUses,
 617:     Internal,
 618: }
 619: 
 620: impl fmt::Display for CredentialError {
 621:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 622:         match self {
 623:             CredentialError::AlreadyExists => f.write_str("credential already exists"),
 624:             CredentialError::NotFound => f.write_str("credential not found"),
 625:             CredentialError::AlreadyConsumed => f.write_str("credential already consumed"),
 626:             CredentialError::Expired => f.write_str("credential expired"),
 627:             CredentialError::Revoked => f.write_str("credential revoked"),
 628:             CredentialError::InvalidStateTransition => f.write_str("invalid lifecycle transition"),
 629:             CredentialError::ScopeTooBroad => f.write_str("reusable credential scope is too broad"),
 630:             CredentialError::TtlTooLong => f.write_str("reusable credential ttl is too long"),
 631:             CredentialError::StoragePolicyViolation => {
 632:                 f.write_str("reusable credential storage policy violation")
 633:             }
 634:             CredentialError::InvalidMaxUses => f.write_str("invalid reusable credential max uses"),
 635:             CredentialError::Internal => f.write_str("internal credential store error"),
 636:         }
 637:     }
 638: }
 639: 
 640: impl std::error::Error for CredentialError {}
 641: 
 642: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 643: pub struct CredentialAuditEvent {
 644:     pub credential_id: String,
 645:     pub from_state: Option<ThrowawayCredentialState>,
 646:     pub to_state: ThrowawayCredentialState,
 647:     pub timestamp_unix: u64,
 648: }
 649: 
 650: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 651: pub struct CredentialRevocationEvent {
 652:     pub credential_id: String,
 653:     pub generation: u64,
 654:     pub revoked_at_unix: u64,
 655: }
 656: 
 657: #[derive(Debug, Default)]
 658: pub struct ThrowawayCredentialStore {
 659:     credentials: Mutex<HashMap<String, ThrowawayCredential>>,
 660:     audit_events: Mutex<Vec<CredentialAuditEvent>>,
 661:     revocation_generation: Mutex<u64>,
 662:     revocation_events: Mutex<Vec<CredentialRevocationEvent>>,
 663: }
 664: 
 665: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 666: pub struct ReusableCredentialPolicy {
 667:     pub max_ttl_secs: u64,
 668:     pub min_max_uses: u8,
 669:     pub max_max_uses: u8,
 670: }
 671: 
 672: impl Default for ReusableCredentialPolicy {
 673:     fn default() -> Self {
 674:         Self {
 675:             max_ttl_secs: 24 * 60 * 60,
 676:             min_max_uses: 2,
 677:             max_max_uses: 25,
 678:         }
 679:     }
 680: }
 681: 
 682: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 683: pub struct ReusableCredentialRequest {
 684:     pub id: String,
 685:     pub creator: String,
 686:     pub scope: String,
 687:     pub created_at_unix: u64,
 688:     pub ttl_secs: u64,
 689:     pub max_uses: u8,
 690:     pub storage_reference: String,
 691: }
 692: 
 693: impl ThrowawayCredentialStore {
 694:     pub fn create(
 695:         &self,
 696:         id: String,
 697:         creator: String,
 698:         scope: String,
 699:         created_at_unix: u64,
 700:         ttl_secs: u64,
 701:     ) -> Result<ThrowawayCredential, CredentialError> {
 702:         let expires_at_unix = created_at_unix.saturating_add(ttl_secs);
 703:         let credential = ThrowawayCredential {
 704:             id: id.clone(),
 705:             creator,
 706:             scope,
 707:             kind: CredentialKind::Throwaway,
 708:             storage_policy: "throwaway_default".to_string(),
 709:             created_at_unix,
 710:             expires_at_unix,
 711:             max_uses: 1,
 712:             uses: 0,
 713:             state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Created,
 714:         };
 715: 
 716:         let mut guard = self
 717:             .credentials
 718:             .lock()
 719:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 720:         if guard.contains_key(&id) {
 721:             return Err(CredentialError::AlreadyExists);
 722:         }
 723: 
 724:         guard.insert(id.clone(), credential.clone());
 725:         drop(guard);
 726: 
 727:         self.record_audit_event(CredentialAuditEvent {
 728:             credential_id: id,
 729:             from_state: None,
 730:             to_state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Created,
 731:             timestamp_unix: created_at_unix,
 732:         })?;
 733: 
 734:         Ok(credential)
 735:     }
 736: 
 737:     pub fn create_reusable(
 738:         &self,
 739:         request: ReusableCredentialRequest,
 740:         policy: ReusableCredentialPolicy,
 741:     ) -> Result<ThrowawayCredential, CredentialError> {
 742:         if !is_strict_automation_scope(&request.scope) {
 743:             return Err(CredentialError::ScopeTooBroad);
 744:         }
 745:         if request.ttl_secs > policy.max_ttl_secs {
 746:             return Err(CredentialError::TtlTooLong);
 747:         }
 748:         if request.max_uses < policy.min_max_uses || request.max_uses > policy.max_max_uses {
 749:             return Err(CredentialError::InvalidMaxUses);
 750:         }
 751:         if !request.storage_reference.starts_with("vault://") {
 752:             return Err(CredentialError::StoragePolicyViolation);
 753:         }
 754: 
 755:         let expires_at_unix = request.created_at_unix.saturating_add(request.ttl_secs);
 756:         let credential = ThrowawayCredential {
 757:             id: request.id.clone(),
 758:             creator: request.creator,
 759:             scope: request.scope,
 760:             kind: CredentialKind::Reusable,
 761:             storage_policy: request.storage_reference,
 762:             created_at_unix: request.created_at_unix,
 763:             expires_at_unix,
 764:             max_uses: request.max_uses,
 765:             uses: 0,
 766:             state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Created,
 767:         };
 768: 
 769:         let mut guard = self
 770:             .credentials
 771:             .lock()
 772:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 773:         if guard.contains_key(&request.id) {
 774:             return Err(CredentialError::AlreadyExists);
 775:         }
 776: 
 777:         guard.insert(request.id.clone(), credential.clone());
 778:         drop(guard);
 779: 
 780:         self.record_audit_event(CredentialAuditEvent {
 781:             credential_id: request.id,
 782:             from_state: None,
 783:             to_state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Created,
 784:             timestamp_unix: request.created_at_unix,
 785:         })?;
 786: 
 787:         Ok(credential)
 788:     }
 789: 
 790:     pub fn consume(&self, id: &str, now_unix: u64) -> Result<ThrowawayCredential, CredentialError> {
 791:         let mut guard = self
 792:             .credentials
 793:             .lock()
 794:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 795:         let credential = guard.get_mut(id).ok_or(CredentialError::NotFound)?;
 796: 
 797:         match credential.state {
 798:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Created => {}
 799:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Used => return Err(CredentialError::AlreadyConsumed),
 800:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Expired => return Err(CredentialError::Expired),
 801:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Revoked => return Err(CredentialError::Revoked),
 802:         }
 803: 
 804:         if now_unix > credential.expires_at_unix {
 805:             credential.state = ThrowawayCredentialState::Expired;
 806:             drop(guard);
 807:             self.record_audit_event(CredentialAuditEvent {
 808:                 credential_id: id.to_string(),
 809:                 from_state: Some(ThrowawayCredentialState::Created),
 810:                 to_state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Expired,
 811:                 timestamp_unix: now_unix,
 812:             })?;
 813:             return Err(CredentialError::Expired);
 814:         }
 815: 
 816:         if credential.uses >= credential.max_uses {
 817:             credential.state = ThrowawayCredentialState::Used;
 818:             return Err(CredentialError::AlreadyConsumed);
 819:         }
 820: 
 821:         credential.uses = credential.uses.saturating_add(1);
 822:         credential.state = if credential.uses >= credential.max_uses {
 823:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Used
 824:         } else {
 825:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Created
 826:         };
 827:         let to_state = credential.state;
 828:         let snapshot = credential.clone();
 829:         drop(guard);
 830: 
 831:         self.record_audit_event(CredentialAuditEvent {
 832:             credential_id: id.to_string(),
 833:             from_state: Some(ThrowawayCredentialState::Created),
 834:             to_state,
 835:             timestamp_unix: now_unix,
 836:         })?;
 837: 
 838:         Ok(snapshot)
 839:     }
 840: 
 841:     pub fn revoke(&self, id: &str, now_unix: u64) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
 842:         let mut guard = self
 843:             .credentials
 844:             .lock()
 845:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 846:         let credential = guard.get_mut(id).ok_or(CredentialError::NotFound)?;
 847: 
 848:         match credential.state {
 849:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Created => {
 850:                 credential.state = ThrowawayCredentialState::Revoked;
 851:             }
 852:             ThrowawayCredentialState::Used
 853:             | ThrowawayCredentialState::Expired
 854:             | ThrowawayCredentialState::Revoked => {
 855:                 return Err(CredentialError::InvalidStateTransition);
 856:             }
 857:         }
 858: 
 859:         drop(guard);
 860:         let generation = self.increment_revocation_generation()?;
 861:         self.record_revocation_event(CredentialRevocationEvent {
 862:             credential_id: id.to_string(),
 863:             generation,
 864:             revoked_at_unix: now_unix,
 865:         })?;
 866:         self.record_audit_event(CredentialAuditEvent {
 867:             credential_id: id.to_string(),
 868:             from_state: Some(ThrowawayCredentialState::Created),
 869:             to_state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Revoked,
 870:             timestamp_unix: now_unix,
 871:         })?;
 872: 
 873:         Ok(())
 874:     }
 875: 
 876:     pub fn expire_stale(&self, now_unix: u64) -> Result<usize, CredentialError> {
 877:         let mut guard = self
 878:             .credentials
 879:             .lock()
 880:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 881:         let mut expired_ids = Vec::new();
 882: 
 883:         for (id, credential) in &mut *guard {
 884:             if credential.state == ThrowawayCredentialState::Created
 885:                 && now_unix > credential.expires_at_unix
 886:             {
 887:                 credential.state = ThrowawayCredentialState::Expired;
 888:                 expired_ids.push(id.clone());
 889:             }
 890:         }
 891:         drop(guard);
 892: 
 893:         for id in &expired_ids {
 894:             self.record_audit_event(CredentialAuditEvent {
 895:                 credential_id: id.clone(),
 896:                 from_state: Some(ThrowawayCredentialState::Created),
 897:                 to_state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Expired,
 898:                 timestamp_unix: now_unix,
 899:             })?;
 900:         }
 901: 
 902:         Ok(expired_ids.len())
 903:     }
 904: 
 905:     pub fn get(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<ThrowawayCredential>, CredentialError> {
 906:         let guard = self
 907:             .credentials
 908:             .lock()
 909:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 910:         Ok(guard.get(id).cloned())
 911:     }
 912: 
 913:     pub fn audit_events(&self) -> Result<Vec<CredentialAuditEvent>, CredentialError> {
 914:         let events = self
 915:             .audit_events
 916:             .lock()
 917:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 918:         Ok(events.clone())
 919:     }
 920: 
 921:     fn record_audit_event(&self, event: CredentialAuditEvent) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
 922:         let mut guard = self
 923:             .audit_events
 924:             .lock()
 925:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 926:         guard.push(event);
 927:         Ok(())
 928:     }
 929: 
 930:     fn increment_revocation_generation(&self) -> Result<u64, CredentialError> {
 931:         let mut guard = self
 932:             .revocation_generation
 933:             .lock()
 934:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 935:         *guard = guard.saturating_add(1);
 936:         Ok(*guard)
 937:     }
 938: 
 939:     fn record_revocation_event(
 940:         &self,
 941:         event: CredentialRevocationEvent,
 942:     ) -> Result<(), CredentialError> {
 943:         let mut guard = self
 944:             .revocation_events
 945:             .lock()
 946:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 947:         guard.push(event);
 948:         Ok(())
 949:     }
 950: 
 951:     pub fn revocation_generation(&self) -> Result<u64, CredentialError> {
 952:         let guard = self
 953:             .revocation_generation
 954:             .lock()
 955:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 956:         Ok(*guard)
 957:     }
 958: 
 959:     pub fn revocations_since(
 960:         &self,
 961:         generation: u64,
 962:     ) -> Result<Vec<CredentialRevocationEvent>, CredentialError> {
 963:         let guard = self
 964:             .revocation_events
 965:             .lock()
 966:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 967:         Ok(guard
 968:             .iter()
 969:             .filter(|event| event.generation > generation)
 970:             .cloned()
 971:             .collect())
 972:     }
 973: 
 974:     pub fn is_revoked(&self, id: &str) -> Result<bool, CredentialError> {
 975:         let guard = self
 976:             .credentials
 977:             .lock()
 978:             .map_err(|_| CredentialError::Internal)?;
 979:         Ok(matches!(
 980:             guard.get(id),
 981:             Some(ThrowawayCredential {
 982:                 state: ThrowawayCredentialState::Revoked,
 983:                 ..
 984:             })
 985:         ))
 986:     }
 987: }
 988: 
 989: fn is_strict_automation_scope(scope: &str) -> bool {
 990:     let scope = scope.trim();
 991:     if scope.is_empty() {
 992:         return false;
 993:     }
 994:     if scope.contains('*') || scope.contains(' ') {
 995:         return false;
 996:     }
 997:     scope.starts_with("tag:")
 998: }
 999: 
1000: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
1001: pub struct TrustState {
1002:     pub generation: u64,
1003:     pub signing_fingerprint: String,
1004:     pub updated_at_unix: u64,
1005: }
1006: 
1007: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
1008: pub enum TrustStateError {
1009:     Missing,
1010:     Corrupt,
1011:     InvalidFormat,
1012:     PersistFailure,
1013:     IntegrityMismatch,
1014:     PermissionDenied,
1015:     KeyUnavailable,
1016: }
1017: 
1018: impl fmt::Display for TrustStateError {
1019:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
1020:         match self {
1021:             TrustStateError::Missing => f.write_str("trust state missing"),
1022:             TrustStateError::Corrupt => f.write_str("trust state corrupt"),
1023:             TrustStateError::InvalidFormat => f.write_str("trust state invalid format"),
1024:             TrustStateError::PersistFailure => f.write_str("trust state persist failure"),
1025:             TrustStateError::IntegrityMismatch => f.write_str("trust state integrity mismatch"),
1026:             TrustStateError::PermissionDenied => f.write_str("trust state permission denied"),
1027:             TrustStateError::KeyUnavailable => f.write_str("trust state integrity key unavailable"),
1028:         }
1029:     }
1030: }
1031: 
1032: impl std::error::Error for TrustStateError {}
1033: 
1034: pub fn persist_trust_state(
1035:     path: impl AsRef<Path>,
1036:     state: &TrustState,
1037: ) -> Result<(), TrustStateError> {
1038:     let path = path.as_ref();
1039:     ensure_secure_parent_directory(path)?;
1040:     let key_path = trust_state_key_path(path);
1041:     let key = load_or_create_trust_state_mac_key(&key_path)?;
1042:     let payload = trust_state_payload(state);
1043:     let mac = compute_trust_state_mac(payload.as_bytes(), &key)?;
1044:     let body = format!("{payload}mac={mac}\n");
1045:     atomic_write_secure(path, body.as_bytes(), 0o600)?;
1046:     validate_secure_file(path, "trust state", 0o077)?;
1047:     Ok(())
1048: }
1049: 
1050: pub fn load_trust_state(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<TrustState, TrustStateError> {
1051:     let path = path.as_ref();
1052:     validate_secure_file(path, "trust state", 0o077)?;
1053:     let content = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::Missing)?;
1054:     let mut generation: Option<u64> = None;
1055:     let mut fingerprint: Option<String> = None;
1056:     let mut updated_at: Option<u64> = None;
1057:     let mut version: Option<u8> = None;
1058:     let mut mac: Option<String> = None;
1059: 
1060:     for line in content.lines() {
1061:         let Some((key, value)) = line.split_once('=') else {
1062:             return Err(TrustStateError::InvalidFormat);
1063:         };
1064: 
1065:         match key {
1066:             "version" => {
1067:                 version = value.parse::<u8>().ok();
1068:             }
1069:             "generation" => {
1070:                 generation = value.parse::<u64>().ok();
1071:             }
1072:             "signing_fingerprint" => {
1073:                 fingerprint = Some(value.to_string());
1074:             }
1075:             "updated_at_unix" => {
1076:                 updated_at = value.parse::<u64>().ok();
1077:             }
1078:             "mac" => {
1079:                 mac = Some(value.to_string());
1080:             }
1081:             _ => return Err(TrustStateError::InvalidFormat),
1082:         }
1083:     }
1084:     if version != Some(2) {
1085:         return Err(TrustStateError::InvalidFormat);
1086:     }
1087: 
1088:     let state = TrustState {
1089:         generation: generation.ok_or(TrustStateError::Corrupt)?,
1090:         signing_fingerprint: fingerprint.ok_or(TrustStateError::Corrupt)?,
1091:         updated_at_unix: updated_at.ok_or(TrustStateError::Corrupt)?,
1092:     };
1093: 
1094:     let expected_mac = mac.ok_or(TrustStateError::Corrupt)?;
1095:     let payload = trust_state_payload(&state);
1096:     let key_path = trust_state_key_path(path);
1097:     let key = load_trust_state_mac_key(&key_path)?;
1098:     verify_trust_state_mac(payload.as_bytes(), &key, expected_mac.as_str())?;
1099: 
1100:     Ok(state)
1101: }
1102: 
1103: fn trust_state_payload(state: &TrustState) -> String {
1104:     format!(
1105:         "version=2\ngeneration={}\nsigning_fingerprint={}\nupdated_at_unix={}\n",
1106:         state.generation, state.signing_fingerprint, state.updated_at_unix
1107:     )
1108: }
1109: 
1110: fn trust_state_key_path(path: &Path) -> std::path::PathBuf {
1111:     let mut out = path.as_os_str().to_os_string();
1112:     out.push(".integrity.key");
1113:     std::path::PathBuf::from(out)
1114: }
1115: 
1116: fn load_or_create_trust_state_mac_key(path: &Path) -> Result<[u8; 32], TrustStateError> {
1117:     if path.exists() {
1118:         return load_trust_state_mac_key(path);
1119:     }
1120:     ensure_secure_parent_directory(path)?;
1121:     let mut key = [0u8; 32];
1122:     rand::rngs::OsRng
1123:         .try_fill_bytes(&mut key)
1124:         .map_err(|_| TrustStateError::KeyUnavailable)?;
1125:     let body = format!("{}\n", hex_bytes(&key));
1126:     atomic_write_secure(path, body.as_bytes(), 0o600)?;
1127:     validate_secure_file(path, "trust state integrity key", 0o077)?;
1128:     Ok(key)
1129: }
1130: 
1131: fn load_trust_state_mac_key(path: &Path) -> Result<[u8; 32], TrustStateError> {
1132:     validate_secure_file(path, "trust state integrity key", 0o077)
1133:         .map_err(|_| TrustStateError::KeyUnavailable)?;
1134:     let mut content = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::KeyUnavailable)?;
1135:     let mut key_line = content
1136:         .lines()
1137:         .map(str::trim)
1138:         .find(|line| !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with('#'))
1139:         .ok_or(TrustStateError::KeyUnavailable)?
1140:         .to_string();
1141:     content.zeroize();
1142:     let key = decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(&key_line).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::InvalidFormat)?;
1143:     key_line.zeroize();
1144:     Ok(key)
1145: }
1146: 
1147: fn compute_trust_state_mac(payload: &[u8], key: &[u8; 32]) -> Result<String, TrustStateError> {
1148:     let mut mac =
1149:         Hmac::<Sha256>::new_from_slice(key).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::KeyUnavailable)?;
1150:     mac.update(payload);
1151:     Ok(hex_bytes(mac.finalize().into_bytes().as_slice()))
1152: }
1153: 
1154: fn verify_trust_state_mac(
1155:     payload: &[u8],
1156:     key: &[u8; 32],
1157:     mac_hex: &str,
1158: ) -> Result<(), TrustStateError> {
1159:     let expected =
1160:         decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(mac_hex).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::InvalidFormat)?;
1161:     let mut mac =
1162:         Hmac::<Sha256>::new_from_slice(key).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::KeyUnavailable)?;
1163:     mac.update(payload);
1164:     mac.verify_slice(&expected)
1165:         .map_err(|_| TrustStateError::IntegrityMismatch)?;
1166:     Ok(())
1167: }
1168: 
1169: fn ensure_secure_parent_directory(path: &Path) -> Result<(), TrustStateError> {
1170:     let Some(parent) = path.parent() else {
1171:         return Err(TrustStateError::PersistFailure);
1172:     };
1173:     if parent.exists() {
1174:         let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(parent).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1175:         if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() || !metadata.file_type().is_dir() {
1176:             return Err(TrustStateError::PermissionDenied);
1177:         }
1178:         #[cfg(unix)]
1179:         {
1180:             let metadata = fs::metadata(parent).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1181:             let mode = metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o777;
1182:             if mode & 0o077 != 0 {
1183:                 return Err(TrustStateError::PermissionDenied);
1184:             }
1185:             let owner_uid = metadata.uid();
1186:             let expected_uid = Uid::effective().as_raw();
1187:             if owner_uid != expected_uid {
1188:                 return Err(TrustStateError::PermissionDenied);
1189:             }
1190:         }
1191:         return Ok(());
1192:     }
1193:     fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1194:     #[cfg(unix)]
1195:     {
1196:         fs::set_permissions(parent, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700))
1197:             .map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1198:     }
1199:     Ok(())
1200: }
1201: 
1202: fn atomic_write_secure(
1203:     path: &Path,
1204:     body: &[u8],
1205:     #[cfg_attr(not(unix), allow(unused_variables))] mode: u32,
1206: ) -> Result<(), TrustStateError> {
1207:     ensure_secure_parent_directory(path)?;
1208:     if path.exists() {
1209:         let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(path).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1210:         if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
1211:             return Err(TrustStateError::PermissionDenied);
1212:         }
1213:     }
1214:     let temp_path = path.with_extension(format!(
1215:         "tmp.{}.{}",
1216:         std::process::id(),
1217:         std::time::SystemTime::now()
1218:             .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
1219:             .map(|duration| duration.as_nanos())
1220:             .unwrap_or(0)
1221:     ));
1222:     let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new();
1223:     options.write(true).create_new(true);
1224:     #[cfg(unix)]
1225:     {
1226:         options.mode(mode);
1227:     }
1228:     let mut temp = options
1229:         .open(&temp_path)
1230:         .map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1231:     if temp.write_all(body).is_err() {
1232:         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp_path);
1233:         return Err(TrustStateError::PersistFailure);
1234:     }
1235:     if temp.sync_all().is_err() {
1236:         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp_path);
1237:         return Err(TrustStateError::PersistFailure);
1238:     }
1239:     if fs::rename(&temp_path, path).is_err() {
1240:         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp_path);
1241:         return Err(TrustStateError::PersistFailure);
1242:     }
1243:     if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
1244:         let parent_dir = fs::File::open(parent).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1245:         parent_dir
1246:             .sync_all()
1247:             .map_err(|_| TrustStateError::PersistFailure)?;
1248:     }
1249:     Ok(())
1250: }
1251: 
1252: fn validate_secure_file(
1253:     path: &Path,
1254:     label: &str,
1255:     disallowed_mode_mask: u32,
1256: ) -> Result<(), TrustStateError> {
1257:     let link_metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(path).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::Missing)?;
1258:     if link_metadata.file_type().is_symlink() || !link_metadata.file_type().is_file() {
1259:         return Err(TrustStateError::PermissionDenied);
1260:     }
1261:     #[cfg(unix)]
1262:     {
1263:         let metadata = fs::metadata(path).map_err(|_| TrustStateError::Missing)?;
1264:         let mode = metadata.permissions().mode() & 0o777;
1265:         if mode & disallowed_mode_mask != 0 {
1266:             return Err(TrustStateError::PermissionDenied);
1267:         }
1268:         let owner_uid = metadata.uid();
1269:         let expected_uid = Uid::effective().as_raw();
1270:         if owner_uid != expected_uid {
1271:             return Err(TrustStateError::PermissionDenied);
1272:         }
1273:     }
1274:     let _ = label;
1275:     let _ = disallowed_mode_mask;
1276:     Ok(())
1277: }
1278: 
1279: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
1280: pub enum PolicyDecision {
1281:     Allow,
1282:     Deny,
1283: }
1284: 
1285: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
1286: pub struct PolicyCheckRequest {
1287:     pub source: String,
1288:     pub destination: String,
1289:     pub protocol: String,
1290: }
1291: 
1292: #[derive(Debug, Default)]
1293: pub struct PolicyGuard {
1294:     allow_rules: HashSet<(String, String, String)>,
1295: }
1296: 
1297: impl PolicyGuard {
1298:     pub fn allow(
1299:         &mut self,
1300:         source: impl Into<String>,
1301:         destination: impl Into<String>,
1302:         protocol: impl Into<String>,
1303:     ) {
1304:         self.allow_rules
1305:             .insert((source.into(), destination.into(), protocol.into()));
1306:     }
1307: 
1308:     pub fn evaluate(&self, request: &PolicyCheckRequest) -> PolicyDecision {
1309:         if self.allow_rules.contains(&(
1310:             request.source.clone(),
1311:             request.destination.clone(),
1312:             request.protocol.clone(),
1313:         )) {
1314:             return PolicyDecision::Allow;
1315:         }
1316: 
1317:         PolicyDecision::Deny
1318:     }
1319: }

1343:     }

1348:     }

1353:     }
1354: }

1427: #[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]

1481: /// The only accepted scope value for a relay session token.
1482: /// A token with any other scope value must be rejected at the relay.
1483: pub const RELAY_TOKEN_SCOPE: &str = "forward_ciphertext_only";
1484: 
1485: /// Signed relay session token issued by the control plane.
1486: ///
1487: /// `PartialEq`/`Eq` are intentionally **not** derived to prevent accidental
1488: /// non-constant-time comparisons on secret fields (`nonce`, `relay_id`,
1489: /// `signature`).  Use [`RelaySessionToken::ct_eq`] when comparing tokens
1490: /// for equality.
1491: #[derive(Clone)]
1492: pub struct RelaySessionToken {
1493:     pub node_id: String,
1494:     pub peer_node_id: String,
1495:     pub relay_id: [u8; 16],
1496:     /// Must equal [`RELAY_TOKEN_SCOPE`].  Present in the signed payload.
1497:     pub scope: String,
1498:     pub issued_at_unix: u64,
1499:     pub expires_at_unix: u64,
1500:     pub nonce: [u8; 16],
1501:     pub signature: [u8; 64],
1502: }
1503: 
1504: impl fmt::Debug for RelaySessionToken {
1505:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
1506:         f.debug_struct("RelaySessionToken")
1507:             .field("node_id", &self.node_id)
1508:             .field("peer_node_id", &self.peer_node_id)
1509:             .field("relay_id", &"REDACTED")
1510:             .field("scope", &self.scope)
1511:             .field("issued_at_unix", &self.issued_at_unix)
1512:             .field("expires_at_unix", &self.expires_at_unix)
1513:             .field("nonce", &"REDACTED")
1514:             .field("signature", &"REDACTED")
1515:             .finish()
1516:     }
1517: }
1518: 
1519: impl RelaySessionToken {
1520:     /// Create and sign a new relay session token.
1521:     ///
1522:     /// Scope is fixed to [`RELAY_TOKEN_SCOPE`].  The nonce is drawn from
1523:     /// `OsRng`.  The returned token has a valid signature over its canonical
1524:     /// payload.
1525:     pub fn sign(
1526:         signing_key: &SigningKey,
1527:         node_id: &str,
1528:         peer_node_id: &str,
1529:         relay_id: [u8; 16],
1530:         ttl_secs: u64,
1531:     ) -> Self {
1532:         let now_unix = std::time::SystemTime::now()
1533:             .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
1534:             .expect("system clock must be after UNIX_EPOCH")
1535:             .as_secs();
1536:         let mut nonce = [0u8; 16];
1537:         rand::rngs::OsRng
1538:             .try_fill_bytes(&mut nonce)
1539:             .expect("os randomness unavailable for relay token nonce");
1540:         let mut token = Self {
1541:             node_id: node_id.to_string(),
1542:             peer_node_id: peer_node_id.to_string(),
1543:             relay_id,
1544:             scope: RELAY_TOKEN_SCOPE.to_string(),
1545:             issued_at_unix: now_unix,
1546:             expires_at_unix: now_unix.saturating_add(ttl_secs),
1547:             nonce,
1548:             signature: [0u8; 64],
1549:         };
1550:         let payload = token.canonical_payload();
1551:         let sig = signing_key.sign(payload.as_bytes());
1552:         token.signature = sig.to_bytes();
1553:         token
1554:     }
1555: 
1556:     pub fn verify_signature(&self, verifying_key: &VerifyingKey) -> Result<(), String> {
1557:         let payload = self.canonical_payload();
1558:         let signature = Signature::from_bytes(&self.signature);
1559:         verifying_key
1560:             .verify(payload.as_bytes(), &signature)
1561:             .map_err(|e| format!("signature verification failed: {e}"))?;
1562:         Ok(())
1563:     }
1564: 
1565:     /// Canonical signed payload.  All fields that appear here are covered by
1566:     /// the signature.  **Changing this format is a breaking change.**
1567:     pub fn canonical_payload(&self) -> String {
1568:         format!(
1569:             "version=1\nnode_id={}\npeer_node_id={}\nrelay_id={}\nscope={}\nissued_at_unix={}\nexpires_at_unix={}\nnonce={}\n",
1570:             self.node_id,
1571:             self.peer_node_id,
1572:             hex_bytes(&self.relay_id),
1573:             self.scope,
1574:             self.issued_at_unix,
1575:             self.expires_at_unix,
1576:             hex_bytes(&self.nonce),
1577:         )
1578:     }
1579: 
1580:     pub fn is_expired(&self, now_unix: u64, clock_skew_tolerance_secs: u64) -> bool {
1581:         now_unix
1582:             > self
1583:                 .expires_at_unix
1584:                 .saturating_add(clock_skew_tolerance_secs)
1585:     }
1586: 
1587:     pub fn ttl_secs(&self) -> u64 {
1588:         self.expires_at_unix.saturating_sub(self.issued_at_unix)
1589:     }
1590: 
1591:     /// Constant-time equality check covering all fields, including secret
1592:     /// fields (`nonce`, `relay_id`, `signature`).
1593:     ///
1594:     /// Do **not** use `==` (which is not available; `PartialEq` is not
1595:     /// derived) for auth-path comparisons.
1596:     pub fn ct_eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
1597:         use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;
1598:         let nonce_eq: bool = self.nonce.ct_eq(&other.nonce).into();
1599:         let sig_eq: bool = self.signature.ct_eq(&other.signature).into();
1600:         let relay_eq: bool = self.relay_id.ct_eq(&other.relay_id).into();
1601:         let node_eq: bool = self
1602:             .node_id
1603:             .as_bytes()
1604:             .ct_eq(other.node_id.as_bytes())
1605:             .into();
1606:         let peer_eq: bool = self
1607:             .peer_node_id
1608:             .as_bytes()
1609:             .ct_eq(other.peer_node_id.as_bytes())
1610:             .into();
1611:         let scope_eq: bool = self.scope.as_bytes().ct_eq(other.scope.as_bytes()).into();
1612:         let issued_eq = self.issued_at_unix == other.issued_at_unix;
1613:         let expires_eq = self.expires_at_unix == other.expires_at_unix;
1614:         nonce_eq & sig_eq & relay_eq & node_eq & peer_eq & scope_eq & issued_eq & expires_eq
1615:     }
1616: }
1617:

1680: #[derive(Debug)]
1681: pub struct ControlPlanePersistence {
1682:     store: Mutex<persistence::SqliteStore>,
1683: }
1684: 
1685: impl ControlPlanePersistence {
1686:     pub fn open_sqlite(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self, persistence::PersistenceError> {
1687:         let store = persistence::SqliteStore::open(path)?;
1688:         store.apply_migrations()?;
1689:         Ok(Self {
1690:             store: Mutex::new(store),
1691:         })
1692:     }
1693: 
1694:     pub fn open_in_memory() -> Result<Self, persistence::PersistenceError> {
1695:         let store = persistence::SqliteStore::open_in_memory()?;
1696:         store.apply_migrations()?;
1697:         Ok(Self {
1698:             store: Mutex::new(store),
1699:         })
1700:     }
1701: 
1702:     pub fn upsert_user(
1703:         &self,
1704:         user: &persistence::UserRow,
1705:     ) -> Result<(), persistence::PersistenceError> {
1706:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1707:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1708:         })?;
1709:         guard.upsert_user(user)
1710:     }
1711: 
1712:     pub fn upsert_node(
1713:         &self,
1714:         node: &persistence::NodeRow,
1715:     ) -> Result<(), persistence::PersistenceError> {
1716:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1717:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1718:         })?;
1719:         guard.upsert_node(node)
1720:     }
1721: 
1722:     pub fn insert_credential(
1723:         &self,
1724:         credential: &persistence::CredentialRow,
1725:     ) -> Result<(), persistence::PersistenceError> {
1726:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1727:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1728:         })?;
1729:         guard.insert_credential(credential)
1730:     }
1731: 
1732:     pub fn consume_single_use_credential(
1733:         &self,
1734:         credential_id: &str,
1735:         now_unix: u64,
1736:     ) -> Result<bool, persistence::PersistenceError> {
1737:         let mut guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1738:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1739:         })?;
1740:         guard.consume_single_use_credential(credential_id, now_unix)
1741:     }
1742: 
1743:     pub fn insert_credential_audit_event(
1744:         &self,
1745:         credential_id: &str,
1746:         from_state: Option<&str>,
1747:         to_state: &str,
1748:         event_at_unix: u64,
1749:         actor_user_id: &str,
1750:     ) -> Result<(), persistence::PersistenceError> {
1751:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1752:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1753:         })?;
1754:         guard.insert_credential_audit_event(
1755:             credential_id,
1756:             from_state,
1757:             to_state,
1758:             event_at_unix,
1759:             actor_user_id,
1760:         )
1761:     }
1762: 
1763:     pub fn credential_state(
1764:         &self,
1765:         credential_id: &str,
1766:     ) -> Result<Option<String>, persistence::PersistenceError> {
1767:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1768:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1769:         })?;
1770:         guard.credential_state(credential_id)
1771:     }
1772: 
1773:     pub fn user_exists(&self, user_id: &str) -> Result<bool, persistence::PersistenceError> {
1774:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1775:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1776:         })?;
1777:         guard.user_exists(user_id)
1778:     }
1779: 
1780:     pub fn node_exists(&self, node_id: &str) -> Result<bool, persistence::PersistenceError> {
1781:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1782:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1783:         })?;
1784:         guard.node_exists(node_id)
1785:     }
1786: 
1787:     pub fn credential_audit_event_count(
1788:         &self,
1789:         credential_id: &str,
1790:     ) -> Result<u64, persistence::PersistenceError> {
1791:         let guard = self.store.lock().map_err(|_| {
1792:             persistence::PersistenceError::InvariantViolation("persistence lock poisoned")
1793:         })?;
1794:         guard.credential_audit_event_count(credential_id)
1795:     }
1796: }

1798: #[derive(Debug)]
1799: pub struct ControlPlaneCore {
1800:     pub auth_guard: AuthSurfaceGuard,
1801:     pub credentials: ThrowawayCredentialStore,
1802:     pub nodes: NodeRegistry,
1803:     pub policy: PolicySet,
1804:     transport_policy: ControlPlaneTransportPolicy,
1805:     assignment_signing_key: SigningKey,
1806:     assignment_verifying_key: [u8; 32],
1807:     dns_zone_signing_key: SigningKey,
1808:     dns_zone_verifying_key: [u8; 32],
1809:     endpoint_hint_signing_key: SigningKey,
1810:     pub endpoint_hint_verifying_key: [u8; 32],
1811:     access_token_signing_key: SigningKey,
1812:     access_token_verifying_key: [u8; 32],
1813: }
1814: 
1815: impl ControlPlaneCore {
1816:     pub fn new(mut signing_secret: Vec<u8>, policy: PolicySet) -> Self {
1817:         let mut assignment_seed =
1818:             derive_signing_seed(ASSIGNMENT_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, &signing_secret);
1819:         let mut dns_zone_seed = derive_signing_seed(DNS_ZONE_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, &signing_secret);
1820:         let mut endpoint_hint_seed =
1821:             derive_signing_seed(ENDPOINT_HINT_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, &signing_secret);
1822:         let mut access_token_seed =
1823:             derive_signing_seed(ACCESS_TOKEN_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, &signing_secret);
1824:         signing_secret.zeroize();
1825: 
1826:         let assignment_signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&assignment_seed);
1827:         assignment_seed.zeroize();
1828:         let assignment_verifying_key = *assignment_signing_key.verifying_key().as_bytes();
1829:         let dns_zone_signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&dns_zone_seed);
1830:         dns_zone_seed.zeroize();
1831:         let dns_zone_verifying_key = *dns_zone_signing_key.verifying_key().as_bytes();
1832:         let endpoint_hint_signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&endpoint_hint_seed);
1833:         endpoint_hint_seed.zeroize();
1834:         let endpoint_hint_verifying_key = *endpoint_hint_signing_key.verifying_key().as_bytes();
1835:         let access_token_signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&access_token_seed);
1836:         access_token_seed.zeroize();
1837:         let access_token_verifying_key = *access_token_signing_key.verifying_key().as_bytes();
1838:         Self {
1839:             auth_guard: AuthSurfaceGuard::default(),
1840:             credentials: ThrowawayCredentialStore::default(),
1841:             nodes: NodeRegistry::default(),
1842:             policy,
1843:             transport_policy: ControlPlaneTransportPolicy::default(),
1844:             assignment_signing_key,
1845:             assignment_verifying_key,
1846:             dns_zone_signing_key,
1847:             dns_zone_verifying_key,
1848:             endpoint_hint_signing_key,
1849:             endpoint_hint_verifying_key,
1850:             access_token_signing_key,
1851:             access_token_verifying_key,
1852:         }
1853:     }
1854: 
1855:     pub fn transport_policy(&self) -> ControlPlaneTransportPolicy {
1856:         self.transport_policy
1857:     }
1858: 
1859:     pub fn validate_transport_security(
1860:         &self,
1861:         negotiated_tls: ControlPlaneTlsVersion,
1862:         signature_present: bool,
1863:     ) -> Result<(), TransportPolicyError> {
1864:         self.transport_policy
1865:             .validate_negotiated_tls(negotiated_tls)?;
1866:         self.transport_policy
1867:             .validate_control_data_signature(signature_present)
1868:     }
1869: 
1870:     pub fn enroll_with_throwaway(
1871:         &self,
1872:         request: EnrollmentRequest,
1873:     ) -> Result<EnrollmentResponse, ControlPlaneError> {
1874:         self.credentials
1875:             .consume(&request.credential_id, request.now_unix)
1876:             .map_err(ControlPlaneError::Credential)?;
1877: 
1878:         let node = NodeMetadata {
1879:             node_id: request.node_id.clone(),
1880:             hostname: request.hostname,
1881:             os: request.os,
1882:             tags: request.tags,
1883:             owner: request.owner.clone(),
1884:             endpoint: request.endpoint,
1885:             last_seen_unix: request.now_unix,
1886:             public_key: request.public_key,
1887:         };
1888: 
1889:         self.nodes.upsert(node)?;
1890: 
1891:         let token_claims = TokenClaims {
1892:             subject: request.owner,
1893:             issued_at_unix: request.now_unix,
1894:             expires_at_unix: request
1895:                 .now_unix
1896:                 .saturating_add(ReplayPolicy::default().token_lifetime_secs),
1897:             nonce: random_nonce_hex(16),
1898:         };
1899:         let token = self.sign_access_token(&token_claims);
1900: 
1901:         Ok(EnrollmentResponse {
1902:             node_id: request.node_id,
1903:             access_token: token,
1904:         })
1905:     }
1906: 
1907:     pub fn enroll_with_throwaway_and_persist(
1908:         &self,
1909:         request: EnrollmentRequest,
1910:         persistence: &ControlPlanePersistence,
1911:     ) -> Result<EnrollmentResponse, ControlPlaneError> {
1912:         let consumed = persistence
1913:             .consume_single_use_credential(&request.credential_id, request.now_unix)
1914:             .map_err(ControlPlaneError::Persistence)?;
1915:         if !consumed {
1916:             let credential_state = persistence
1917:                 .credential_state(&request.credential_id)
1918:                 .map_err(ControlPlaneError::Persistence)?;
1919:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Credential(
1920:                 map_persisted_credential_state_to_error(credential_state.as_deref()),
1921:             ));
1922:         }
1923: 
1924:         match self
1925:             .credentials
1926:             .consume(&request.credential_id, request.now_unix)
1927:         {
1928:             Ok(_) | Err(CredentialError::NotFound) => {}
1929:             Err(err) => return Err(ControlPlaneError::Credential(err)),
1930:         }
1931: 
1932:         let node = NodeMetadata {
1933:             node_id: request.node_id.clone(),
1934:             hostname: request.hostname.clone(),
1935:             os: request.os.clone(),
1936:             tags: request.tags.clone(),
1937:             owner: request.owner.clone(),
1938:             endpoint: request.endpoint.clone(),
1939:             last_seen_unix: request.now_unix,
1940:             public_key: request.public_key,
1941:         };
1942:         self.nodes.upsert(node)?;
1943: 
1944:         let token_claims = TokenClaims {
1945:             subject: request.owner.clone(),
1946:             issued_at_unix: request.now_unix,
1947:             expires_at_unix: request
1948:                 .now_unix
1949:                 .saturating_add(ReplayPolicy::default().token_lifetime_secs),
1950:             nonce: random_nonce_hex(16),
1951:         };
1952:         let token = self.sign_access_token(&token_claims);
1953: 
1954:         let owner = request.owner.clone();
1955:         let credential_id = request.credential_id.clone();
1956: 
1957:         let user_row = persistence::UserRow {
1958:             user_id: owner.clone(),
1959:             email: owner.clone(),
1960:             mfa_enabled: false,
1961:             updated_at_unix: request.now_unix,
1962:             created_at_unix: request.now_unix,
1963:         };
1964:         persistence
1965:             .upsert_user(&user_row)
1966:             .map_err(ControlPlaneError::Persistence)?;
1967: 
1968:         let node_row = persistence::NodeRow {
1969:             node_id: request.node_id.clone(),
1970:             owner_user_id: owner.clone(),
1971:             hostname: request.hostname.clone(),
1972:             os: request.os.clone(),
1973:             tags_csv: request.tags.join(","),
1974:             public_key_hex: hex_bytes(&request.public_key),
1975:             last_seen_unix: request.now_unix,
1976:             updated_at_unix: request.now_unix,
1977:             created_at_unix: request.now_unix,
1978:         };
1979:         persistence
1980:             .upsert_node(&node_row)
1981:             .map_err(ControlPlaneError::Persistence)?;
1982: 
1983:         persistence
1984:             .insert_credential_audit_event(
1985:                 &credential_id,
1986:                 Some("created"),
1987:                 "used",
1988:                 request.now_unix,
1989:                 &owner,
1990:             )
1991:             .map_err(ControlPlaneError::Persistence)?;
1992: 
1993:         Ok(EnrollmentResponse {
1994:             node_id: request.node_id,
1995:             access_token: token,
1996:         })
1997:     }
1998: 
1999:     pub fn signed_peer_map(&self, now_unix: u64) -> Result<SignedPeerMap, ControlPlaneError> {
2000:         let mut peers = self.nodes.list()?;
2001:         peers.sort_by(|left, right| left.node_id.cmp(&right.node_id));
2002: 
2003:         let mut payload = String::new();
2004:         for peer in &peers {
2005:             payload.push_str(&format!(
2006:                 "{}|{}|{}|{}|{}|{}\n",
2007:                 peer.node_id,
2008:                 peer.hostname,
2009:                 peer.os,
2010:                 peer.owner,
2011:                 peer.last_seen_unix,
2012:                 hex_bytes(&peer.public_key)
2013:             ));
2014:         }
2015: 
2016:         let signature = self.sign_peer_map_payload(&payload);
2017:         Ok(SignedPeerMap {
2018:             payload,
2019:             signature,
2020:             generated_at_unix: now_unix,
2021:         })
2022:     }
2023: 
2024:     pub fn verify_signed_peer_map(&self, map: &SignedPeerMap) -> bool {
2025:         self.verify_peer_map_signature(&map.payload, &map.signature)
2026:     }

2028:     pub fn assignment_verifier_key_hex(&self) -> String {
2029:         hex_bytes(&self.assignment_verifying_key)
2030:     }

2032:     pub fn dns_zone_verifier_key_hex(&self) -> String {
2033:         hex_bytes(&self.dns_zone_verifying_key)
2034:     }

2036:     pub fn access_token_verifier_key_hex(&self) -> String {
2037:         hex_bytes(&self.access_token_verifying_key)
2038:     }

2040:     pub fn endpoint_hint_verifier_key_hex(&self) -> String {
2041:         hex_bytes(&self.endpoint_hint_verifying_key)
2042:     }
