2044:     pub fn verify_access_token(&self, token: &SignedTokenClaims) -> bool {
2045:         let signature_bytes = match decode_hex_to_fixed::<64>(&token.signature_hex) {
2046:             Ok(bytes) => bytes,
2047:             Err(()) => return false,
2048:         };
2049:         let signature = Signature::from_bytes(&signature_bytes);
2050:         let verifying_key = match VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&self.access_token_verifying_key) {
2051:             Ok(key) => key,
2052:             Err(_) => return false,
2053:         };
2054:         let payload = token_claims_payload(&token.claims);
2055:         verifying_key.verify(payload.as_bytes(), &signature).is_ok()
2056:     }
2057: 
2058:     pub fn validate_signed_token_and_nonce(
2059:         &self,
2060:         guard: &mut AuthSurfaceGuard,
2061:         token: &SignedTokenClaims,
2062:         now_unix: u64,
2063:     ) -> Result<(), AuthError> {
2064:         if !self.verify_access_token(token) {
2065:             return Err(AuthError::TokenSignatureInvalid);
2066:         }
2067:         guard.validate_token_and_nonce(&token.claims, now_unix)
2068:     }
2069: 
2070:     pub fn signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(
2071:         &self,
2072:         request: AutoTunnelBundleRequest,
2073:     ) -> Result<SignedAutoTunnelBundle, ControlPlaneError> {
2074:         if request.ttl_secs == 0 {
2075:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2076:                 "auto-tunnel ttl must be greater than zero".to_string(),
2077:             ));
2078:         }
2079:         if request.ttl_secs > 24 * 60 * 60 {
2080:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2081:                 "auto-tunnel ttl exceeds max supported value".to_string(),
2082:             ));
2083:         }
2084:         if !is_valid_ipv4_or_ipv6_cidr(&request.mesh_cidr) {
2085:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2086:                 "mesh cidr is invalid".to_string(),
2087:             ));
2088:         }
2089: 
2090:         let target = self.nodes.get(&request.node_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
2091:             ControlPlaneError::Assignment("requested node does not exist".to_string())
2092:         })?;
2093:         if target.endpoint.parse::<SocketAddr>().is_err() {
2094:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2095:                 "requested node endpoint is invalid".to_string(),
2096:             ));
2097:         }
2098: 
2099:         let expires_at_unix = request.generated_at_unix.saturating_add(request.ttl_secs);
2100: 
2101:         let mut peers = self.nodes.list()?;
2102:         peers.sort_by(|left, right| left.node_id.cmp(&right.node_id));
2103:         let tunnel_assignments =
2104:             Self::deterministic_tunnel_assignments(peers.iter().map(|peer| peer.node_id.as_str()))?;
2105:         let target_cidr = tunnel_assignments
2106:             .get(target.node_id.as_str())
2107:             .cloned()
2108:             .ok_or_else(|| {
2109:                 ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2110:                     "requested node assignment is unavailable".to_string(),
2111:                 )
2112:             })?;
2113: 
2114:         let mut selected_peers = Vec::new();
2115:         let mut bundle_routes = Vec::new();
2116:         for peer in peers
2117:             .iter()
2118:             .filter(|candidate| candidate.node_id != target.node_id)
2119:         {
2120:             if !self.policy_allows_node_pair(&target, peer) {
2121:                 continue;
2122:             }
2123:             if peer.endpoint.parse::<SocketAddr>().is_err() {
2124:                 continue;
2125:             }
2126: 
2127:             let peer_cidr = tunnel_assignments
2128:                 .get(peer.node_id.as_str())
2129:                 .cloned()
2130:                 .ok_or_else(|| {
2131:                     ControlPlaneError::Assignment(format!(
2132:                         "peer assignment is unavailable: {}",
2133:                         peer.node_id
2134:                     ))
2135:                 })?;
2136:             selected_peers.push((peer.clone(), peer_cidr.clone()));
2137:             bundle_routes.push(AutoTunnelRoute {
2138:                 destination_cidr: peer_cidr,
2139:                 via_node: peer.node_id.clone(),
2140:                 kind: AutoTunnelRouteKind::Mesh,
2141:             });
2142:         }
2143: 
2144:         if let Some(exit_node_id) = request.exit_node_id.as_deref() {
2145:             let exit_node = self.nodes.get(exit_node_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
2146:                 ControlPlaneError::Assignment("exit node does not exist".to_string())
2147:             })?;
2148:             if !self.policy_allows_node_pair(&target, &exit_node) {
2149:                 return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2150:                     "exit node denied by policy".to_string(),
2151:                 ));
2152:             }
2153:             bundle_routes.push(AutoTunnelRoute {
2154:                 destination_cidr: "0.0.0.0/0".to_string(),
2155:                 via_node: exit_node.node_id.clone(),
2156:                 kind: AutoTunnelRouteKind::ExitNodeDefault,
2157:             });
2158:             for cidr in &request.lan_routes {
2159:                 if !is_valid_ipv4_or_ipv6_cidr(cidr) {
2160:                     return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2161:                         "lan route cidr is invalid".to_string(),
2162:                     ));
2163:                 }
2164:                 bundle_routes.push(AutoTunnelRoute {
2165:                     destination_cidr: cidr.clone(),
2166:                     via_node: exit_node.node_id.clone(),
2167:                     kind: AutoTunnelRouteKind::ExitNodeLan,
2168:                 });
2169:             }
2170:         }
2171: 
2172:         let mut peer_allowed_ips = HashMap::<String, BTreeSet<String>>::new();
2173:         for (peer, peer_cidr) in &selected_peers {
2174:             peer_allowed_ips
2175:                 .entry(peer.node_id.clone())
2176:                 .or_default()
2177:                 .insert(peer_cidr.clone());
2178:         }
2179:         for route in &bundle_routes {
2180:             if let Some(allowed_ips) = peer_allowed_ips.get_mut(route.via_node.as_str()) {
2181:                 allowed_ips.insert(route.destination_cidr.clone());
2182:             }
2183:         }
2184: 
2185:         let mut bundle_peers = Vec::with_capacity(selected_peers.len());
2186:         for (peer, _peer_cidr) in selected_peers {
2187:             let allowed_ips = peer_allowed_ips
2188:                 .remove(peer.node_id.as_str())
2189:                 .map(|ips| ips.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>())
2190:                 .unwrap_or_default();
2191:             bundle_peers.push(AutoTunnelPeer {
2192:                 node_id: peer.node_id,
2193:                 endpoint: peer.endpoint,
2194:                 public_key: peer.public_key,
2195:                 allowed_ips,
2196:             });
2197:         }
2198: 
2199:         let payload = serialize_auto_tunnel_payload(
2200:             &AutoTunnelPayloadHeader {
2201:                 node_id: &target.node_id,
2202:                 mesh_cidr: &request.mesh_cidr,
2203:                 assigned_cidr: &target_cidr,
2204:                 generated_at_unix: request.generated_at_unix,
2205:                 expires_at_unix,
2206:                 nonce: request.nonce,
2207:             },
2208:             &bundle_peers,
2209:             &bundle_routes,
2210:         );
2211:         let signature = self.assignment_signing_key.sign(payload.as_bytes());
2212: 
2213:         Ok(SignedAutoTunnelBundle {
2214:             payload,
2215:             signature_hex: hex_bytes(&signature.to_bytes()),
2216:             generated_at_unix: request.generated_at_unix,
2217:             expires_at_unix,
2218:             node_id: request.node_id,
2219:         })
2220:     }
2221: 
2222:     pub fn signed_dns_zone_bundle(
2223:         &self,
2224:         request: SignedDnsZoneBundleRequest,
2225:     ) -> Result<SignedDnsZoneBundle, ControlPlaneError> {
2226:         let subject = self
2227:             .nodes
2228:             .get(&request.subject_node_id)?
2229:             .ok_or_else(|| ControlPlaneError::Dns("subject node does not exist".to_string()))?;
2230: 
2231:         let mut peers = self.nodes.list()?;
2232:         peers.sort_by(|left, right| left.node_id.cmp(&right.node_id));
2233:         let tunnel_assignments =
2234:             Self::deterministic_tunnel_assignments(peers.iter().map(|peer| peer.node_id.as_str()))?;
2235: 
2236:         let mut canonical_records = Vec::with_capacity(request.records.len());
2237: 
2238:         for record in request.records {
2239:             let target = self
2240:                 .nodes
2241:                 .get(&record.target_node_id)?
2242:                 .ok_or_else(|| ControlPlaneError::Dns("target node does not exist".to_string()))?;
2243:             if target.node_id != subject.node_id && !self.policy_allows_node_pair(&subject, &target)
2244:             {
2245:                 return Err(ControlPlaneError::Dns(
2246:                     "dns record target denied by policy".to_string(),
2247:                 ));
2248:             }
2249: 
2250:             let expected_cidr =
2251:                 tunnel_assignments
2252:                     .get(target.node_id.as_str())
2253:                     .ok_or_else(|| {
2254:                         ControlPlaneError::Dns("target node assignment is unavailable".to_string())
2255:                     })?;
2256:             let expected_ip = host_ip_from_host_cidr(expected_cidr.as_str()).ok_or_else(|| {
2257:                 ControlPlaneError::Dns("target node assignment must be a host cidr".to_string())
2258:             })?;
2259:             canonical_records.push(DnsZoneRecordInput {
2260:                 label: record.label,
2261:                 target_node_id: target.node_id.clone(),
2262:                 rr_type: record.rr_type,
2263:                 target_addr_kind: record.target_addr_kind,
2264:                 expected_ip,
2265:                 ttl_secs: record.ttl_secs,
2266:                 aliases: record.aliases,
2267:             });
2268:         }
2269: 
2270:         build_signed_dns_zone_bundle(
2271:             &self.dns_zone_signing_key,
2272:             request.zone_name.as_str(),
2273:             subject.node_id.as_str(),
2274:             request.generated_at_unix,
2275:             request.ttl_secs,
2276:             request.nonce,
2277:             &canonical_records,
2278:         )
2279:         .map_err(map_dns_zone_error)
2280:     }
2281: 
2282:     pub fn signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(
2283:         &self,
2284:         request: EndpointHintBundleRequest,
2285:     ) -> Result<SignedEndpointHintBundle, ControlPlaneError> {
2286:         if request.ttl_secs == 0 {
2287:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2288:                 "endpoint hint ttl must be greater than zero".to_string(),
2289:             ));
2290:         }
2291:         if request.ttl_secs > 120 {
2292:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2293:                 "endpoint hint ttl exceeds max supported value".to_string(),
2294:             ));
2295:         }
2296:         if request.generated_at_unix == 0 {
2297:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2298:                 "generated_at_unix must be greater than zero".to_string(),
2299:             ));
2300:         }
2301:         if request.candidates.is_empty() {
2302:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2303:                 "endpoint hints require at least one candidate".to_string(),
2304:             ));
2305:         }
2306:         if request.candidates.len() > 8 {
2307:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2308:                 "endpoint hints exceed max candidate count".to_string(),
2309:             ));
2310:         }
2311: 
2312:         let source = self.nodes.get(&request.source_node_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
2313:             ControlPlaneError::Traversal("source node does not exist".to_string())
2314:         })?;
2315:         let target = self.nodes.get(&request.target_node_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
2316:             ControlPlaneError::Traversal("target node does not exist".to_string())
2317:         })?;
2318:         if !self.policy_allows_node_pair(&source, &target) {
2319:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2320:                 "endpoint hints denied by policy".to_string(),
2321:             ));
2322:         }
2323: 
2324:         let expires_at_unix = request.generated_at_unix.saturating_add(request.ttl_secs);
2325:         if request.generated_at_unix >= expires_at_unix {
2326:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2327:                 "invalid generated/expires ordering".to_string(),
2328:             ));
2329:         }
2330: 
2331:         let mut seen_candidates = HashSet::new();
2332:         for candidate in &request.candidates {
2333:             let endpoint = candidate.endpoint.parse::<SocketAddr>().map_err(|_| {
2334:                 ControlPlaneError::Traversal("candidate endpoint is invalid".to_string())
2335:             })?;
2336:             if endpoint.port() == 0 {
2337:                 return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2338:                     "candidate endpoint port must be non-zero".to_string(),
2339:                 ));
2340:             }
2341:             if matches!(candidate.candidate_type, EndpointHintCandidateType::Relay) {
2342:                 let relay_id = candidate.relay_id.as_deref().unwrap_or("").trim();
2343:                 if relay_id.is_empty() {
2344:                     return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2345:                         "relay candidates require relay_id".to_string(),
2346:                     ));
2347:                 }
2348:             } else if candidate.relay_id.is_some() {
2349:                 return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2350:                     "relay_id is only valid for relay candidates".to_string(),
2351:                 ));
2352:             }
2353: 
2354:             let relay_key = candidate
2355:                 .relay_id
2356:                 .as_deref()
2357:                 .unwrap_or("")
2358:                 .trim()
2359:                 .to_string();
2360:             let uniqueness = format!(
2361:                 "{}|{}|{}",
2362:                 candidate.candidate_type.as_str(),
2363:                 endpoint,
2364:                 relay_key
2365:             );
2366:             if !seen_candidates.insert(uniqueness) {
2367:                 return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2368:                     "duplicate endpoint hint candidate".to_string(),
2369:                 ));
2370:             }
2371:         }
2372: 
2373:         let payload = serialize_endpoint_hint_payload(
2374:             request.source_node_id.as_str(),
2375:             request.target_node_id.as_str(),
2376:             request.generated_at_unix,
2377:             expires_at_unix,
2378:             request.nonce,
2379:             &request.candidates,
2380:         )?;
2381:         let signature = self.endpoint_hint_signing_key.sign(payload.as_bytes());
2382: 
2383:         Ok(SignedEndpointHintBundle {
2384:             payload,
2385:             signature_hex: hex_bytes(&signature.to_bytes()),
2386:             generated_at_unix: request.generated_at_unix,
2387:             expires_at_unix,
2388:             source_node_id: request.source_node_id,
2389:             target_node_id: request.target_node_id,
2390:         })
2391:     }
2392: 
2393:     pub fn signed_traversal_coordination_record(
2394:         &self,
2395:         record: TraversalCoordinationRecord,
2396:     ) -> Result<SignedTraversalCoordinationRecord, ControlPlaneError> {
2397:         if record.issued_at_unix == 0 {
2398:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2399:                 "coordination issued_at_unix must be greater than zero".to_string(),
2400:             ));
2401:         }
2402:         if record.probe_start_unix == 0 {
2403:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2404:                 "coordination probe_start_unix must be greater than zero".to_string(),
2405:             ));
2406:         }
2407:         if record.issued_at_unix >= record.expires_at_unix {
2408:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2409:                 "coordination expires_at_unix must be greater than issued_at_unix".to_string(),
2410:             ));
2411:         }
2412:         if record.expires_at_unix.saturating_sub(record.issued_at_unix) > 30 {
2413:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2414:                 "coordination ttl exceeds max supported value".to_string(),
2415:             ));
2416:         }
2417:         if record.probe_start_unix > record.expires_at_unix {
2418:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2419:                 "coordination probe_start_unix must not exceed expires_at_unix".to_string(),
2420:             ));
2421:         }
2422:         if record.node_a.trim() == record.node_b.trim() {
2423:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2424:                 "coordination requires distinct node_a and node_b".to_string(),
2425:             ));
2426:         }
2427:         if !is_valid_node_id_text(record.node_a.as_str()) {
2428:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2429:                 "coordination node_a must not be empty".to_string(),
2430:             ));
2431:         }
2432:         if !is_valid_node_id_text(record.node_b.as_str()) {
2433:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2434:                 "coordination node_b must not be empty".to_string(),
2435:             ));
2436:         }
2437: 
2438:         if record.session_id.iter().all(|value| *value == 0) {
2439:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2440:                 "coordination session_id must not be all zeros".to_string(),
2441:             ));
2442:         }
2443:         if record.nonce.iter().all(|value| *value == 0) {
2444:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2445:                 "coordination nonce must not be all zeros".to_string(),
2446:             ));
2447:         }
2448: 
2449:         let node_a = self.nodes.get(record.node_a.as_str())?.ok_or_else(|| {
2450:             ControlPlaneError::Traversal("coordination node_a does not exist".to_string())
2451:         })?;
2452:         let node_b = self.nodes.get(record.node_b.as_str())?.ok_or_else(|| {
2453:             ControlPlaneError::Traversal("coordination node_b does not exist".to_string())
2454:         })?;
2455:         if !self.policy_allows_node_pair(&node_a, &node_b) {
2456:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2457:                 "coordination denied by policy".to_string(),
2458:             ));
2459:         }
2460: 
2461:         let payload = serialize_traversal_coordination_payload(&record)?;
2462:         let signature = self.endpoint_hint_signing_key.sign(payload.as_bytes());
2463:         Ok(SignedTraversalCoordinationRecord {
2464:             payload,
2465:             signature_hex: hex_bytes(&signature.to_bytes()),
2466:             session_id: record.session_id,
2467:             probe_start_unix: record.probe_start_unix,
2468:             node_a: record.node_a,
2469:             node_b: record.node_b,
2470:             issued_at_unix: record.issued_at_unix,
2471:             expires_at_unix: record.expires_at_unix,
2472:             nonce: record.nonce,
2473:         })
2474:     }
2475: 
2476:     pub fn verify_signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(&self, bundle: &SignedEndpointHintBundle) -> bool {
2477:         if bundle.generated_at_unix >= bundle.expires_at_unix {
2478:             return false;
2479:         }
2480:         let signature_bytes = match decode_hex_to_fixed::<64>(&bundle.signature_hex) {
2481:             Ok(bytes) => bytes,
2482:             Err(()) => return false,
2483:         };
2484:         let signature = Signature::from_bytes(&signature_bytes);
2485:         let verifying_key = match VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&self.endpoint_hint_verifying_key) {
2486:             Ok(key) => key,
2487:             Err(_) => return false,
2488:         };
2489:         verifying_key
2490:             .verify(bundle.payload.as_bytes(), &signature)
2491:             .is_ok()
2492:     }
2493: 
2494:     pub fn verify_signed_traversal_coordination_record(
2495:         &self,
2496:         record: &SignedTraversalCoordinationRecord,
2497:     ) -> bool {
2498:         if record.issued_at_unix >= record.expires_at_unix {
2499:             return false;
2500:         }
2501:         if record.expires_at_unix.saturating_sub(record.issued_at_unix) > 30 {
2502:             return false;
2503:         }
2504:         if record.probe_start_unix > record.expires_at_unix {
2505:             return false;
2506:         }
2507:         if record.node_a.trim() == record.node_b.trim() {
2508:             return false;
2509:         }
2510:         if !is_valid_node_id_text(record.node_a.as_str())
2511:             || !is_valid_node_id_text(record.node_b.as_str())
2512:         {
2513:             return false;
2514:         }
2515:         if record.session_id.iter().all(|value| *value == 0) {
2516:             return false;
2517:         }
2518:         if record.nonce.iter().all(|value| *value == 0) {
2519:             return false;
2520:         }
2521: 
2522:         let expected_payload =
2523:             match serialize_traversal_coordination_payload(&TraversalCoordinationRecord {
2524:                 session_id: record.session_id,
2525:                 probe_start_unix: record.probe_start_unix,
2526:                 node_a: record.node_a.clone(),
2527:                 node_b: record.node_b.clone(),
2528:                 issued_at_unix: record.issued_at_unix,
2529:                 expires_at_unix: record.expires_at_unix,
2530:                 nonce: record.nonce,
2531:             }) {
2532:                 Ok(payload) => payload,
2533:                 Err(_) => return false,
2534:             };
2535:         if expected_payload != record.payload {
2536:             return false;
2537:         }
2538: 
2539:         let signature_bytes = match decode_hex_to_fixed::<64>(&record.signature_hex) {
2540:             Ok(bytes) => bytes,
2541:             Err(()) => return false,
2542:         };
2543:         let signature = Signature::from_bytes(&signature_bytes);
2544:         let verifying_key = match VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&self.endpoint_hint_verifying_key) {
2545:             Ok(key) => key,
2546:             Err(_) => return false,
2547:         };
2548:         verifying_key
2549:             .verify(record.payload.as_bytes(), &signature)
2550:             .is_ok()
2551:     }
2552: 
2553:     pub fn signed_endpoint_hint_bundle_to_wire(bundle: &SignedEndpointHintBundle) -> String {
2554:         format!("{}signature={}\n", bundle.payload, bundle.signature_hex)
2555:     }
2556: 
2557:     pub fn verify_signed_dns_zone_bundle(&self, bundle: &SignedDnsZoneBundle) -> bool {
2558:         let verifying_key = match VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&self.dns_zone_verifying_key) {
2559:             Ok(key) => key,
2560:             Err(_) => return false,
2561:         };
2562:         verify_dns_zone_bundle(bundle, &verifying_key).is_ok()
2563:     }
2564: 
2565:     pub fn signed_dns_zone_bundle_to_wire(bundle: &SignedDnsZoneBundle) -> String {
2566:         render_signed_dns_zone_bundle_wire(bundle)
2567:     }
2568: 
2569:     pub fn verify_signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(&self, bundle: &SignedAutoTunnelBundle) -> bool {
2570:         if bundle.generated_at_unix > bundle.expires_at_unix {
2571:             return false;
2572:         }
2573:         let signature_bytes = match decode_hex_to_fixed::<64>(&bundle.signature_hex) {
2574:             Ok(bytes) => bytes,
2575:             Err(()) => return false,
2576:         };
2577:         let signature = Signature::from_bytes(&signature_bytes);
2578:         let verifying_key = match VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&self.assignment_verifying_key) {
2579:             Ok(key) => key,
2580:             Err(_) => return false,
2581:         };
2582:         verifying_key
2583:             .verify(bundle.payload.as_bytes(), &signature)
2584:             .is_ok()
2585:     }
2586: 
2587:     pub fn signed_auto_tunnel_bundle_to_wire(bundle: &SignedAutoTunnelBundle) -> String {
2588:         format!("{}signature={}\n", bundle.payload, bundle.signature_hex)
2589:     }
2590: 
2591:     fn deterministic_tunnel_assignments<'a>(
2592:         node_ids: impl Iterator<Item = &'a str>,
2593:     ) -> Result<HashMap<String, String>, ControlPlaneError> {
2594:         const MAX_OFFSET: u32 = 0x3f_ffff;
2595:         let mut ordered_ids = node_ids.map(ToOwned::to_owned).collect::<Vec<String>>();
2596:         ordered_ids.sort();
2597:         ordered_ids.dedup();
2598: 
2599:         if ordered_ids.len() as u32 > MAX_OFFSET {
2600:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2601:                 "no available tunnel cidr assignment remains".to_string(),
2602:             ));
2603:         }
2604: 
2605:         let mut used_offsets = HashSet::new();
2606:         let mut assignments = HashMap::with_capacity(ordered_ids.len());
2607:         for node_id in ordered_ids {
2608:             let mut offset = Self::deterministic_offset_for_node_id(node_id.as_str());
2609:             let start = offset;
2610:             loop {
2611:                 if used_offsets.insert(offset) {
2612:                     assignments.insert(node_id, Self::cidr_from_offset(offset));
2613:                     break;
2614:                 }
2615:                 offset = if offset == MAX_OFFSET { 1 } else { offset + 1 };
2616:                 if offset == start {
2617:                     return Err(ControlPlaneError::Assignment(
2618:                         "no available tunnel cidr assignment remains".to_string(),
2619:                     ));
2620:                 }
2621:             }
2622:         }
2623: 
2624:         Ok(assignments)
2625:     }
2626: 
2627:     fn deterministic_offset_for_node_id(node_id: &str) -> u32 {
2628:         const MAX_OFFSET: u32 = 0x3f_ffff;
2629:         let digest = sha256_digest(node_id.as_bytes());
2630:         let raw = u32::from_be_bytes([digest[0], digest[1], digest[2], digest[3]]);
2631:         (raw % MAX_OFFSET) + 1
2632:     }
2633: 
2634:     fn cidr_from_offset(offset: u32) -> String {
2635:         let second_octet = 64 + ((offset >> 16) & 0x3f);
2636:         let third_octet = (offset >> 8) & 0xff;
2637:         let fourth_octet = offset & 0xff;
2638:         format!("100.{second_octet}.{third_octet}.{fourth_octet}/32")
2639:     }
2640: 
2641:     fn policy_allows_node_pair(&self, source: &NodeMetadata, destination: &NodeMetadata) -> bool {
2642:         let source_selectors = selectors_for_node(source);
2643:         let destination_selectors = selectors_for_node(destination);
2644:         for src in &source_selectors {
2645:             for dst in &destination_selectors {
2646:                 for protocol in [Protocol::Any, Protocol::Udp, Protocol::Tcp] {
2647:                     let decision = self.policy.evaluate(&AccessRequest {
2648:                         src: src.clone(),
2649:                         dst: dst.clone(),
2650:                         protocol,
2651:                     });
2652:                     if decision == PolicyEngineDecision::Allow {
2653:                         return true;
2654:                     }
2655:                 }
2656:             }
2657:         }
2658:         false
2659:     }
2660: 
2661:     fn sign_peer_map_payload(&self, payload: &str) -> String {
2662:         let signature = self.assignment_signing_key.sign(payload.as_bytes());
2663:         hex_bytes(&signature.to_bytes())
2664:     }
2665: 
2666:     fn sign_access_token(&self, claims: &TokenClaims) -> SignedTokenClaims {
2667:         let payload = token_claims_payload(claims);
2668:         let signature = self.access_token_signing_key.sign(payload.as_bytes());
2669:         SignedTokenClaims {
2670:             claims: claims.clone(),
2671:             signature_hex: hex_bytes(&signature.to_bytes()),
2672:         }
2673:     }
2674: 
2675:     fn verify_peer_map_signature(&self, payload: &str, signature_hex: &str) -> bool {
2676:         let signature_bytes = match decode_hex_to_fixed::<64>(signature_hex) {
2677:             Ok(bytes) => bytes,
2678:             Err(()) => return false,
2679:         };
2680:         let signature = Signature::from_bytes(&signature_bytes);
2681:         let verifying_key = match VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&self.assignment_verifying_key) {
2682:             Ok(key) => key,
2683:             Err(_) => return false,
2684:         };
2685:         verifying_key.verify(payload.as_bytes(), &signature).is_ok()
2686:     }
2687: }
2688: 
2689: #[derive(Debug)]
2690: pub enum ControlPlaneError {
2691:     Credential(CredentialError),
2692:     Auth(AuthError),
2693:     Trust(TrustStateError),
2694:     Persistence(persistence::PersistenceError),
2695:     Assignment(String),
2696:     Dns(String),
2697:     Traversal(String),
2698:     Internal,
2699: }
2700: 
2701: impl fmt::Display for ControlPlaneError {
2702:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
2703:         match self {
2704:             ControlPlaneError::Credential(err) => write!(f, "credential error: {err}"),
2705:             ControlPlaneError::Auth(err) => write!(f, "auth error: {err}"),
2706:             ControlPlaneError::Trust(err) => write!(f, "trust error: {err}"),
2707:             ControlPlaneError::Persistence(err) => write!(f, "persistence error: {err}"),
2708:             ControlPlaneError::Assignment(err) => write!(f, "assignment error: {err}"),
2709:             ControlPlaneError::Dns(err) => write!(f, "dns error: {err}"),
2710:             ControlPlaneError::Traversal(err) => write!(f, "traversal error: {err}"),
2711:             ControlPlaneError::Internal => f.write_str("control-plane internal error"),
2712:         }
2713:     }
2714: }
2715: 
2716: impl std::error::Error for ControlPlaneError {}
2717: 
2718: /// Derive the traversal endpoint-hint signing key from the control-plane
2719: /// signing secret.
2720: ///
2721: /// Relay session tokens are bound to the same verifier as traversal hints, so
2722: /// runtime code can use this helper to issue relay tokens that verify against
2723: /// the pinned traversal verifier key.
2724: pub fn derive_endpoint_hint_signing_key(mut signing_secret: Vec<u8>) -> SigningKey {
2725:     let mut seed = derive_signing_seed(ENDPOINT_HINT_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, &signing_secret);
2726:     signing_secret.zeroize();
2727:     let signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&seed);
2728:     seed.zeroize();
2729:     signing_key
2730: }
2731: 
2732: fn derive_signing_seed(domain: &[u8], secret: &[u8]) -> [u8; 32] {
2733:     let mut seed = [0u8; 32];
2734:     let hkdf = Hkdf::<Sha256>::new(Some(SIGNING_SEED_HKDF_SALT_V1), secret);
2735:     hkdf.expand(domain, &mut seed)
2736:         .expect("hkdf expand length is fixed and valid");
2737:     seed
2738: }
2739: 
2740: fn map_persisted_credential_state_to_error(state: Option<&str>) -> CredentialError {
2741:     match state {
2742:         Some("revoked") => CredentialError::Revoked,
2743:         Some("expired") => CredentialError::Expired,
2744:         Some("used") | Some("created") | Some(_) => CredentialError::AlreadyConsumed,
2745:         None => CredentialError::NotFound,
2746:     }
2747: }
2748: 
2749: fn map_dns_zone_error(err: DnsZoneError) -> ControlPlaneError {
2750:     ControlPlaneError::Dns(err.to_string())
2751: }
2752: 
2753: fn random_nonce_hex(length_bytes: usize) -> String {
2754:     let mut nonce = vec![0u8; length_bytes];
2755:     rand::rngs::OsRng
2756:         .try_fill_bytes(nonce.as_mut_slice())
2757:         .expect("os randomness unavailable for control-plane nonce");
2758:     let encoded = hex_bytes(nonce.as_slice());
2759:     nonce.zeroize();
2760:     encoded
2761: }
2762: 
2763: fn sha256_digest(payload: &[u8]) -> [u8; 32] {
2764:     let mut hasher = Sha256::new();
2765:     hasher.update(payload);
2766:     let digest = hasher.finalize();
2767:     let mut out = [0u8; 32];
2768:     out.copy_from_slice(&digest[..32]);
2769:     out
2770: }
2771: 
2772: fn token_claims_payload(claims: &TokenClaims) -> String {
2773:     format!(
2774:         "version=1\nsubject={}\nissued_at_unix={}\nexpires_at_unix={}\nnonce={}\n",
2775:         claims.subject, claims.issued_at_unix, claims.expires_at_unix, claims.nonce
2776:     )
2777: }
2778: 
2779: fn hex_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
2780:     let mut hex = String::with_capacity(bytes.len() * 2);
2781:     for byte in bytes {
2782:         hex.push_str(&format!("{byte:02x}"));
2783:     }
2784:     hex
2785: }
2786: 
2787: fn decode_hex_to_fixed<const N: usize>(encoded: &str) -> Result<[u8; N], ()> {
2788:     let mut bytes = [0u8; N];
2789:     let trimmed = encoded.trim();
2790:     if trimmed.len() != N * 2 {
2791:         return Err(());
2792:     }
2793:     let raw = trimmed.as_bytes();
2794:     let mut index = 0usize;
2795:     while index < N {
2796:         let hi = decode_hex_nibble(raw[index * 2])?;
2797:         let lo = decode_hex_nibble(raw[index * 2 + 1])?;
2798:         bytes[index] = (hi << 4) | lo;
2799:         index += 1;
2800:     }
2801:     Ok(bytes)
2802: }
2803: 
2804: fn decode_hex_nibble(value: u8) -> Result<u8, ()> {
2805:     match value {
2806:         b'0'..=b'9' => Ok(value - b'0'),
2807:         b'a'..=b'f' => Ok(value - b'a' + 10),
2808:         b'A'..=b'F' => Ok(value - b'A' + 10),
2809:         _ => Err(()),
2810:     }
2811: }
2812: 
2813: fn selectors_for_node(node: &NodeMetadata) -> Vec<String> {
2814:     let mut selectors = vec![
2815:         format!("node:{}", node.node_id),
2816:         format!("user:{}", node.owner),
2817:     ];
2818:     for tag in &node.tags {
2819:         selectors.push(format!("tag:{tag}"));
2820:     }
2821:     selectors
2822: }
2823: 
2824: fn is_valid_node_id_text(value: &str) -> bool {
2825:     !value.trim().is_empty()
2826: }
2827: 
2828: struct AutoTunnelPayloadHeader<'a> {
2829:     node_id: &'a str,
2830:     mesh_cidr: &'a str,
2831:     assigned_cidr: &'a str,
2832:     generated_at_unix: u64,
2833:     expires_at_unix: u64,
2834:     nonce: u64,
2835: }
2836: 
2837: fn serialize_auto_tunnel_payload(
2838:     header: &AutoTunnelPayloadHeader<'_>,
2839:     peers: &[AutoTunnelPeer],
2840:     routes: &[AutoTunnelRoute],
2841: ) -> String {
2842:     let mut payload = String::new();
2843:     payload.push_str("version=1\n");
2844:     payload.push_str(&format!("node_id={}\n", header.node_id));
2845:     payload.push_str(&format!("mesh_cidr={}\n", header.mesh_cidr));
2846:     payload.push_str(&format!("assigned_cidr={}\n", header.assigned_cidr));
2847:     payload.push_str(&format!("generated_at_unix={}\n", header.generated_at_unix));
2848:     payload.push_str(&format!("expires_at_unix={}\n", header.expires_at_unix));
2849:     payload.push_str(&format!("nonce={}\n", header.nonce));
2850:     payload.push_str(&format!("peer_count={}\n", peers.len()));
2851:     for (index, peer) in peers.iter().enumerate() {
2852:         payload.push_str(&format!("peer.{index}.node_id={}\n", peer.node_id));
2853:         payload.push_str(&format!("peer.{index}.endpoint={}\n", peer.endpoint));
2854:         payload.push_str(&format!(
2855:             "peer.{index}.public_key_hex={}\n",
2856:             hex_bytes(&peer.public_key)
2857:         ));
2858:         payload.push_str(&format!(
2859:             "peer.{index}.allowed_ips={}\n",
2860:             peer.allowed_ips.join(",")
2861:         ));
2862:     }
2863:     payload.push_str(&format!("route_count={}\n", routes.len()));
2864:     for (index, route) in routes.iter().enumerate() {
2865:         payload.push_str(&format!(
2866:             "route.{index}.destination_cidr={}\n",
2867:             route.destination_cidr
2868:         ));
2869:         payload.push_str(&format!("route.{index}.via_node={}\n", route.via_node));
2870:         let kind = match route.kind {
2871:             AutoTunnelRouteKind::Mesh => "mesh",
2872:             AutoTunnelRouteKind::ExitNodeLan => "exit_lan",
2873:             AutoTunnelRouteKind::ExitNodeDefault => "exit_default",
2874:         };
2875:         payload.push_str(&format!("route.{index}.kind={kind}\n"));
2876:     }
2877:     payload
2878: }
2879: 
2880: fn host_ip_from_host_cidr(value: &str) -> Option<String> {
2881:     let (ip, prefix) = value.split_once('/')?;
2882:     if prefix != "32" && prefix != "128" {
2883:         return None;
2884:     }
2885:     Some(ip.to_string())
2886: }
2887: 
2888: fn serialize_endpoint_hint_payload(
2889:     source_node_id: &str,
2890:     target_node_id: &str,
2891:     generated_at_unix: u64,
2892:     expires_at_unix: u64,
2893:     nonce: u64,
2894:     candidates: &[EndpointHintCandidate],
2895: ) -> Result<String, ControlPlaneError> {
2896:     let mut ordered = candidates.to_vec();
2897:     ordered.sort_by(|left, right| {
2898:         right
2899:             .priority
2900:             .cmp(&left.priority)
2901:             .then(
2902:                 left.candidate_type
2903:                     .as_str()
2904:                     .cmp(right.candidate_type.as_str()),
2905:             )
2906:             .then(left.endpoint.cmp(&right.endpoint))
2907:             .then(
2908:                 left.relay_id
2909:                     .as_deref()
2910:                     .unwrap_or("")
2911:                     .cmp(right.relay_id.as_deref().unwrap_or("")),
2912:             )
2913:     });
2914: 
2915:     let mut payload = String::new();
2916:     payload.push_str("version=1\n");
2917:     payload.push_str("path_policy=direct_preferred_relay_allowed\n");
2918:     payload.push_str(&format!("source_node_id={source_node_id}\n"));
2919:     payload.push_str(&format!("target_node_id={target_node_id}\n"));
2920:     payload.push_str(&format!("generated_at_unix={generated_at_unix}\n"));
2921:     payload.push_str(&format!("expires_at_unix={expires_at_unix}\n"));
2922:     payload.push_str(&format!("nonce={nonce}\n"));
2923:     payload.push_str(&format!("candidate_count={}\n", ordered.len()));
2924:     for (index, candidate) in ordered.iter().enumerate() {
2925:         let endpoint = candidate.endpoint.parse::<SocketAddr>().map_err(|_| {
2926:             ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2927:                 "candidate endpoint failed canonical serialization".to_string(),
2928:             )
2929:         })?;
2930:         if endpoint.port() == 0 {
2931:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2932:                 "candidate endpoint port must be non-zero".to_string(),
2933:             ));
2934:         }
2935: 
2936:         let family = if endpoint.ip().is_ipv4() {
2937:             "ipv4"
2938:         } else {
2939:             "ipv6"
2940:         };
2941:         let relay_id = candidate.relay_id.as_deref().unwrap_or("").trim();
2942:         if matches!(candidate.candidate_type, EndpointHintCandidateType::Relay) {
2943:             if relay_id.is_empty() {
2944:                 return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2945:                     "relay candidates require relay_id".to_string(),
2946:                 ));
2947:             }
2948:         } else if !relay_id.is_empty() {
2949:             return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2950:                 "relay_id is only valid for relay candidates".to_string(),
2951:             ));
2952:         }
2953: 
2954:         payload.push_str(&format!(
2955:             "candidate.{index}.type={}\n",
2956:             candidate.candidate_type.as_str()
2957:         ));
2958:         payload.push_str(&format!("candidate.{index}.addr={}\n", endpoint.ip()));
2959:         payload.push_str(&format!("candidate.{index}.port={}\n", endpoint.port()));
2960:         payload.push_str(&format!("candidate.{index}.family={family}\n"));
2961:         payload.push_str(&format!("candidate.{index}.relay_id={relay_id}\n"));
2962:         payload.push_str(&format!(
2963:             "candidate.{index}.priority={}\n",
2964:             candidate.priority
2965:         ));
2966:     }
2967:     Ok(payload)
2968: }
2969: 
2970: fn serialize_traversal_coordination_payload(
2971:     record: &TraversalCoordinationRecord,
2972: ) -> Result<String, ControlPlaneError> {
2973:     if !is_valid_node_id_text(record.node_a.as_str())
2974:         || !is_valid_node_id_text(record.node_b.as_str())
2975:     {
2976:         return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2977:             "coordination node ids must not be empty".to_string(),
2978:         ));
2979:     }
2980:     if record.node_a.trim() == record.node_b.trim() {
2981:         return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2982:             "coordination requires distinct nodes".to_string(),
2983:         ));
2984:     }
2985:     if record.issued_at_unix >= record.expires_at_unix {
2986:         return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2987:             "coordination expires_at_unix must be greater than issued_at_unix".to_string(),
2988:         ));
2989:     }
2990:     if record.expires_at_unix.saturating_sub(record.issued_at_unix) > 30 {
2991:         return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2992:             "coordination ttl exceeds max supported value".to_string(),
2993:         ));
2994:     }
2995:     if record.probe_start_unix > record.expires_at_unix {
2996:         return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
2997:             "coordination probe_start_unix must not exceed expires_at_unix".to_string(),
2998:         ));
2999:     }
3000:     if record.session_id.iter().all(|value| *value == 0) {
3001:         return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
3002:             "coordination session_id must not be all zeros".to_string(),
3003:         ));
3004:     }
3005:     if record.nonce.iter().all(|value| *value == 0) {
3006:         return Err(ControlPlaneError::Traversal(
3007:             "coordination nonce must not be all zeros".to_string(),
3008:         ));
3009:     }
3010: 
3011:     let mut payload = String::new();
3012:     payload.push_str("version=1\n");
3013:     payload.push_str("type=traversal_coordination\n");
3014:     payload.push_str(&format!(
3015:         "session_id={}\n",
3016:         hex_bytes(record.session_id.as_slice())
3017:     ));
3018:     payload.push_str(&format!("probe_start_unix={}\n", record.probe_start_unix));
3019:     payload.push_str(&format!("node_a={}\n", record.node_a.trim()));
3020:     payload.push_str(&format!("node_b={}\n", record.node_b.trim()));
3021:     payload.push_str(&format!("issued_at_unix={}\n", record.issued_at_unix));
3022:     payload.push_str(&format!("expires_at_unix={}\n", record.expires_at_unix));
3023:     payload.push_str(&format!("nonce={}\n", hex_bytes(record.nonce.as_slice())));
3024:     Ok(payload)
3025: }
3026: 
3027: fn is_valid_ipv4_or_ipv6_cidr(value: &str) -> bool {
3028:     let Some((ip_part, prefix_part)) = value.split_once('/') else {
3029:         return false;
3030:     };
3031:     if ip_part.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_err() {
3032:         return false;
3033:     }
3034:     let Ok(prefix) = prefix_part.parse::<u8>() else {
3035:         return false;
3036:     };
3037:     if ip_part.contains(':') {
3038:         prefix <= 128
3039:     } else {
3040:         prefix <= 32
3041:     }
3042: }

3062:     fn payload_field(payload: &str, key: &str) -> Option<String> {
3063:         payload.lines().find_map(|line| {
3064:             let (line_key, value) = line.split_once('=')?;
3065:             if line_key == key {
3066:                 Some(value.to_string())
3067:             } else {
3068:                 None
3069:             }
3070:         })
3071:     }

3076:             AuthRateLimitConfig {
3077:                 ip_burst: 2,
3078:                 ip_refill_per_minute: 0,
3079:                 identity_burst: 10,
3080:                 identity_refill_per_15_min: 0,
3081:             },
3082:             LockoutConfig::default(),
3083:             ReplayPolicy::default(),
3084:         );
3085: 
3086:         assert!(
3087:             guard
3088:                 .authorize_attempt("/auth/login", "198.51.100.5", "alice", 100)
3089:                 .is_ok()
3090:         );
3091:         assert!(
3092:             guard
3093:                 .authorize_attempt("/auth/login", "198.51.100.5", "alice", 100)
3094:                 .is_ok()
3095:         );
3096: 
3097:         let blocked = guard.authorize_attempt("/auth/login", "198.51.100.5", "alice", 100);
3098:         assert_eq!(blocked.err(), Some(AuthError::RateLimited));

3103:         let mut guard = AuthSurfaceGuard::new(
3104:             AuthRateLimitConfig::default(),
3105:             LockoutConfig {
3106:                 initial_backoff_secs: 10,
3107:                 max_backoff_secs: 60,
3108:             },
3109:             ReplayPolicy::default(),
3110:         );
3111: 
3112:         guard.register_failure("alice", 100);
3113:         let blocked = guard.authorize_attempt("/auth/login", "198.51.100.5", "alice", 105);
3114:         match blocked {
3115:             Err(AuthError::LockedOutUntil(until)) => assert_eq!(until, 110),
3116:             other => panic!("unexpected lockout result: {other:?}"),
3117:         }
3118: 
3119:         guard.register_success("alice");
3120:         assert!(
3121:             guard
3122:                 .authorize_attempt("/auth/login", "198.51.100.5", "alice", 120)
3123:                 .is_ok()
3124:         );
3125:     }

3187:     fn throwaway_credential_lifecycle_and_audit_events() {
3188:         let store = ThrowawayCredentialStore::default();
3189:         let created = store
3190:             .create(
3191:                 "cred-1".to_string(),
3192:                 "alice".to_string(),
3193:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
3194:                 100,
3195:                 30,
3196:             )
3197:             .expect("credential should be created");
3198:         assert_eq!(created.state, ThrowawayCredentialState::Created);
3199: 
3200:         let consumed = store
3201:             .consume("cred-1", 110)
3202:             .expect("consume should succeed exactly once");
3203:         assert_eq!(consumed.state, ThrowawayCredentialState::Used);
3204: 
3205:         let second = store.consume("cred-1", 111);
3206:         assert_eq!(second.err(), Some(CredentialError::AlreadyConsumed));
3207: 
3208:         let events = store
3209:             .audit_events()
3210:             .expect("audit events should be readable");
3211:         assert!(
3212:             events
3213:                 .iter()
3214:                 .any(|event| event.to_state == ThrowawayCredentialState::Created)
3215:         );
3216:         assert!(
3217:             events
3218:                 .iter()
3219:                 .any(|event| event.to_state == ThrowawayCredentialState::Used)
3220:         );
3221:     }

3223:     #[test]
3224:     fn throwaway_credential_atomic_single_use_under_concurrency() {
3225:         use std::sync::Arc;
3226:         use std::thread;
3227: 
3228:         let store = Arc::new(ThrowawayCredentialStore::default());
3229:         store
3230:             .create(
3231:                 "cred-race".to_string(),
3232:                 "alice".to_string(),
3233:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
3234:                 200,
3235:                 120,
3236:             )
3237:             .expect("credential should be created");
3238: 
3239:         let mut handles = Vec::new();
3240:         for _ in 0..16 {
3241:             let store = Arc::clone(&store);
3242:             handles.push(thread::spawn(move || {
3243:                 store.consume("cred-race", 210).is_ok()
3244:             }));
3245:         }
3246: 
3247:         let mut success_count = 0usize;
3248:         for handle in handles {
3249:             if handle.join().expect("thread should join") {
3250:                 success_count += 1;
3251:             }
3252:         }
3253: 
3254:         assert_eq!(success_count, 1);
3255:     }

3257:     #[test]
3258:     fn trust_state_persist_and_integrity_check() {
3259:         let unique_dir = format!(
3260:             "rustynet-trust-state-dir-{}",
3261:             std::time::SystemTime::now()
3262:                 .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
3263:                 .expect("clock should be valid")
3264:                 .as_nanos()
3265:         );
3266:         let test_dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(unique_dir);
3267:         std::fs::create_dir_all(&test_dir).expect("test directory should be creatable");
3268:         #[cfg(unix)]
3269:         {
3270:             use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
3271:             std::fs::set_permissions(&test_dir, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700))
3272:                 .expect("test directory permissions should be set");
3273:         }
3274:         let path = test_dir.join("trust.state");
3275: 
3276:         let state = TrustState {
3277:             generation: 7,
3278:             signing_fingerprint: "ed25519:abc123".to_string(),
3279:             updated_at_unix: 1_000,
3280:         };
3281: 
3282:         persist_trust_state(&path, &state).expect("persist should succeed");
3283:         let loaded = load_trust_state(&path).expect("load should succeed");
3284:         assert_eq!(loaded, state);
3285: 
3286:         let mut tampered = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).expect("should read state file");
3287:         tampered = tampered.replace("generation=7", "generation=8");
3288:         std::fs::write(&path, tampered).expect("should write tampered state");
3289: 
3290:         let err = load_trust_state(&path).expect_err("tampered state must fail integrity check");
3291:         assert!(matches!(err, super::TrustStateError::IntegrityMismatch));
3292: 
3293:         let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
3294:         let _ = std::fs::remove_file(format!("{}.integrity.key", path.display()));
3295:         let _ = std::fs::remove_dir(&test_dir);
3296:     }

3407:     fn auto_tunnel_bundle_is_centrally_assigned_and_signed() {
3408:         let policy = PolicySet {
3409:             rules: vec![PolicyRule {
3410:                 src: "*".to_string(),
3411:                 dst: "*".to_string(),
3412:                 protocol: Protocol::Any,
3413:                 action: RuleAction::Allow,
3414:             }],
3415:         };
3416:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), policy);
3417: 
3418:         core.credentials
3419:             .create(
3420:                 "cred-node-a".to_string(),
3421:                 "admin".to_string(),
3422:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
3423:                 100,
3424:                 60,
3425:             )
3426:             .expect("credential should be created");
3427:         core.credentials
3428:             .create(
3429:                 "cred-node-b".to_string(),
3430:                 "admin".to_string(),
3431:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
3432:                 100,
3433:                 60,
3434:             )
3435:             .expect("credential should be created");
3436: 
3437:         core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3438:             credential_id: "cred-node-a".to_string(),
3439:             node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3440:             hostname: "node-a".to_string(),
3441:             os: "linux".to_string(),
3442:             tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3443:             owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3444:             endpoint: "198.51.100.40:51820".to_string(),
3445:             public_key: [41; 32],
3446:             now_unix: 120,
3447:         })
3448:         .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3449:         core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3450:             credential_id: "cred-node-b".to_string(),
3451:             node_id: "node-b".to_string(),
3452:             hostname: "node-b".to_string(),
3453:             os: "linux".to_string(),
3454:             tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3455:             owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3456:             endpoint: "198.51.100.41:51820".to_string(),
3457:             public_key: [42; 32],
3458:             now_unix: 121,
3459:         })
3460:         .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3461: 
3462:         let bundle = core
3463:             .signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(AutoTunnelBundleRequest {
3464:                 node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3465:                 generated_at_unix: 200,
3466:                 ttl_secs: 300,
3467:                 nonce: 11,
3468:                 mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),
3469:                 exit_node_id: Some("node-b".to_string()),
3470:                 lan_routes: vec!["192.168.1.0/24".to_string()],
3471:             })
3472:             .expect("auto tunnel bundle should be emitted");
3473: 
3474:         assert!(core.verify_signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(&bundle));
3475:         assert_eq!(
3476:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "node_id").as_deref(),
3477:             Some("node-a")
3478:         );
3479:         assert_eq!(
3480:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "route_count").as_deref(),
3481:             Some("3")
3482:         );
3483:         assert_eq!(
3484:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "peer_count").as_deref(),
3485:             Some("1")
3486:         );
3487:         let peer_allowed_ips = payload_field(&bundle.payload, "peer.0.allowed_ips")
3488:             .expect("peer allowed ips should be present");
3489:         assert!(peer_allowed_ips.contains("0.0.0.0/0"));
3490:         assert!(peer_allowed_ips.contains("192.168.1.0/24"));
3491: 
3492:         let wire = ControlPlaneCore::signed_auto_tunnel_bundle_to_wire(&bundle);
3493:         assert!(wire.contains("signature="));
3494: 
3495:         let mut tampered = bundle.clone();
3496:         tampered.payload.push_str("peer.99.node_id=tampered\n");
3497:         assert!(!core.verify_signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(&tampered));
3498:     }

3501:     fn auto_tunnel_bundle_is_policy_gated_and_assignment_is_stable() {
3502:         let policy = PolicySet {
3503:             rules: vec![PolicyRule {
3504:                 src: "node:node-a".to_string(),
3505:                 dst: "node:node-b".to_string(),
3506:                 protocol: Protocol::Udp,
3507:                 action: RuleAction::Allow,
3508:             }],
3509:         };
3510:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), policy);
3511: 
3512:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
3513:             ("cred-a", "node-a", "198.51.100.50:51820", [51; 32]),
3514:             ("cred-b", "node-b", "198.51.100.51:51820", [52; 32]),
3515:             ("cred-c", "node-c", "198.51.100.52:51820", [53; 32]),
3516:         ] {
3517:             core.credentials
3518:                 .create(
3519:                     credential_id.to_string(),
3520:                     "admin".to_string(),
3521:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
3522:                     100,
3523:                     60,
3524:                 )
3525:                 .expect("credential should be created");
3526:             core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3527:                 credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
3528:                 node_id: node_id.to_string(),
3529:                 hostname: node_id.to_string(),
3530:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
3531:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3532:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3533:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
3534:                 public_key,
3535:                 now_unix: 120,
3536:             })
3537:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3538:         }
3539: 
3540:         let first = core
3541:             .signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(AutoTunnelBundleRequest {
3542:                 node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3543:                 generated_at_unix: 200,
3544:                 ttl_secs: 300,
3545:                 nonce: 22,
3546:                 mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),
3547:                 exit_node_id: None,
3548:                 lan_routes: Vec::new(),
3549:             })
3550:             .expect("bundle should be generated");
3551:         let second = core
3552:             .signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(AutoTunnelBundleRequest {
3553:                 node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3554:                 generated_at_unix: 201,
3555:                 ttl_secs: 300,
3556:                 nonce: 23,
3557:                 mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),
3558:                 exit_node_id: None,
3559:                 lan_routes: Vec::new(),
3560:             })
3561:             .expect("bundle should be generated");
3562: 
3563:         assert_eq!(
3564:             payload_field(&first.payload, "assigned_cidr"),
3565:             payload_field(&second.payload, "assigned_cidr")
3566:         );
3567:         assert_eq!(
3568:             payload_field(&first.payload, "peer_count").as_deref(),
3569:             Some("1")
3570:         );
3571:         assert!(first.payload.contains("peer.0.node_id=node-b"));
3572:         assert!(!first.payload.contains("node-c"));
3573:     }

3576:     fn auto_tunnel_bundle_assignments_are_consistent_across_targets() {
3577:         let policy = PolicySet {
3578:             rules: vec![PolicyRule {
3579:                 src: "*".to_string(),
3580:                 dst: "*".to_string(),
3581:                 protocol: Protocol::Any,
3582:                 action: RuleAction::Allow,
3583:             }],
3584:         };
3585:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), policy);
3586: 
3587:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
3588:             ("cred-a", "node-a", "198.51.100.50:51820", [51; 32]),
3589:             ("cred-b", "node-b", "198.51.100.51:51820", [52; 32]),
3590:         ] {
3591:             core.credentials
3592:                 .create(
3593:                     credential_id.to_string(),
3594:                     "admin".to_string(),
3595:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
3596:                     100,
3597:                     60,
3598:                 )
3599:                 .expect("credential should be created");
3600:             core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3601:                 credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
3602:                 node_id: node_id.to_string(),
3603:                 hostname: node_id.to_string(),
3604:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
3605:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3606:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3607:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
3608:                 public_key,
3609:                 now_unix: 120,
3610:             })
3611:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3612:         }
3613: 
3614:         let bundle_a = core
3615:             .signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(AutoTunnelBundleRequest {
3616:                 node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3617:                 generated_at_unix: 200,
3618:                 ttl_secs: 300,
3619:                 nonce: 31,
3620:                 mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),
3621:                 exit_node_id: None,
3622:                 lan_routes: Vec::new(),
3623:             })
3624:             .expect("bundle should be generated");
3625:         let bundle_b = core
3626:             .signed_auto_tunnel_bundle(AutoTunnelBundleRequest {
3627:                 node_id: "node-b".to_string(),
3628:                 generated_at_unix: 200,
3629:                 ttl_secs: 300,
3630:                 nonce: 32,
3631:                 mesh_cidr: "100.64.0.0/10".to_string(),
3632:                 exit_node_id: None,
3633:                 lan_routes: Vec::new(),
3634:             })
3635:             .expect("bundle should be generated");
3636: 
3637:         let assigned_a =
3638:             payload_field(&bundle_a.payload, "assigned_cidr").expect("assigned cidr for node-a");
3639:         let assigned_b =
3640:             payload_field(&bundle_b.payload, "assigned_cidr").expect("assigned cidr for node-b");
3641:         assert_ne!(assigned_a, assigned_b);
3642:         assert_eq!(
3643:             payload_field(&bundle_a.payload, "peer.0.allowed_ips"),
3644:             Some(assigned_b.clone())
3645:         );
3646:         assert_eq!(
3647:             payload_field(&bundle_b.payload, "peer.0.allowed_ips"),
3648:             Some(assigned_a.clone())
3649:         );
3650:     }

3652:     #[test]
3653:     fn dns_zone_bundle_is_signed_and_tamper_detected() {
3654:         let policy = PolicySet {
3655:             rules: vec![PolicyRule {
3656:                 src: "*".to_string(),
3657:                 dst: "*".to_string(),
3658:                 protocol: Protocol::Any,
3659:                 action: RuleAction::Allow,
3660:             }],
3661:         };
3662:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), policy);
3663: 
3664:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
3665:             ("cred-a", "node-a", "198.51.100.50:51820", [51; 32]),
3666:             ("cred-b", "node-b", "198.51.100.51:51820", [52; 32]),
3667:         ] {
3668:             core.credentials
3669:                 .create(
3670:                     credential_id.to_string(),
3671:                     "admin".to_string(),
3672:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
3673:                     100,
3674:                     60,
3675:                 )
3676:                 .expect("credential should be created");
3677:             core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3678:                 credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
3679:                 node_id: node_id.to_string(),
3680:                 hostname: node_id.to_string(),
3681:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
3682:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3683:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3684:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
3685:                 public_key,
3686:                 now_unix: 120,
3687:             })
3688:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3689:         }
3690: 
3691:         let bundle = core
3692:             .signed_dns_zone_bundle(SignedDnsZoneBundleRequest {
3693:                 zone_name: "rustynet".to_string(),
3694:                 subject_node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3695:                 generated_at_unix: 200,
3696:                 ttl_secs: 120,
3697:                 nonce: 41,
3698:                 records: vec![DnsRecordRequest {
3699:                     label: "nas".to_string(),
3700:                     target_node_id: "node-b".to_string(),
3701:                     ttl_secs: 60,
3702:                     rr_type: DnsRecordType::A,
3703:                     target_addr_kind: DnsTargetAddrKind::MeshIpv4,
3704:                     aliases: vec!["storage".to_string()],
3705:                 }],
3706:             })
3707:             .expect("dns zone bundle should be emitted");
3708: 
3709:         assert!(core.verify_signed_dns_zone_bundle(&bundle));
3710:         assert_eq!(
3711:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "zone_name").as_deref(),
3712:             Some("rustynet")
3713:         );
3714:         assert_eq!(
3715:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "record.0.fqdn").as_deref(),
3716:             Some("nas.rustynet")
3717:         );
3718:         let expected_ip =
3719:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "record.0.expected_ip").expect("expected_ip present");
3720:         assert!(expected_ip.parse::<std::net::Ipv4Addr>().is_ok());
3721: 
3722:         let wire = ControlPlaneCore::signed_dns_zone_bundle_to_wire(&bundle);
3723:         assert!(wire.contains("signature="));
3724: 
3725:         let mut tampered = bundle.clone();
3726:         tampered
3727:             .payload
3728:             .push_str("record.9.fqdn=tampered.rustynet\n");
3729:         assert!(!core.verify_signed_dns_zone_bundle(&tampered));
3730:     }

3799:                     ttl_secs: 60,

3802:                     aliases: vec!["storage".to_string()],
3803:                 },

3818:     fn endpoint_hint_bundle_is_signed_and_tamper_detected() {
3819:         let policy = PolicySet {
3820:             rules: vec![PolicyRule {
3821:                 src: "*".to_string(),
3822:                 dst: "*".to_string(),
3823:                 protocol: Protocol::Any,
3824:                 action: RuleAction::Allow,
3825:             }],
3826:         };
3827:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), policy);
3828:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
3829:             ("cred-a", "node-a", "198.51.100.70:51820", [70; 32]),
3830:             ("cred-b", "node-b", "198.51.100.71:51820", [71; 32]),
3831:         ] {
3832:             core.credentials
3833:                 .create(
3834:                     credential_id.to_string(),
3835:                     "admin".to_string(),
3836:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
3837:                     100,
3838:                     60,
3839:                 )
3840:                 .expect("credential should be created");
3841:             core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3842:                 credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
3843:                 node_id: node_id.to_string(),
3844:                 hostname: node_id.to_string(),
3845:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
3846:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3847:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3848:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
3849:                 public_key,
3850:                 now_unix: 120,
3851:             })
3852:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3853:         }
3854: 
3855:         let bundle = core
3856:             .signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(EndpointHintBundleRequest {
3857:                 source_node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3858:                 target_node_id: "node-b".to_string(),
3859:                 generated_at_unix: 200,
3860:                 ttl_secs: 60,
3861:                 nonce: 7,
3862:                 candidates: vec![
3863:                     EndpointHintCandidate {
3864:                         candidate_type: EndpointHintCandidateType::Host,
3865:                         endpoint: "10.0.0.3:51820".to_string(),
3866:                         relay_id: None,
3867:                         priority: 10,
3868:                     },
3869:                     EndpointHintCandidate {
3870:                         candidate_type: EndpointHintCandidateType::Relay,
3871:                         endpoint: "203.0.113.44:443".to_string(),
3872:                         relay_id: Some("relay-eu-1".to_string()),
3873:                         priority: 20,
3874:                     },
3875:                 ],
3876:             })
3877:             .expect("endpoint hint bundle should be issued");
3878: 
3879:         assert!(core.verify_signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(&bundle));
3880:         assert_eq!(
3881:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "candidate_count").as_deref(),
3882:             Some("2")
3883:         );
3884:         assert_eq!(
3885:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "candidate.0.type").as_deref(),
3886:             Some("relay")
3887:         );
3888:         assert_eq!(
3889:             payload_field(&bundle.payload, "candidate.0.relay_id").as_deref(),
3890:             Some("relay-eu-1")
3891:         );
3892: 
3893:         let wire = ControlPlaneCore::signed_endpoint_hint_bundle_to_wire(&bundle);
3894:         assert!(wire.contains("signature="));
3895: 
3896:         let mut tampered = bundle.clone();
3897:         tampered.payload.push_str("candidate.99.type=relay\n");
3898:         assert!(!core.verify_signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(&tampered));
3899:     }

3901:     #[test]
3902:     fn endpoint_hint_bundle_enforces_policy_and_candidate_validation() {
3903:         let deny_all = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), PolicySet::default());
3904:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
3905:             ("cred-a", "node-a", "198.51.100.80:51820", [80; 32]),
3906:             ("cred-b", "node-b", "198.51.100.81:51820", [81; 32]),
3907:         ] {
3908:             deny_all
3909:                 .credentials
3910:                 .create(
3911:                     credential_id.to_string(),
3912:                     "admin".to_string(),
3913:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
3914:                     100,
3915:                     60,
3916:                 )
3917:                 .expect("credential should be created");
3918:             deny_all
3919:                 .enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3920:                     credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
3921:                     node_id: node_id.to_string(),
3922:                     hostname: node_id.to_string(),
3923:                     os: "linux".to_string(),
3924:                     tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3925:                     owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3926:                     endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
3927:                     public_key,
3928:                     now_unix: 120,
3929:                 })
3930:                 .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3931:         }
3932:         let denied = deny_all.signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(EndpointHintBundleRequest {
3933:             source_node_id: "node-a".to_string(),
3934:             target_node_id: "node-b".to_string(),
3935:             generated_at_unix: 200,
3936:             ttl_secs: 60,
3937:             nonce: 1,
3938:             candidates: vec![EndpointHintCandidate {
3939:                 candidate_type: EndpointHintCandidateType::Host,
3940:                 endpoint: "10.0.0.2:51820".to_string(),
3941:                 relay_id: None,
3942:                 priority: 1,
3943:             }],
3944:         });
3945:         assert!(
3946:             denied
3947:                 .expect_err("policy must deny source->target endpoint hints")
3948:                 .to_string()
3949:                 .contains("denied by policy")
3950:         );
3951: 
3952:         let allow_all = ControlPlaneCore::new(
3953:             b"control-secret".to_vec(),
3954:             PolicySet {
3955:                 rules: vec![PolicyRule {
3956:                     src: "*".to_string(),
3957:                     dst: "*".to_string(),
3958:                     protocol: Protocol::Any,
3959:                     action: RuleAction::Allow,
3960:                 }],
3961:             },
3962:         );
3963:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
3964:             ("cred-c", "node-c", "198.51.100.82:51820", [82; 32]),
3965:             ("cred-d", "node-d", "198.51.100.83:51820", [83; 32]),
3966:         ] {
3967:             allow_all
3968:                 .credentials
3969:                 .create(
3970:                     credential_id.to_string(),
3971:                     "admin".to_string(),
3972:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
3973:                     100,
3974:                     60,
3975:                 )
3976:                 .expect("credential should be created");
3977:             allow_all
3978:                 .enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
3979:                     credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
3980:                     node_id: node_id.to_string(),
3981:                     hostname: node_id.to_string(),
3982:                     os: "linux".to_string(),
3983:                     tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
3984:                     owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
3985:                     endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
3986:                     public_key,
3987:                     now_unix: 120,
3988:                 })
3989:                 .expect("enrollment should succeed");
3990:         }
3991: 
3992:         let duplicate = allow_all.signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(EndpointHintBundleRequest {
3993:             source_node_id: "node-c".to_string(),
3994:             target_node_id: "node-d".to_string(),
3995:             generated_at_unix: 200,
3996:             ttl_secs: 60,
3997:             nonce: 2,
3998:             candidates: vec![
3999:                 EndpointHintCandidate {
4000:                     candidate_type: EndpointHintCandidateType::Host,
4001:                     endpoint: "10.2.0.2:51820".to_string(),
4002:                     relay_id: None,
4003:                     priority: 100,
4004:                 },
4005:                 EndpointHintCandidate {
4006:                     candidate_type: EndpointHintCandidateType::Host,
4007:                     endpoint: "10.2.0.2:51820".to_string(),
4008:                     relay_id: None,
4009:                     priority: 1,
4010:                 },
4011:             ],
4012:         });
4013:         assert!(
4014:             duplicate
4015:                 .expect_err("duplicate endpoint candidates must fail")
4016:                 .to_string()
4017:                 .contains("duplicate endpoint hint candidate")
4018:         );
4019: 
4020:         let relay_missing_id = allow_all.signed_endpoint_hint_bundle(EndpointHintBundleRequest {
4021:             source_node_id: "node-c".to_string(),
4022:             target_node_id: "node-d".to_string(),
4023:             generated_at_unix: 200,
4024:             ttl_secs: 60,
4025:             nonce: 3,
4026:             candidates: vec![EndpointHintCandidate {
4027:                 candidate_type: EndpointHintCandidateType::Relay,
4028:                 endpoint: "203.0.113.55:443".to_string(),
4029:                 relay_id: None,
4030:                 priority: 1,
4031:             }],
4032:         });
4033:         assert!(
4034:             relay_missing_id
4035:                 .expect_err("relay candidates without relay_id must fail")

4037:                 .contains("relay candidates require relay_id")
4038:         );

4041:     #[test]
4042:     fn traversal_coordination_record_is_signed_and_tamper_detected() {
4043:         let policy = PolicySet {
4044:             rules: vec![PolicyRule {
4045:                 src: "*".to_string(),
4046:                 dst: "*".to_string(),
4047:                 protocol: Protocol::Any,
4048:                 action: RuleAction::Allow,
4049:             }],
4050:         };
4051:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), policy);
4052:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
4053:             (
4054:                 "coord-cred-a",
4055:                 "coord-node-a",
4056:                 "198.51.100.170:51820",
4057:                 [170; 32],
4058:             ),
4059:             (
4060:                 "coord-cred-b",
4061:                 "coord-node-b",
4062:                 "198.51.100.171:51820",
4063:                 [171; 32],
4064:             ),
4065:         ] {
4066:             core.credentials
4067:                 .create(
4068:                     credential_id.to_string(),
4069:                     "admin".to_string(),
4070:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
4071:                     100,
4072:                     60,
4073:                 )
4074:                 .expect("credential should be created");
4075:             core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
4076:                 credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
4077:                 node_id: node_id.to_string(),
4078:                 hostname: node_id.to_string(),
4079:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
4080:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
4081:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
4082:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
4083:                 public_key,
4084:                 now_unix: 120,
4085:             })
4086:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
4087:         }
4088: 
4089:         let signed = core
4090:             .signed_traversal_coordination_record(TraversalCoordinationRecord {
4091:                 session_id: [0x11; 16],
4092:                 probe_start_unix: 205,
4093:                 node_a: "coord-node-a".to_string(),
4094:                 node_b: "coord-node-b".to_string(),
4095:                 issued_at_unix: 200,
4096:                 expires_at_unix: 225,
4097:                 nonce: [0x22; 16],
4098:             })
4099:             .expect("coordination record should be issued");
4100:         assert!(core.verify_signed_traversal_coordination_record(&signed));
4101: 
4102:         let mut tampered_payload = signed.clone();
4103:         tampered_payload.payload.push_str("node_b=coord-node-c\n");
4104:         assert!(!core.verify_signed_traversal_coordination_record(&tampered_payload));
4105: 
4106:         let mut tampered_signature = signed.clone();
4107:         tampered_signature.signature_hex = "00".repeat(64);
4108:         assert!(!core.verify_signed_traversal_coordination_record(&tampered_signature));
4109:     }
