4112:     fn traversal_coordination_record_enforces_validation_rules() {
4113:         let policy = PolicySet {
4114:             rules: vec![PolicyRule {
4115:                 src: "node:coord-node-a".to_string(),
4116:                 dst: "node:coord-node-b".to_string(),
4117:                 protocol: Protocol::Any,
4118:                 action: RuleAction::Allow,
4119:             }],
4120:         };
4121:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), policy);
4122:         for (credential_id, node_id, endpoint, public_key) in [
4123:             (
4124:                 "coord-cred-c",
4125:                 "coord-node-a",
4126:                 "198.51.100.172:51820",
4127:                 [172; 32],
4128:             ),
4129:             (
4130:                 "coord-cred-d",
4131:                 "coord-node-b",
4132:                 "198.51.100.173:51820",
4133:                 [173; 32],
4134:             ),
4135:         ] {
4136:             core.credentials
4137:                 .create(
4138:                     credential_id.to_string(),
4139:                     "admin".to_string(),
4140:                     "tag:servers".to_string(),
4141:                     100,
4142:                     60,
4143:                 )
4144:                 .expect("credential should be created");
4145:             core.enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
4146:                 credential_id: credential_id.to_string(),
4147:                 node_id: node_id.to_string(),
4148:                 hostname: node_id.to_string(),
4149:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
4150:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
4151:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
4152:                 endpoint: endpoint.to_string(),
4153:                 public_key,
4154:                 now_unix: 120,
4155:             })
4156:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
4157:         }
4158: 
4159:         let ttl_error = core
4160:             .signed_traversal_coordination_record(TraversalCoordinationRecord {
4161:                 session_id: [0x33; 16],
4162:                 probe_start_unix: 205,
4163:                 node_a: "coord-node-a".to_string(),
4164:                 node_b: "coord-node-b".to_string(),
4165:                 issued_at_unix: 200,
4166:                 expires_at_unix: 231,
4167:                 nonce: [0x44; 16],
4168:             })
4169:             .expect_err("ttl > 30s must be rejected");
4170:         assert!(ttl_error.to_string().contains("ttl exceeds"));
4171: 
4172:         let node_error = core
4173:             .signed_traversal_coordination_record(TraversalCoordinationRecord {
4174:                 session_id: [0x33; 16],
4175:                 probe_start_unix: 205,
4176:                 node_a: "coord-node-a".to_string(),
4177:                 node_b: "missing-node".to_string(),
4178:                 issued_at_unix: 200,
4179:                 expires_at_unix: 225,
4180:                 nonce: [0x44; 16],
4181:             })
4182:             .expect_err("missing node must be rejected");
4183:         assert!(node_error.to_string().contains("does not exist"));
4184: 
4185:         let same_node_error = core
4186:             .signed_traversal_coordination_record(TraversalCoordinationRecord {
4187:                 session_id: [0x33; 16],
4188:                 probe_start_unix: 205,
4189:                 node_a: "coord-node-a".to_string(),
4190:                 node_b: "coord-node-a".to_string(),
4191:                 issued_at_unix: 200,
4192:                 expires_at_unix: 225,
4193:                 nonce: [0x44; 16],
4194:             })
4195:             .expect_err("same node ids must be rejected");
4196:         assert!(same_node_error.to_string().contains("distinct"));
4197:     }

4278:                     ttl_secs: 600,
4279:                     max_uses: 3,
4280:                     storage_reference: "vault://rustynet/reusable-ok".to_string(),
4281:                 },
4282:                 policy,
4283:             )
4284:             .expect("reusable credential should be created");
4285:         assert_eq!(reusable.max_uses, 3);
4286:

4313:     #[test]
4314:     fn revocation_events_are_generation_tracked() {
4315:         let store = ThrowawayCredentialStore::default();
4316:         store
4317:             .create(
4318:                 "cred-revoked".to_string(),
4319:                 "admin".to_string(),
4320:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
4321:                 100,
4322:                 60,
4323:             )
4324:             .expect("credential should be created");
4325: 
4326:         let before = store
4327:             .revocation_generation()
4328:             .expect("generation should be readable");
4329:         assert_eq!(before, 0);
4330: 
4331:         store
4332:             .revoke("cred-revoked", 120)
4333:             .expect("credential should be revoked");
4334:         assert!(
4335:             store
4336:                 .is_revoked("cred-revoked")
4337:                 .expect("revocation status should be readable")
4338:         );
4339: 
4340:         let after = store
4341:             .revocation_generation()
4342:             .expect("generation should be readable");
4343:         assert_eq!(after, 1);
4344: 
4345:         let events = store
4346:             .revocations_since(0)
4347:             .expect("revocations should be readable");
4348:         assert_eq!(events.len(), 1);
4349:         assert_eq!(events[0].credential_id, "cred-revoked");
4350:         assert_eq!(events[0].generation, 1);
4351:     }

4380:                 mfa_enabled: false,

4382:                 updated_at_unix: 100,
4383:             })
4384:             .expect("admin user should be persisted");
4385: 
4386:         core.credentials
4387:             .create(
4388:                 "cred-persist".to_string(),
4389:                 "admin".to_string(),
4390:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),

4392:                 60,
4393:             )
4394:             .expect("credential should be created");

4396:             .insert_credential(&super::persistence::CredentialRow {
4397:                 credential_id: "cred-persist".to_string(),

4407:                 storage_policy: "throwaway_default".to_string(),
4408:             })
4409:             .expect("credential should be persisted before enrollment");
4410:         persistence
4411:             .insert_credential_audit_event("cred-persist", None, "created", 100, "admin")

4423:                     endpoint: "198.51.100.30:51820".to_string(),
4424:                     public_key: [7; 32],
4425:                     now_unix: 150,
4426:                 },
4427:                 &persistence,
4428:             )
4429:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
4430: 
4431:         assert_eq!(response.node_id, "node-persist");
4432:         assert!(
4433:             persistence
4434:                 .user_exists("alice@example.local")
4435:                 .expect("user lookup should succeed")
4436:         );
4437:         assert!(
4438:             persistence
4439:                 .node_exists("node-persist")
4440:                 .expect("node lookup should succeed")
4441:         );
4442:         let audit_count = persistence
4443:             .credential_audit_event_count("cred-persist")
4444:             .expect("audit count should be readable");
4445:         assert_eq!(audit_count, 2);
4446:     }

4608:     #[test]
4609:     fn signed_token_claims_are_verified_and_replay_guarded() {
4610:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), PolicySet::default());
4611:         core.credentials
4612:             .create(
4613:                 "cred-token".to_string(),
4614:                 "admin".to_string(),
4615:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
4616:                 100,
4617:                 60,
4618:             )
4619:             .expect("credential should be created");
4620: 
4621:         let response = core
4622:             .enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
4623:                 credential_id: "cred-token".to_string(),
4624:                 node_id: "node-token".to_string(),
4625:                 hostname: "mini-pc-token".to_string(),
4626:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
4627:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
4628:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
4629:                 endpoint: "198.51.100.60:51820".to_string(),
4630:                 public_key: [60; 32],
4631:                 now_unix: 120,
4632:             })
4633:             .expect("enrollment should succeed");
4634: 
4635:         assert!(core.verify_access_token(&response.access_token));
4636: 
4637:         let mut guard = AuthSurfaceGuard::default();
4638:         assert!(
4639:             core.validate_signed_token_and_nonce(&mut guard, &response.access_token, 121)
4640:                 .is_ok()
4641:         );
4642:         let replay = core.validate_signed_token_and_nonce(&mut guard, &response.access_token, 122);
4643:         assert_eq!(replay.err(), Some(AuthError::ReplayDetected));
4644: 
4645:         let mut tampered = response.access_token.clone();
4646:         tampered.claims.subject = "mallory@example.local".to_string();
4647:         let tampered_result = core.validate_signed_token_and_nonce(&mut guard, &tampered, 123);
4648:         assert_eq!(
4649:             tampered_result.err(),
4650:             Some(AuthError::TokenSignatureInvalid)
4651:         );
4652:     }

4654:     #[test]
4655:     fn enrollment_tokens_use_randomized_nonce_values() {
4656:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(b"control-secret".to_vec(), PolicySet::default());
4657:         core.credentials
4658:             .create(
4659:                 "cred-token-a".to_string(),
4660:                 "admin".to_string(),
4661:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
4662:                 100,
4663:                 60,
4664:             )
4665:             .expect("credential should be created");
4666:         core.credentials
4667:             .create(
4668:                 "cred-token-b".to_string(),
4669:                 "admin".to_string(),
4670:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
4671:                 100,
4672:                 60,
4673:             )
4674:             .expect("credential should be created");
4675: 
4676:         let token_a = core
4677:             .enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
4678:                 credential_id: "cred-token-a".to_string(),
4679:                 node_id: "node-token-a".to_string(),
4680:                 hostname: "mini-pc-token-a".to_string(),
4681:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
4682:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
4683:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
4684:                 endpoint: "198.51.100.60:51820".to_string(),
4685:                 public_key: [61; 32],
4686:                 now_unix: 120,
4687:             })
4688:             .expect("first enrollment should succeed")
4689:             .access_token;
4690:         let token_b = core
4691:             .enroll_with_throwaway(EnrollmentRequest {
4692:                 credential_id: "cred-token-b".to_string(),
4693:                 node_id: "node-token-b".to_string(),
4694:                 hostname: "mini-pc-token-b".to_string(),
4695:                 os: "linux".to_string(),
4696:                 tags: vec!["servers".to_string()],
4697:                 owner: "alice@example.local".to_string(),
4698:                 endpoint: "198.51.100.61:51820".to_string(),
4699:                 public_key: [62; 32],
4700:                 now_unix: 120,
4701:             })
4702:             .expect("second enrollment should succeed")
4703:             .access_token;
4704: 
4705:         assert_eq!(token_a.claims.nonce.len(), 32);
4706:         assert_eq!(token_b.claims.nonce.len(), 32);
4707:         assert!(
4708:             token_a
4709:                 .claims
4710:                 .nonce
4711:                 .bytes()
4712:                 .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_hexdigit())
4713:         );
4714:         assert!(
4715:             token_b
4716:                 .claims
4717:                 .nonce
4718:                 .bytes()
4719:                 .all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_hexdigit())
4720:         );
4721:         assert_ne!(token_a.claims.nonce, token_b.claims.nonce);
4722:     }

4724:     #[test]
4725:     fn signing_seed_derivation_uses_stable_hkdf_vectors() {
4726:         let assignment_seed =
4727:             derive_signing_seed(ASSIGNMENT_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, b"control-secret");
4728:         let endpoint_seed =
4729:             derive_signing_seed(ENDPOINT_HINT_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, b"control-secret");
4730:         let access_seed = derive_signing_seed(ACCESS_TOKEN_SIGNING_SEED_INFO_V1, b"control-secret");
4731:         assert_eq!(
4732:             hex_bytes(&assignment_seed),
4733:             "823450eb42a8e622264f36041cc7c2bbe1f39b90eabb622f1b73c35aa496764a"
4734:         );
4735:         assert_eq!(
4736:             hex_bytes(&endpoint_seed),
4737:             "45c6b4a8cf265219f296bb670a1cb8dfbe077a6dee3689540d346a4d6cdeb513"
4738:         );
4739:         assert_eq!(
4740:             hex_bytes(&access_seed),
4741:             "8ae34416e7e185594a0cd9e154b8ae2885e2f70e0e9470bfeb4ab128fb42aac4"
4742:         );
4743:         assert_ne!(assignment_seed, access_seed);
4744:         assert_ne!(assignment_seed, endpoint_seed);
4745:         assert_ne!(endpoint_seed, access_seed);
4746:     }
4747: 
4748:     #[test]
4749:     fn throwaway_credential_debug_redacts_sensitive_fields() {
4750:         let credential = ThrowawayCredentialStore::default()
4751:             .create(
4752:                 "cred-redact".to_string(),
4753:                 "admin".to_string(),
4754:                 "tag:servers".to_string(),
4755:                 100,
4756:                 60,
4757:             )
4758:             .expect("credential should be created");
4759:         let rendered = format!("{credential:?}");
4760:         assert!(!rendered.contains("cred-redact"));
4761:         assert!(!rendered.contains("throwaway_default"));
4762:         assert!(rendered.contains("REDACTED"));
4763:     }
4764: 
4765:     // ── RelaySessionToken tests ───
4766:     //
4767:     // These tests verify the relay session token signing, verification,
4768:     // expiry, and constant-time comparison behavior.
4769: 
4770:     #[test]
4771:     fn relay_session_token_sign_and_verify() {
4772:         use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
4773: 
4774:         let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1u8; 32]);
4775:         let vk = sk.verifying_key();
4776: 
4777:         let token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4778: 
4779:         // Signature must be valid
4780:         assert!(
4781:             token.verify_signature(&vk).is_ok(),
4782:             "token signature must verify with correct key"
4783:         );
4784: 
4785:         // Scope must be set correctly
4786:         assert_eq!(token.scope, RELAY_TOKEN_SCOPE);
4787:         assert_eq!(token.scope, "forward_ciphertext_only");
4788:     }
4789: 
4790:     #[test]
4791:     fn derive_endpoint_hint_signing_key_matches_control_plane_verifier() {
4792:         let signing_secret = vec![9u8; 32];
4793:         let core = ControlPlaneCore::new(signing_secret.clone(), PolicySet { rules: Vec::new() });
4794:         let derived = derive_endpoint_hint_signing_key(signing_secret);
4795:         assert_eq!(
4796:             derived.verifying_key().as_bytes(),
4797:             &core.endpoint_hint_verifying_key
4798:         );
4799:     }
4800: 
4801:     #[test]
4802:     fn relay_session_token_rejects_wrong_key() {

4804:

4806:         let wrong_sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[2u8; 32]);
4807:         let wrong_vk = wrong_sk.verifying_key();
4808: 
4809:         let token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4810: 
4811:         // Verification with wrong key must fail
4812:         assert!(

4814:             "token signature must not verify with wrong key"
4815:         );
4816:     }

4818:     #[test]
4819:     fn relay_session_token_rejects_tampered_signature() {
4820:         use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
4821: 
4822:         let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1u8; 32]);
4823:         let vk = sk.verifying_key();
4824: 
4825:         let mut token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4826:         token.signature[0] ^= 0xFF; // Flip bits in signature
4827: 
4828:         assert!(
4829:             token.verify_signature(&vk).is_err(),
4830:             "tampered signature must fail verification"
4831:         );
4832:     }

4834:     #[test]
4835:     fn relay_session_token_expiry_check() {
4836:         use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
4837: 
4838:         let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1u8; 32]);
4839:         let token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4840: 
4841:         let now_unix = std::time::SystemTime::now()
4842:             .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
4843:             .unwrap()
4844:             .as_secs();
4845: 
4846:         // Token should not be expired immediately
4847:         assert!(
4848:             !token.is_expired(now_unix, 0),
4849:             "fresh token must not be expired"
4850:         );
4851: 
4852:         // Token should be expired after TTL
4853:         assert!(
4854:             token.is_expired(now_unix + 120, 0),
4855:             "token must be expired after TTL"
4856:         );
4857: 
4858:         // Clock skew tolerance should extend validity
4859:         assert!(
4860:             !token.is_expired(now_unix + 70, 30),
4861:             "token with 30s skew tolerance must not be expired at 70s"
4862:         );
4863:     }
4864:

4870:         let token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 120);
4871: 
4872:         assert_eq!(token.ttl_secs(), 120, "TTL must match requested value");
4873:     }

4880:         let token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4881: 
4882:         // Same token compared to itself must be ct_eq
4883:         assert!(token.ct_eq(&token), "token must be ct_eq to itself");
4884: 
4885:         // Clone must also be ct_eq
4886:         let clone = token.clone();
4887:         assert!(token.ct_eq(&clone), "cloned token must be ct_eq");
4888:     }

4891:     fn relay_session_token_ct_eq_different_nonces() {
4892:         use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
4893: 
4894:         let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1u8; 32]);
4895:         let token_a = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4896:         let token_b = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4897: 
4898:         // Different nonces mean tokens are not ct_eq even if other fields match
4899:         assert!(
4900:             !token_a.ct_eq(&token_b),
4901:             "tokens with different nonces must not be ct_eq"
4902:         );
4903:     }

4908:

4910:         let token_a = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4911:         let token_c = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-c", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4912: 
4913:         assert!(
4914:             !token_a.ct_eq(&token_c),
4915:             "tokens with different peer_node_id must not be ct_eq"

4917:     }

4919:     #[test]
4920:     fn relay_session_token_canonical_payload_is_deterministic() {
4921:         use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
4922: 
4923:         let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1u8; 32]);
4924:         let token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4925: 
4926:         let payload1 = token.canonical_payload();
4927:         let payload2 = token.canonical_payload();
4928: 
4929:         assert_eq!(
4930:             payload1, payload2,
4931:             "canonical_payload must be deterministic"
4932:         );
4933: 
4934:         // Canonical payload must contain all signed fields
4935:         assert!(payload1.contains("version=1"));
4936:         assert!(payload1.contains("node_id=node-a"));
4937:         assert!(payload1.contains("peer_node_id=node-b"));
4938:         assert!(payload1.contains(&format!("relay_id={}", hex_bytes(&[0xAA; 16]))));
4939:         assert!(payload1.contains("scope=forward_ciphertext_only"));
4940:     }
4941:

4943:     fn relay_session_token_debug_redacts_sensitive_fields() {
4944:         use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
4945: 
4946:         let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1u8; 32]);
4947:         let token = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4948: 
4949:         let rendered = format!("{token:?}");
4950: 
4951:         // Debug output must redact sensitive fields
4952:         assert!(rendered.contains("REDACTED"), "debug must contain REDACTED");
4953:         assert!(
4954:             !rendered.contains("aaaa"),
4955:             "debug must not contain raw relay_id hex"
4956:         );
4957:         // node_id and peer_node_id are semi-public, so they may appear
4958:         assert!(rendered.contains("node-a"), "debug should show node_id");
4959:         assert!(
4960:             rendered.contains("node-b"),
4961:             "debug should show peer_node_id"
4962:         );
4963:     }
4964: 
4965:     #[test]
4966:     fn relay_session_token_nonce_is_random() {
4967:         use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
4968: 
4969:         let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1u8; 32]);
4970:         let token_a = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4971:         let token_b = RelaySessionToken::sign(&sk, "node-a", "node-b", [0xAA; 16], 60);
4972: 
4973:         // Each token must have a unique nonce (extremely unlikely to collide)
4974:         assert_ne!(
4975:             token_a.nonce, token_b.nonce,
4976:             "nonces must be unique per token"
4977:         );
4978:     }

===== /crates/rustynet-control/src/main.rs =====

   1: #![forbid(unsafe_code)]
   2: 
   3: use rustynet_crypto::NodeKeyPair;
   4: use rustynet_policy::{AccessRequest, PolicyRule, PolicySet, Protocol, RuleAction};
   5: 
   6: fn main() {
   7:     if let Err(err) = run() {
   8:         eprintln!("rustynet-control startup failed: {err}");
   9:         std::process::exit(1);
  10:     }
  11: }
  12: 
  13: fn run() -> Result<(), String> {
  14:     let args = std::env::args().skip(1).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  15:     match args.as_slice() {
  16:         [] => print_scaffold_ready(),
  17:         _ => Err(help_text()),
  18:     }
  19: }
  20: 
  21: fn print_scaffold_ready() -> Result<(), String> {
  22:     let policy = PolicySet {
  23:         rules: vec![PolicyRule {
  24:             src: "group:family".to_string(),
  25:             dst: "tag:servers".to_string(),
  26:             protocol: Protocol::Any,
  27:             action: RuleAction::Allow,
  28:         }],
  29:     };
  30: 
  31:     let decision = policy.evaluate(&AccessRequest {
  32:         src: "group:family".to_string(),
  33:         dst: "tag:servers".to_string(),
  34:         protocol: Protocol::Udp,
  35:     });
  36: 
  37:     let keypair = NodeKeyPair::from_raw([11; 32], [13; 32]).map_err(|err| err.to_string())?;
  38: 
  39:     println!(
  40:         "rustynet-control scaffold ready: decision={decision:?}, signing_pubkey_prefix={}",
  41:         keypair.public_key.as_bytes()[0]
  42:     );
  43: 
  44:     Ok(())
  45: }
  46: 
  47: fn help_text() -> String {
  48:     ["rustynet-control usage:", "  rustynet-control"].join("\n")
  49: }

===== /crates/rustynet-control/src/admin.rs =====

   1: #![forbid(unsafe_code)]
   2: 
   3: #[cfg(test)]
   4: use std::collections::BTreeMap;
   5: use std::fmt;
   6: use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;
   7: 
   8: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
   9: pub enum Role {
  10:     Viewer,
  11:     Operator,
  12:     Admin,
  13: }
  14: 
  15: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  16: pub enum AdminAction {
  17:     ViewNodes,
  18:     ManagePolicy,
  19:     ManageExitNodes,
  20:     ManageCredentials,
  21:     ManageUsers,
  22: }
  23: 
  24: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  25: pub struct AdminPrincipal {
  26:     pub user_id: String,
  27:     pub role: Role,
  28:     pub mfa_verified: bool,
  29: }
  30: 
  31: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  32: pub struct SessionSecurity {
  33:     pub secure_cookie: bool,
  34:     pub http_only_cookie: bool,
  35:     pub same_site_strict: bool,
  36:     pub csrf_token: String,
  37: }
  38: 
  39: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  40: pub enum AdminError {
  41:     Unauthorized,
  42:     MfaRequired,
  43:     CsrfInvalid,
  44:     SessionInsecure,
  45:     InvalidInput,
  46:     CommandRejected,
  47: }
  48: 
  49: impl fmt::Display for AdminError {
  50:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
  51:         match self {
  52:             AdminError::Unauthorized => f.write_str("unauthorized"),
  53:             AdminError::MfaRequired => f.write_str("mfa required"),
  54:             AdminError::CsrfInvalid => f.write_str("invalid csrf token"),
  55:             AdminError::SessionInsecure => f.write_str("insecure session policy"),
  56:             AdminError::InvalidInput => f.write_str("invalid input"),
  57:             AdminError::CommandRejected => f.write_str("privileged command rejected"),
  58:         }
  59:     }
  60: }
  61: 
  62: impl std::error::Error for AdminError {}
  63: 
  64: pub struct AdminAuthorizer;
  65: 
  66: impl AdminAuthorizer {
  67:     pub fn authorize(
  68:         principal: &AdminPrincipal,
  69:         action: AdminAction,
  70:         submitted_csrf_token: &str,
  71:         session: &SessionSecurity,
  72:     ) -> Result<(), AdminError> {
  73:         if !session.secure_cookie || !session.http_only_cookie || !session.same_site_strict {
  74:             return Err(AdminError::SessionInsecure);
  75:         }
  76:         if submitted_csrf_token
  77:             .as_bytes()
  78:             .ct_eq(session.csrf_token.as_bytes())
  79:             .unwrap_u8()
  80:             != 1
  81:         {
  82:             return Err(AdminError::CsrfInvalid);
  83:         }
  84: 
  85:         if !role_allows(principal.role, action) {
  86:             return Err(AdminError::Unauthorized);
  87:         }
  88:         if is_privileged(action) && !principal.mfa_verified {
  89:             return Err(AdminError::MfaRequired);
  90:         }
  91:         Ok(())
  92:     }
  93: }
  94: 
  95: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  96: pub struct WebSecurityHeaders {
  97:     pub x_frame_options: String,
  98:     pub content_security_policy: String,
  99:     pub referrer_policy: String,
 100: }
 101: 
 102: #[cfg(test)]
 103: fn default_web_security_headers() -> WebSecurityHeaders {
 104:     WebSecurityHeaders {
 105:         x_frame_options: "DENY".to_string(),
 106:         content_security_policy: "frame-ancestors 'none'".to_string(),
 107:         referrer_policy: "no-referrer".to_string(),
 108:     }
 109: }

 111: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
 112: pub struct AdminApiState {
 113:     nodes: Vec<String>,
 114:     policies: Vec<String>,
 115:     exit_nodes: Vec<String>,
 116:     credentials: Vec<String>,
 117: }
 118: 
 119: impl AdminApiState {
 120:     pub fn add_node(&mut self, node_id: impl Into<String>) {
 121:         self.nodes.push(node_id.into());
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     pub fn list_nodes(
 125:         &self,
 126:         principal: &AdminPrincipal,
 127:         submitted_csrf_token: &str,
 128:         session: &SessionSecurity,
 129:     ) -> Result<Vec<String>, AdminError> {
 130:         AdminAuthorizer::authorize(
 131:             principal,
 132:             AdminAction::ViewNodes,
 133:             submitted_csrf_token,
 134:             session,
 135:         )?;
 136:         Ok(self.nodes.clone())
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     pub fn update_policy(
 140:         &mut self,
 141:         principal: &AdminPrincipal,
 142:         submitted_csrf_token: &str,
 143:         session: &SessionSecurity,
 144:         policy: String,
 145:     ) -> Result<(), AdminError> {
 146:         if policy.trim().is_empty() {
 147:             return Err(AdminError::InvalidInput);
 148:         }
 149:         AdminAuthorizer::authorize(
 150:             principal,
 151:             AdminAction::ManagePolicy,
 152:             submitted_csrf_token,
 153:             session,
 154:         )?;
 155:         self.policies.push(policy);
 156:         Ok(())
 157:     }
 158: 
 159:     pub fn set_exit_node(
 160:         &mut self,
 161:         principal: &AdminPrincipal,
 162:         submitted_csrf_token: &str,
 163:         session: &SessionSecurity,
 164:         node_id: String,
 165:     ) -> Result<(), AdminError> {
 166:         if node_id.trim().is_empty() {
 167:             return Err(AdminError::InvalidInput);
 168:         }
 169:         AdminAuthorizer::authorize(
 170:             principal,
 171:             AdminAction::ManageExitNodes,
 172:             submitted_csrf_token,
 173:             session,
 174:         )?;
 175:         self.exit_nodes.push(node_id);
 176:         Ok(())
 177:     }
 178: 
 179:     pub fn create_credential(
 180:         &mut self,
 181:         principal: &AdminPrincipal,
 182:         submitted_csrf_token: &str,
 183:         session: &SessionSecurity,
 184:         credential_id: String,
 185:     ) -> Result<(), AdminError> {
 186:         if credential_id.trim().is_empty() {
 187:             return Err(AdminError::InvalidInput);
 188:         }
 189:         AdminAuthorizer::authorize(
 190:             principal,
 191:             AdminAction::ManageCredentials,
 192:             submitted_csrf_token,
 193:             session,
 194:         )?;
 195:         self.credentials.push(credential_id);
 196:         Ok(())
 197:     }
 198: }
 199: 
 200: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 201: pub struct PrivilegedCommand {
 202:     pub program: String,
 203:     pub args: Vec<String>,
 204:     pub run_as_user: String,
 205: }
 206: 
 207: #[cfg(test)]
 208: fn validate_privileged_command(command: &PrivilegedCommand) -> Result<(), AdminError> {
 209:     if !command.program.starts_with('/') {
 210:         return Err(AdminError::CommandRejected);
 211:     }
 212:     if command.run_as_user.trim().is_empty() || command.run_as_user == "root" {
 213:         return Err(AdminError::CommandRejected);
 214:     }
 215:     if command.args.iter().any(|arg| contains_shell_meta(arg)) {
 216:         return Err(AdminError::CommandRejected);
 217:     }
 218:     Ok(())
 219: }
 220: 
 221: #[cfg(test)]
 222: fn command_preview(command: &PrivilegedCommand) -> Result<String, AdminError> {
 223:     validate_privileged_command(command)?;
 224:     let mut output = String::new();
 225:     output.push_str("program=");
 226:     output.push_str(&command.program);
 227:     output.push_str(" args=");
 228:     output.push_str(&command.args.join(" "));
 229:     output.push_str(" run_as=");
 230:     output.push_str(&command.run_as_user);
 231:     Ok(output)
 232: }
 233: 
 234: fn role_allows(role: Role, action: AdminAction) -> bool {
 235:     match role {
 236:         Role::Viewer => matches!(action, AdminAction::ViewNodes),
 237:         Role::Operator => matches!(
 238:             action,
 239:             AdminAction::ViewNodes | AdminAction::ManageExitNodes
 240:         ),
 241:         Role::Admin => true,
 242:     }
 243: }
 244: 
 245: fn is_privileged(action: AdminAction) -> bool {
 246:     matches!(
 247:         action,
 248:         AdminAction::ManagePolicy
 249:             | AdminAction::ManageExitNodes
 250:             | AdminAction::ManageCredentials
 251:             | AdminAction::ManageUsers
 252:     )
 253: }
 254: 
 255: #[cfg(test)]
 256: fn contains_shell_meta(value: &str) -> bool {
 257:     value.chars().any(|ch| {
 258:         matches!(
 259:             ch,
 260:             ';' | '|' | '&' | '$' | '`' | '<' | '>' | '(' | ')' | '{' | '}'
 261:         )
 262:     })
 263: }
 264: 
 265: #[cfg(test)]
 266: fn policy_bootstrap_defaults() -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
 267:     let mut defaults = BTreeMap::new();
 268:     defaults.insert("mode".to_string(), "default-deny".to_string());
 269:     defaults.insert("allow_all".to_string(), "false".to_string());
 270:     defaults
 271: }

 273: #[cfg(test)]
 274: mod tests {
 275:     use super::{
 276:         AdminApiState, AdminError, AdminPrincipal, PrivilegedCommand, Role, SessionSecurity,
 277:         command_preview, default_web_security_headers, policy_bootstrap_defaults,
 278:         validate_privileged_command,
 279:     };
 280: 
 281:     fn secure_session() -> SessionSecurity {
 282:         SessionSecurity {
 283:             secure_cookie: true,
 284:             http_only_cookie: true,
 285:             same_site_strict: true,
 286:             csrf_token: "csrf-123".to_string(),
 287:         }
 288:     }
 289: 
 290:     #[test]
 291:     fn rbac_is_deny_by_default_for_privileged_actions() {
 292:         let mut api = AdminApiState::default();
 293:         let viewer = AdminPrincipal {
 294:             user_id: "viewer".to_string(),
 295:             role: Role::Viewer,
 296:             mfa_verified: true,
 297:         };
 298: 
 299:         let result = api.update_policy(
 300:             &viewer,
 301:             "csrf-123",
 302:             &secure_session(),
 303:             "allow group:family -> tag:servers".to_string(),
 304:         );
 305:         assert_eq!(result.err(), Some(AdminError::Unauthorized));
 306:     }
 307: 
 308:     #[test]
 309:     fn mfa_is_required_for_privileged_mutations() {
 310:         let mut api = AdminApiState::default();
 311:         let admin_without_mfa = AdminPrincipal {
 312:             user_id: "admin".to_string(),
 313:             role: Role::Admin,
 314:             mfa_verified: false,
 315:         };
 316: 
 317:         let result = api.update_policy(
 318:             &admin_without_mfa,
 319:             "csrf-123",
 320:             &secure_session(),
 321:             "allow group:family -> tag:servers".to_string(),
 322:         );
 323:         assert_eq!(result.err(), Some(AdminError::MfaRequired));
 324:     }

 331:             user_id: "admin".to_string(),
 332:             role: Role::Admin,
 333:             mfa_verified: true,
 334:         };
 335:

 341:         let insecure_err = api.list_nodes(&admin, "csrf-123", &insecure);
 342:         assert_eq!(insecure_err.err(), Some(AdminError::SessionInsecure));
 343:     }

===== /crates/rustynet-control/src/ga.rs =====

   1: #![forbid(unsafe_code)]
   2: 
   3: use std::collections::BTreeSet;
   4: 
   5: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
   6: pub struct ApiVersion {
   7:     pub major: u16,
   8:     pub minor: u16,
   9: }
  10: 
  11: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  12: pub enum CompatibilityDecision {
  13:     Supported,
  14:     Deprecated,
  15:     Unsupported,
  16: }
  17: 
  18: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  19: pub enum CompatibilityPolicyError {
  20:     InvalidSupportWindow,
  21: }
  22: 
  23: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  24: pub struct CompatibilityPolicy {
  25:     pub minimum_supported_client: ApiVersion,
  26:     pub latest_server: ApiVersion,
  27:     pub deprecation_window_days: u32,
  28: }
  29: 
  30: impl CompatibilityPolicy {
  31:     pub fn validate_configuration(&self) -> Result<(), CompatibilityPolicyError> {
  32:         if self.minimum_supported_client > self.latest_server || self.deprecation_window_days == 0 {
  33:             return Err(CompatibilityPolicyError::InvalidSupportWindow);
  34:         }
  35:         Ok(())
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     pub fn evaluate(&self, client_version: ApiVersion) -> CompatibilityDecision {
  39:         if self.validate_configuration().is_err() {
  40:             return CompatibilityDecision::Unsupported;
  41:         }
  42: 
  43:         if client_version.major != self.latest_server.major {
  44:             return CompatibilityDecision::Unsupported;
  45:         }
  46: 
  47:         if client_version < self.minimum_supported_client {
  48:             return CompatibilityDecision::Unsupported;
  49:         }
  50: 
  51:         if client_version.minor < self.latest_server.minor {
  52:             return CompatibilityDecision::Deprecated;
  53:         }
  54: 
  55:         CompatibilityDecision::Supported
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     pub fn is_supported(&self, client_version: ApiVersion) -> bool {
  59:         matches!(
  60:             self.evaluate(client_version),
  61:             CompatibilityDecision::Supported | CompatibilityDecision::Deprecated
  62:         )
  63:     }
  64: }
  65: 
  66: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  67: pub enum AlgorithmLifecycle {
  68:     Allowed,
  69:     Deprecated,
  70:     Denied,
  71: }
  72: 
  73: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  74: pub struct CryptoDeprecationRecord {
  75:     pub algorithm: String,
  76:     pub deprecates_at_unix: u64,
  77:     pub removal_at_unix: u64,
  78: }
  79: 
  80: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  81: pub enum CryptoDeprecationError {
  82:     DuplicateAlgorithm,
  83:     InvalidDeprecationWindow,
  84: }
  85: 
  86: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
  87: pub struct CryptoDeprecationCalendar {
  88:     pub records: Vec<CryptoDeprecationRecord>,
  89: }
  90: 
  91: impl CryptoDeprecationCalendar {
  92:     pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), CryptoDeprecationError> {
  93:         let mut names = BTreeSet::new();
  94:         for record in &self.records {
  95:             if record.algorithm.trim().is_empty() {
  96:                 return Err(CryptoDeprecationError::InvalidDeprecationWindow);
  97:             }
  98:             if record.removal_at_unix <= record.deprecates_at_unix {
  99:                 return Err(CryptoDeprecationError::InvalidDeprecationWindow);
 100:             }
 101:             if !names.insert(record.algorithm.to_ascii_lowercase()) {
 102:                 return Err(CryptoDeprecationError::DuplicateAlgorithm);
 103:             }
 104:         }
 105:         Ok(())
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     pub fn lifecycle_for(&self, algorithm: &str, now_unix: u64) -> AlgorithmLifecycle {
 109:         if self.validate().is_err() {
 110:             return AlgorithmLifecycle::Denied;
 111:         }
 112: 
 113:         let normalized = algorithm.to_ascii_lowercase();
 114:         let Some(record) = self
 115:             .records
 116:             .iter()
 117:             .find(|entry| entry.algorithm.to_ascii_lowercase() == normalized)
 118:         else {
 119:             return AlgorithmLifecycle::Allowed;
 120:         };
 121: 
 122:         if now_unix >= record.removal_at_unix {
 123:             return AlgorithmLifecycle::Denied;
 124:         }
 125:         if now_unix >= record.deprecates_at_unix {
 126:             return AlgorithmLifecycle::Deprecated;
 127:         }
 128: 
 129:         AlgorithmLifecycle::Allowed
 130:     }
 131: 
 132:     pub fn denied_algorithms(&self, now_unix: u64) -> Vec<String> {
 133:         if self.validate().is_err() {
 134:             return self
 135:                 .records
 136:                 .iter()
 137:                 .map(|record| record.algorithm.to_ascii_lowercase())
 138:                 .collect();
 139:         }
 140: 
 141:         self.records
 142:             .iter()
 143:             .filter(|record| now_unix >= record.removal_at_unix)
 144:             .map(|record| record.algorithm.to_ascii_lowercase())
 145:             .collect()
 146:     }
 147: }
 148: 
 149: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 150: pub enum CompatibilityExceptionError {
 151:     MissingRiskAcceptance,
 152:     NotEnabled,
 153:     Expired,
 154:     InvalidTtl,
 155: }
 156: 
 157: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 158: pub struct InsecureCompatibilityException {
 159:     pub mode: String,
 160:     pub risk_acceptance_id: String,
 161:     pub approved_by: String,
 162:     pub created_at_unix: u64,
 163:     pub expires_at_unix: u64,
 164:     pub enabled: bool,
 165: }
 166: 
 167: impl InsecureCompatibilityException {
 168:     pub fn validate_active(&self, now_unix: u64) -> Result<(), CompatibilityExceptionError> {
 169:         if self.risk_acceptance_id.trim().is_empty() || self.approved_by.trim().is_empty() {
 170:             return Err(CompatibilityExceptionError::MissingRiskAcceptance);
 171:         }
 172:         if !self.enabled {
 173:             return Err(CompatibilityExceptionError::NotEnabled);
 174:         }
 175:         if self.expires_at_unix <= self.created_at_unix {
 176:             return Err(CompatibilityExceptionError::InvalidTtl);
 177:         }
 178:         if now_unix > self.expires_at_unix {
 179:             return Err(CompatibilityExceptionError::Expired);
 180:         }
 181:         Ok(())
 182:     }
 183: 
 184:     pub fn is_active(&self, now_unix: u64) -> bool {
 185:         self.validate_active(now_unix).is_ok()
 186:     }
 187: }

 189: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
 190: pub struct ErrorBudgetGate {
 191:     pub availability_slo_percent: f64,
 192:     pub measured_availability_percent: f64,
 193:     pub max_error_budget_consumed_percent: f64,
 194:     pub measured_error_budget_consumed_percent: f64,
 195: }

 197: impl ErrorBudgetGate {
 198:     pub fn passes(&self) -> bool {
 199:         self.measured_availability_percent >= self.availability_slo_percent
 200:             && self.measured_error_budget_consumed_percent <= self.max_error_budget_consumed_percent
 201:     }

 214: impl PerformanceBudgetSnapshot {

 216:         self.idle_cpu_percent <= 2.0
 217:             && self.idle_memory_mb <= 120.0
 218:             && self.reconnect_seconds <= 5.0
 219:             && self.route_apply_p95_seconds <= 2.0
 220:             && self.throughput_overhead_percent <= 15.0
 221:             && self.soak_test_hours >= 24.0

 226: pub struct DisasterRecoveryValidation {

 228:     pub rpo_target_minutes: u32,
 229:     pub rto_target_minutes: u32,
 230:     pub measured_rpo_minutes: u32,
 231:     pub measured_rto_minutes: u32,
 232:     pub restore_integrity_verified: bool,
 233: }
 234: 
 235: impl DisasterRecoveryValidation {
 236:     pub fn passes(&self) -> bool {
 237:         self.region_count >= 2
 238:             && self.measured_rpo_minutes <= self.rpo_target_minutes
 239:             && self.measured_rto_minutes <= self.rto_target_minutes
 240:             && self.restore_integrity_verified
 241:     }

 244: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 245: pub struct BackendAgilityValidation {
 246:     pub default_backend_configured: bool,
 247:     pub additional_backend_paths: u8,
 248:     pub conformance_passed: bool,
 249:     pub security_review_complete: bool,
 250:     pub backend_adapter_boundary_preserved: bool,
 251:     pub protocol_leakage_detected: bool,
 252: }
 253: 
 254: impl BackendAgilityValidation {
 255:     pub fn passes(&self) -> bool {
 256:         self.default_backend_configured
 257:             && self.additional_backend_paths >= 1
 258:             && self.conformance_passed
 259:             && self.security_review_complete
 260:             && self.backend_adapter_boundary_preserved
 261:             && !self.protocol_leakage_detected
 262:     }
 263: }

 265: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
 266: pub struct GaReleaseReadiness {
 267:     pub compatibility_ok: bool,
 268:     pub error_budget_gate: ErrorBudgetGate,
 269:     pub performance_budget: PerformanceBudgetSnapshot,
 270:     pub dr_validation: DisasterRecoveryValidation,
 271:     pub backend_agility: BackendAgilityValidation,
 272:     pub incident_drill_completed: bool,
 273:     pub oncall_readiness_confirmed: bool,
 274: }
 275: 
 276: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 277: pub enum GaGateFailure {
 278:     Compatibility,
 279:     ErrorBudget,
 280:     PerformanceBudget,
 281:     DisasterRecovery,
 282:     BackendAgility,
 283:     IncidentDrill,
 284:     OnCallReadiness,
 285: }
 286: 
 287: impl GaReleaseReadiness {
 288:     pub fn evaluate(&self) -> Result<(), GaGateFailure> {
 289:         if !self.compatibility_ok {
 290:             return Err(GaGateFailure::Compatibility);
 291:         }
 292:         if !self.error_budget_gate.passes() {
 293:             return Err(GaGateFailure::ErrorBudget);
 294:         }
 295:         if !self.performance_budget.passes() {
 296:             return Err(GaGateFailure::PerformanceBudget);
 297:         }
 298:         if !self.dr_validation.passes() {
 299:             return Err(GaGateFailure::DisasterRecovery);
 300:         }
 301:         if !self.backend_agility.passes() {
 302:             return Err(GaGateFailure::BackendAgility);
 303:         }
 304:         if !self.incident_drill_completed {
 305:             return Err(GaGateFailure::IncidentDrill);
 306:         }
 307:         if !self.oncall_readiness_confirmed {
 308:             return Err(GaGateFailure::OnCallReadiness);
 309:         }
 310: 
 311:         Ok(())
 312:     }
 313: }

 326:     fn compatibility_policy_blocks_outdated_or_cross_major_clients() {
 327:         let policy = CompatibilityPolicy {
 328:             minimum_supported_client: ApiVersion { major: 1, minor: 2 },
 329:             latest_server: ApiVersion { major: 1, minor: 8 },
 330:             deprecation_window_days: 90,
 331:         };
 332: 
 333:         assert_eq!(
 334:             policy.evaluate(ApiVersion { major: 1, minor: 8 }),
 335:             CompatibilityDecision::Supported
 336:         );
 337:         assert_eq!(
 338:             policy.evaluate(ApiVersion { major: 1, minor: 3 }),
 339:             CompatibilityDecision::Deprecated
 340:         );
 341:         assert_eq!(
 342:             policy.evaluate(ApiVersion { major: 1, minor: 1 }),
 343:             CompatibilityDecision::Unsupported
 344:         );
 345:         assert_eq!(
 346:             policy.evaluate(ApiVersion { major: 2, minor: 0 }),
 347:             CompatibilityDecision::Unsupported

  20: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  21: pub enum MembershipNodeStatus {
  22:     Active,
  23:     Revoked,
  24:     Quarantined,
  25: }
  26: 
  27: impl MembershipNodeStatus {
  28:     fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
  29:         match self {
  30:             MembershipNodeStatus::Active => "active",
  31:             MembershipNodeStatus::Revoked => "revoked",
  32:             MembershipNodeStatus::Quarantined => "quarantined",
  33:         }
  34:     }
  35: 
  36:     fn parse(value: &str) -> Result<Self, MembershipError> {
  37:         match value {
  38:             "active" => Ok(MembershipNodeStatus::Active),
  39:             "revoked" => Ok(MembershipNodeStatus::Revoked),
  40:             "quarantined" => Ok(MembershipNodeStatus::Quarantined),
  41:             _ => Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
  42:                 "invalid membership node status {value}"
  43:             ))),
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: }

  51:     Guardian,

  53:
