  57:             MembershipApproverRole::Owner => "owner",
  58:             MembershipApproverRole::Guardian => "guardian",
  59:         }
  60:     }
  61: 
  62:     fn parse(value: &str) -> Result<Self, MembershipError> {
  63:         match value {
  64:             "owner" => Ok(MembershipApproverRole::Owner),
  65:             "guardian" => Ok(MembershipApproverRole::Guardian),
  66:             _ => Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
  67:                 "invalid membership approver role {value}"
  68:             ))),
  69:         }
  70:     }
  71: }
  72: 
  73: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  74: pub enum MembershipApproverStatus {
  75:     Active,
  76:     Revoked,
  77: }
  78: 
  79: impl MembershipApproverStatus {
  80:     fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
  81:         match self {
  82:             MembershipApproverStatus::Active => "active",
  83:             MembershipApproverStatus::Revoked => "revoked",
  84:         }
  85:     }
  86: 
  87:     fn parse(value: &str) -> Result<Self, MembershipError> {
  88:         match value {
  89:             "active" => Ok(MembershipApproverStatus::Active),
  90:             "revoked" => Ok(MembershipApproverStatus::Revoked),
  91:             _ => Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
  92:                 "invalid membership approver status {value}"
  93:             ))),
  94:         }
  95:     }
  96: }
  97: 
  98: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  99: pub struct MembershipNode {
 100:     pub node_id: String,
 101:     pub node_pubkey_hex: String,
 102:     pub owner: String,
 103:     pub status: MembershipNodeStatus,
 104:     pub roles: Vec<String>,
 105:     pub joined_at_unix: u64,
 106:     pub updated_at_unix: u64,
 107: }
 108: 
 109: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 110: pub struct MembershipApprover {
 111:     pub approver_id: String,
 112:     pub approver_pubkey_hex: String,
 113:     pub role: MembershipApproverRole,
 114:     pub status: MembershipApproverStatus,
 115:     pub created_at_unix: u64,
 116: }
 117: 
 118: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 119: pub struct MembershipState {
 120:     pub schema_version: u8,
 121:     pub network_id: String,
 122:     pub epoch: u64,
 123:     pub nodes: Vec<MembershipNode>,
 124:     pub approver_set: Vec<MembershipApprover>,
 125:     pub quorum_threshold: u8,
 126:     pub metadata_hash: Option<String>,
 127: }
 128: 
 129: impl MembershipState {
 130:     pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
 131:         if self.schema_version != MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION {
 132:             return Err(MembershipError::UnsupportedVersion(self.schema_version));
 133:         }
 134:         if self.network_id.trim().is_empty() {
 135:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 136:                 "network id must not be empty".to_string(),
 137:             ));
 138:         }
 139:         if self.quorum_threshold == 0 {
 140:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 141:                 "quorum threshold must be at least 1".to_string(),
 142:             ));
 143:         }
 144: 
 145:         let mut node_ids = HashSet::new();
 146:         for node in &self.nodes {
 147:             if node.node_id.trim().is_empty() {
 148:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 149:                     "node id must not be empty".to_string(),
 150:                 ));
 151:             }
 152:             if !node_ids.insert(node.node_id.clone()) {
 153:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
 154:                     "duplicate node id {}",
 155:                     node.node_id
 156:                 )));
 157:             }
 158:             decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(&node.node_pubkey_hex)?;
 159:             if node.owner.trim().is_empty() {
 160:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
 161:                     "node {} has empty owner",
 162:                     node.node_id
 163:                 )));
 164:             }
 165:         }
 166: 
 167:         let mut approver_ids = HashSet::new();
 168:         let active_approvers = self
 169:             .approver_set
 170:             .iter()
 171:             .filter(|approver| approver.status == MembershipApproverStatus::Active)
 172:             .count() as u8;
 173:         for approver in &self.approver_set {
 174:             if approver.approver_id.trim().is_empty() {
 175:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 176:                     "approver id must not be empty".to_string(),
 177:                 ));
 178:             }
 179:             if !approver_ids.insert(approver.approver_id.clone()) {
 180:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
 181:                     "duplicate approver id {}",
 182:                     approver.approver_id
 183:                 )));
 184:             }
 185:             decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(&approver.approver_pubkey_hex)?;
 186:         }
 187: 
 188:         if active_approvers == 0 {
 189:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 190:                 "at least one active approver is required".to_string(),
 191:             ));
 192:         }
 193:         if self.quorum_threshold > active_approvers {
 194:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
 195:                 "quorum threshold {} exceeds active approver count {}",
 196:                 self.quorum_threshold, active_approvers
 197:             )));
 198:         }
 199:         Ok(())
 200:     }
 201: 
 202:     pub fn canonical_payload(&self) -> Result<String, MembershipError> {
 203:         self.validate()?;
 204:         let mut nodes = self.nodes.clone();
 205:         nodes.sort_by(|left, right| left.node_id.cmp(&right.node_id));
 206:         let mut approvers = self.approver_set.clone();
 207:         approvers.sort_by(|left, right| left.approver_id.cmp(&right.approver_id));
 208: 
 209:         let mut out = String::new();
 210:         out.push_str(&format!("version={}\n", self.schema_version));
 211:         out.push_str(&format!("network_id={}\n", self.network_id));
 212:         out.push_str(&format!("epoch={}\n", self.epoch));
 213:         out.push_str(&format!("quorum_threshold={}\n", self.quorum_threshold));
 214:         out.push_str(&format!(
 215:             "metadata_hash={}\n",
 216:             self.metadata_hash.as_deref().unwrap_or("")
 217:         ));
 218:         out.push_str(&format!("node_count={}\n", nodes.len()));
 219:         for (index, node) in nodes.iter().enumerate() {
 220:             out.push_str(&format!("node.{index}.node_id={}\n", node.node_id));
 221:             out.push_str(&format!(
 222:                 "node.{index}.node_pubkey_hex={}\n",
 223:                 node.node_pubkey_hex
 224:             ));
 225:             out.push_str(&format!("node.{index}.owner={}\n", node.owner));
 226:             out.push_str(&format!("node.{index}.status={}\n", node.status.as_str()));
 227:             let mut roles = node.roles.clone();
 228:             roles.sort();
 229:             roles.dedup();
 230:             out.push_str(&format!("node.{index}.roles={}\n", roles.join(",")));
 231:             out.push_str(&format!(
 232:                 "node.{index}.joined_at_unix={}\n",
 233:                 node.joined_at_unix
 234:             ));
 235:             out.push_str(&format!(
 236:                 "node.{index}.updated_at_unix={}\n",
 237:                 node.updated_at_unix
 238:             ));
 239:         }
 240:         out.push_str(&format!("approver_count={}\n", approvers.len()));
 241:         for (index, approver) in approvers.iter().enumerate() {
 242:             out.push_str(&format!(
 243:                 "approver.{index}.approver_id={}\n",
 244:                 approver.approver_id
 245:             ));
 246:             out.push_str(&format!(
 247:                 "approver.{index}.approver_pubkey_hex={}\n",
 248:                 approver.approver_pubkey_hex
 249:             ));
 250:             out.push_str(&format!(
 251:                 "approver.{index}.role={}\n",
 252:                 approver.role.as_str()
 253:             ));
 254:             out.push_str(&format!(
 255:                 "approver.{index}.status={}\n",
 256:                 approver.status.as_str()
 257:             ));
 258:             out.push_str(&format!(
 259:                 "approver.{index}.created_at_unix={}\n",
 260:                 approver.created_at_unix
 261:             ));
 262:         }
 263: 
 264:         Ok(out)
 265:     }
 266: 
 267:     pub fn state_root_hex(&self) -> Result<String, MembershipError> {
 268:         let payload = self.canonical_payload()?;
 269:         Ok(sha256_hex(payload.as_bytes()))
 270:     }
 271: 
 272:     pub fn active_nodes(&self) -> BTreeSet<String> {
 273:         self.nodes
 274:             .iter()
 275:             .filter(|node| node.status == MembershipNodeStatus::Active)
 276:             .map(|node| node.node_id.clone())
 277:             .collect()
 278:     }
 279: 
 280:     pub fn active_approvers(&self) -> HashMap<String, MembershipApprover> {
 281:         self.approver_set
 282:             .iter()
 283:             .filter(|approver| approver.status == MembershipApproverStatus::Active)
 284:             .map(|approver| (approver.approver_id.clone(), approver.clone()))
 285:             .collect()
 286:     }
 287: 
 288:     pub fn owner_approvers(&self) -> BTreeSet<String> {
 289:         self.approver_set
 290:             .iter()
 291:             .filter(|approver| {
 292:                 approver.status == MembershipApproverStatus::Active
 293:                     && approver.role == MembershipApproverRole::Owner
 294:             })
 295:             .map(|approver| approver.approver_id.clone())
 296:             .collect()
 297:     }
 298: }
 299: 
 300: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 301: pub enum MembershipOperation {
 302:     AddNode(MembershipNode),
 303:     RemoveNode {
 304:         node_id: String,
 305:     },
 306:     RevokeNode {
 307:         node_id: String,
 308:     },
 309:     RestoreNode {
 310:         node_id: String,
 311:     },
 312:     RotateNodeKey {
 313:         node_id: String,
 314:         new_pubkey_hex: String,
 315:     },
 316:     RotateApprover(MembershipApprover),
 317:     SetQuorum {
 318:         threshold: u8,
 319:     },
 320: }
 321: 
 322: impl MembershipOperation {
 323:     fn operation_name(&self) -> &'static str {
 324:         match self {
 325:             MembershipOperation::AddNode(_) => "add_node",
 326:             MembershipOperation::RemoveNode { .. } => "remove_node",
 327:             MembershipOperation::RevokeNode { .. } => "revoke_node",
 328:             MembershipOperation::RestoreNode { .. } => "restore_node",
 329:             MembershipOperation::RotateNodeKey { .. } => "rotate_node_key",
 330:             MembershipOperation::RotateApprover(_) => "rotate_approver",
 331:             MembershipOperation::SetQuorum { .. } => "set_quorum",
 332:         }
 333:     }
 334: 
 335:     fn requires_owner_signer(&self) -> bool {
 336:         matches!(
 337:             self,
 338:             MembershipOperation::RotateApprover(_) | MembershipOperation::SetQuorum { .. }
 339:         )
 340:     }
 341: }
 342: 
 343: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 344: pub struct MembershipUpdateRecord {
 345:     pub network_id: String,
 346:     pub update_id: String,
 347:     pub operation: MembershipOperation,
 348:     pub target: String,
 349:     pub prev_state_root: String,
 350:     pub new_state_root: String,
 351:     pub epoch_prev: u64,
 352:     pub epoch_new: u64,
 353:     pub created_at_unix: u64,
 354:     pub expires_at_unix: u64,
 355:     pub reason_code: String,
 356:     pub policy_context: Option<String>,
 357: }
 358: 
 359: impl MembershipUpdateRecord {
 360:     pub fn canonical_payload(&self) -> Result<String, MembershipError> {
 361:         if self.network_id.trim().is_empty() {
 362:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 363:                 "membership update network id must not be empty".to_string(),
 364:             ));
 365:         }
 366:         if self.update_id.trim().is_empty() {
 367:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 368:                 "membership update id must not be empty".to_string(),
 369:             ));
 370:         }
 371:         if self.target.trim().is_empty() {
 372:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 373:                 "membership update target must not be empty".to_string(),
 374:             ));
 375:         }
 376:         if self.epoch_new != self.epoch_prev.saturating_add(1) {
 377:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidTransition(
 378:                 "membership update epoch chain must increment by exactly 1",
 379:             ));
 380:         }
 381:         if self.created_at_unix >= self.expires_at_unix {
 382:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 383:                 "membership update expires_at_unix must be greater than created_at_unix"
 384:                     .to_string(),
 385:             ));
 386:         }
 387: 
 388:         let mut out = String::new();
 389:         out.push_str(&format!("version={MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION}\n"));
 390:         out.push_str(&format!("network_id={}\n", self.network_id));
 391:         out.push_str(&format!("update_id={}\n", self.update_id));
 392:         out.push_str(&format!("operation={}\n", self.operation.operation_name()));
 393:         out.push_str(&format!("target={}\n", self.target));
 394:         out.push_str(&format!("prev_state_root={}\n", self.prev_state_root));
 395:         out.push_str(&format!("new_state_root={}\n", self.new_state_root));
 396:         out.push_str(&format!("epoch_prev={}\n", self.epoch_prev));
 397:         out.push_str(&format!("epoch_new={}\n", self.epoch_new));
 398:         out.push_str(&format!("created_at_unix={}\n", self.created_at_unix));
 399:         out.push_str(&format!("expires_at_unix={}\n", self.expires_at_unix));
 400:         out.push_str(&format!("reason_code={}\n", self.reason_code));
 401:         out.push_str(&format!(
 402:             "policy_context={}\n",
 403:             self.policy_context.as_deref().unwrap_or("")
 404:         ));
 405: 
 406:         match &self.operation {
 407:             MembershipOperation::AddNode(node) => {
 408:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.node_id={}\n", node.node_id));
 409:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.node_pubkey_hex={}\n", node.node_pubkey_hex));
 410:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.owner={}\n", node.owner));
 411:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.status={}\n", node.status.as_str()));
 412:                 let mut roles = node.roles.clone();
 413:                 roles.sort();
 414:                 roles.dedup();
 415:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.roles={}\n", roles.join(",")));
 416:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.joined_at_unix={}\n", node.joined_at_unix));
 417:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.updated_at_unix={}\n", node.updated_at_unix));
 418:             }
 419:             MembershipOperation::RemoveNode { node_id }
 420:             | MembershipOperation::RevokeNode { node_id }
 421:             | MembershipOperation::RestoreNode { node_id } => {
 422:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.node_id={node_id}\n"));
 423:             }
 424:             MembershipOperation::RotateNodeKey {
 425:                 node_id,
 426:                 new_pubkey_hex,
 427:             } => {
 428:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.node_id={node_id}\n"));
 429:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.new_pubkey_hex={new_pubkey_hex}\n"));
 430:             }
 431:             MembershipOperation::RotateApprover(approver) => {
 432:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.approver_id={}\n", approver.approver_id));
 433:                 out.push_str(&format!(
 434:                     "op.approver_pubkey_hex={}\n",
 435:                     approver.approver_pubkey_hex
 436:                 ));
 437:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.role={}\n", approver.role.as_str()));
 438:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.status={}\n", approver.status.as_str()));
 439:                 out.push_str(&format!(
 440:                     "op.created_at_unix={}\n",
 441:                     approver.created_at_unix
 442:                 ));
 443:             }
 444:             MembershipOperation::SetQuorum { threshold } => {
 445:                 out.push_str(&format!("op.threshold={threshold}\n"));
 446:             }
 447:         }
 448: 
 449:         Ok(out)
 450:     }
 451: }
 452: 
 453: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 454: pub struct MembershipSignature {
 455:     pub approver_id: String,
 456:     pub signature_hex: String,
 457: }
 458: 
 459: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 460: pub struct SignedMembershipUpdate {
 461:     pub record: MembershipUpdateRecord,
 462:     pub approver_signatures: Vec<MembershipSignature>,
 463: }
 464: 
 465: impl SignedMembershipUpdate {
 466:     pub fn canonical_envelope(&self) -> Result<String, MembershipError> {
 467:         let payload = self.record.canonical_payload()?;
 468:         let mut signatures = self.approver_signatures.clone();
 469:         signatures.sort_by(|left, right| left.approver_id.cmp(&right.approver_id));
 470: 
 471:         let mut out = String::new();
 472:         out.push_str(&format!("payload_hex={}\n", hex_encode(payload.as_bytes())));
 473:         out.push_str(&format!("sig_count={}\n", signatures.len()));
 474:         for (index, signature) in signatures.iter().enumerate() {
 475:             out.push_str(&format!(
 476:                 "sig.{index}.approver_id={}\n",
 477:                 signature.approver_id
 478:             ));
 479:             out.push_str(&format!(
 480:                 "sig.{index}.signature_hex={}\n",
 481:                 signature.signature_hex
 482:             ));
 483:         }
 484:         Ok(out)
 485:     }
 486: }
 487: 
 488: #[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
 489: pub struct MembershipReplayCache {
 490:     seen_update_ids: HashSet<String>,
 491:     max_epoch: u64,
 492: }
 493: 
 494: impl MembershipReplayCache {
 495:     pub fn observe(&mut self, update_id: &str, epoch_new: u64) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
 496:         if self.seen_update_ids.contains(update_id) {
 497:             return Err(MembershipError::ReplayDetected);
 498:         }
 499:         if epoch_new <= self.max_epoch {
 500:             return Err(MembershipError::ReplayDetected);
 501:         }
 502:         self.seen_update_ids.insert(update_id.to_string());
 503:         self.max_epoch = epoch_new;
 504:         Ok(())
 505:     }
 506: }
 507: 
 508: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 509: pub struct MembershipLogEntry {
 510:     pub index: u64,
 511:     pub previous_hash: String,
 512:     pub entry_hash: String,
 513:     pub signed_update: SignedMembershipUpdate,
 514: }
 515: 
 516: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 517: pub enum MembershipError {
 518:     UnsupportedVersion(u8),
 519:     InvalidFormat(String),
 520:     InvalidTransition(&'static str),
 521:     SignatureInvalid,
 522:     ThresholdNotMet,
 523:     SignerNotAuthorized(String),
 524:     OwnerSignatureRequired,
 525:     ReplayDetected,
 526:     Expired,
 527:     FutureDated,
 528:     PrevStateRootMismatch,
 529:     NewStateRootMismatch,
 530:     NotFound(String),
 531:     Internal(String),
 532:     Io(String),
 533:     IntegrityMismatch,
 534: }
 535: 
 536: impl fmt::Display for MembershipError {
 537:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
 538:         match self {
 539:             MembershipError::UnsupportedVersion(version) => {
 540:                 write!(f, "unsupported membership version {version}")
 541:             }
 542:             MembershipError::InvalidFormat(message) => {
 543:                 write!(f, "invalid membership format: {message}")
 544:             }
 545:             MembershipError::InvalidTransition(message) => {
 546:                 write!(f, "invalid transition: {message}")
 547:             }
 548:             MembershipError::SignatureInvalid => f.write_str("signature verification failed"),
 549:             MembershipError::ThresholdNotMet => {
 550:                 f.write_str("threshold signature requirements not met")
 551:             }
 552:             MembershipError::SignerNotAuthorized(approver_id) => {
 553:                 write!(f, "signer is not authorized: {approver_id}")
 554:             }
 555:             MembershipError::OwnerSignatureRequired => {
 556:                 f.write_str("owner signature required for this operation")
 557:             }
 558:             MembershipError::ReplayDetected => f.write_str("membership replay detected"),
 559:             MembershipError::Expired => f.write_str("membership update is expired"),
 560:             MembershipError::FutureDated => f.write_str("membership update is future dated"),
 561:             MembershipError::PrevStateRootMismatch => f.write_str("previous state root mismatch"),
 562:             MembershipError::NewStateRootMismatch => f.write_str("new state root mismatch"),
 563:             MembershipError::NotFound(value) => write!(f, "not found: {value}"),
 564:             MembershipError::Internal(message) => write!(f, "internal error: {message}"),
 565:             MembershipError::Io(message) => write!(f, "i/o error: {message}"),
 566:             MembershipError::IntegrityMismatch => f.write_str("integrity mismatch"),
 567:         }
 568:     }
 569: }
 570: 
 571: impl std::error::Error for MembershipError {}
 572: 
 573: pub fn encode_membership_state(state: &MembershipState) -> Result<String, MembershipError> {
 574:     state.canonical_payload()
 575: }
 576: 
 577: pub fn decode_membership_state(payload: &str) -> Result<MembershipState, MembershipError> {
 578:     let state = parse_membership_state_payload(payload)?;
 579:     state.validate()?;
 580:     Ok(state)
 581: }
 582: 
 583: pub fn encode_update_record(record: &MembershipUpdateRecord) -> Result<String, MembershipError> {
 584:     record.canonical_payload()
 585: }
 586: 
 587: pub fn decode_update_record(payload: &str) -> Result<MembershipUpdateRecord, MembershipError> {
 588:     parse_membership_update_payload(payload)
 589: }
 590: 
 591: pub fn encode_signed_update(
 592:     signed_update: &SignedMembershipUpdate,
 593: ) -> Result<String, MembershipError> {
 594:     signed_update.canonical_envelope()
 595: }
 596: 
 597: pub fn decode_signed_update(payload: &str) -> Result<SignedMembershipUpdate, MembershipError> {
 598:     parse_signed_update_envelope(payload)
 599: }
 600: 
 601: pub fn preview_next_state(
 602:     state: &MembershipState,
 603:     operation: &MembershipOperation,
 604: ) -> Result<MembershipState, MembershipError> {
 605:     state.validate()?;
 606:     let mut next = reduce_membership_state(state, operation)?;
 607:     next.epoch = state.epoch.saturating_add(1);
 608:     next.validate()?;
 609:     Ok(next)
 610: }
 611: 
 612: pub fn sign_update_record(
 613:     record: &MembershipUpdateRecord,
 614:     approver_id: &str,
 615:     signing_key: &SigningKey,
 616: ) -> Result<MembershipSignature, MembershipError> {
 617:     if approver_id.trim().is_empty() {
 618:         return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 619:             "approver_id must not be empty".to_string(),
 620:         ));
 621:     }
 622:     let payload = record.canonical_payload()?;
 623:     let signature = signing_key.sign(payload.as_bytes());
 624:     Ok(MembershipSignature {
 625:         approver_id: approver_id.to_string(),
 626:         signature_hex: hex_encode(&signature.to_bytes()),
 627:     })
 628: }
 629: 
 630: pub fn apply_signed_update(
 631:     state: &MembershipState,
 632:     signed_update: &SignedMembershipUpdate,
 633:     now_unix: u64,
 634:     replay_cache: &mut MembershipReplayCache,
 635: ) -> Result<MembershipState, MembershipError> {
 636:     state.validate()?;
 637:     let record = &signed_update.record;
 638:     let payload = record.canonical_payload()?;
 639:     if record.network_id != state.network_id {
 640:         return Err(MembershipError::InvalidTransition(
 641:             "network id mismatch in membership update",
 642:         ));
 643:     }
 644:     if now_unix > record.expires_at_unix {
 645:         return Err(MembershipError::Expired);
 646:     }
 647:     if record.created_at_unix > now_unix.saturating_add(MEMBERSHIP_CLOCK_SKEW_SECS) {
 648:         return Err(MembershipError::FutureDated);
 649:     }
 650:     if record.prev_state_root != state.state_root_hex()? {
 651:         return Err(MembershipError::PrevStateRootMismatch);
 652:     }
 653:     if record.epoch_prev != state.epoch || record.epoch_new != state.epoch.saturating_add(1) {
 654:         return Err(MembershipError::InvalidTransition(
 655:             "epoch chain mismatch for membership update",
 656:         ));
 657:     }
 658: 
 659:     verify_membership_signatures(state, signed_update, payload.as_bytes())?;
 660: 
 661:     let mut next = reduce_membership_state(state, &record.operation)?;
 662:     next.epoch = record.epoch_new;
 663:     next.validate()?;
 664:     let computed_new_root = next.state_root_hex()?;
 665:     if computed_new_root != record.new_state_root {
 666:         return Err(MembershipError::NewStateRootMismatch);
 667:     }
 668:     replay_cache.observe(&record.update_id, record.epoch_new)?;
 669: 
 670:     Ok(next)
 671: }
 672: 
 673: pub fn persist_membership_snapshot(
 674:     path: impl AsRef<Path>,
 675:     state: &MembershipState,
 676: ) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
 677:     let state_payload = state.canonical_payload()?;
 678:     let state_hex = hex_encode(state_payload.as_bytes());
 679:     let body_without_digest =
 680:         format!("version={MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION}\nstate_hex={state_hex}\n");
 681:     let digest = sha256_hex(body_without_digest.as_bytes());
 682:     let body = format!("{body_without_digest}digest={digest}\n");
 683:     atomic_write(path.as_ref(), body.as_bytes(), 0o600)
 684: }
 685: 
 686: pub fn load_membership_snapshot(
 687:     path: impl AsRef<Path>,
 688: ) -> Result<MembershipState, MembershipError> {
 689:     let path = path.as_ref();
 690:     validate_membership_file_security(path, "membership snapshot")?;
 691:     let content = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
 692:     let fields = parse_key_values(&content)?;
 693:     let version = parse_u8_field(&fields, "version")?;
 694:     if version != MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION {
 695:         return Err(MembershipError::UnsupportedVersion(version));
 696:     }
 697:     let state_hex = required_field(&fields, "state_hex")?;
 698:     let digest = required_field(&fields, "digest")?;
 699:     let expected_digest =
 700:         sha256_hex(format!("version={version}\nstate_hex={state_hex}\n").as_bytes());
 701:     if digest != expected_digest {
 702:         return Err(MembershipError::IntegrityMismatch);
 703:     }
 704:     let payload_bytes = hex_decode(state_hex)?;
 705:     let payload = String::from_utf8(payload_bytes)
 706:         .map_err(|_| MembershipError::InvalidFormat("snapshot payload is not utf8".to_string()))?;
 707:     let state = parse_membership_state_payload(&payload)?;
 708:     state.validate()?;
 709:     Ok(state)
 710: }
 711: 
 712: pub fn append_membership_log_entry(
 713:     path: impl AsRef<Path>,
 714:     signed_update: &SignedMembershipUpdate,
 715: ) -> Result<MembershipLogEntry, MembershipError> {
 716:     let path = path.as_ref();
 717:     let mut entries = if path.exists() {
 718:         load_membership_log(path)?
 719:     } else {
 720:         Vec::new()
 721:     };
 722:     let index = entries.len() as u64;
 723:     let previous_hash = entries
 724:         .last()
 725:         .map(|entry| entry.entry_hash.clone())
 726:         .unwrap_or_else(|| "genesis".to_string());
 727:     let encoded_update = signed_update.canonical_envelope()?;
 728:     let encoded_update_hex = hex_encode(encoded_update.as_bytes());
 729:     let entry_material = format!("{index}|{previous_hash}|{encoded_update_hex}");
 730:     let entry_hash = sha256_hex(entry_material.as_bytes());
 731:     let entry = MembershipLogEntry {
 732:         index,
 733:         previous_hash,
 734:         entry_hash,
 735:         signed_update: signed_update.clone(),
 736:     };
 737:     entries.push(entry.clone());
 738:     persist_membership_log(path, &entries)?;
 739:     Ok(entry)
 740: }
 741: 
 742: pub fn load_membership_log(
 743:     path: impl AsRef<Path>,
 744: ) -> Result<Vec<MembershipLogEntry>, MembershipError> {
 745:     let path = path.as_ref();
 746:     validate_membership_file_security(path, "membership log")?;
 747:     let content = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
 748:     let mut lines = content.lines();
 749:     let version_line = lines
 750:         .next()
 751:         .ok_or_else(|| MembershipError::InvalidFormat("missing log version".to_string()))?;
 752:     let Some((version_key, version_value)) = version_line.split_once('=') else {
 753:         return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 754:             "log version line missing separator".to_string(),
 755:         ));
 756:     };
 757:     if version_key != "version" {
 758:         return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 759:             "log first line must be version".to_string(),
 760:         ));
 761:     }
 762:     let version = version_value
 763:         .parse::<u8>()
 764:         .map_err(|_| MembershipError::InvalidFormat("invalid log version".to_string()))?;
 765:     if version != MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION {
 766:         return Err(MembershipError::UnsupportedVersion(version));
 767:     }
 768: 
 769:     let mut entries = Vec::new();
 770:     for line in lines {
 771:         let Some(encoded) = line.strip_prefix("entry=") else {
 772:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
 773:                 "unexpected log line: {line}"
 774:             )));
 775:         };
 776:         let parts = encoded.split('|').collect::<Vec<_>>();
 777:         if parts.len() != 4 {
 778:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 779:                 "log entry field count mismatch".to_string(),
 780:             ));
 781:         }
 782:         let index = parts[0]
 783:             .parse::<u64>()
 784:             .map_err(|_| MembershipError::InvalidFormat("invalid log entry index".to_string()))?;
 785:         let previous_hash = parts[1].to_string();
 786:         let entry_hash = parts[2].to_string();
 787:         let encoded_update_hex = parts[3];
 788:         let expected_hash =
 789:             sha256_hex(format!("{index}|{previous_hash}|{encoded_update_hex}").as_bytes());
 790:         if expected_hash != entry_hash {
 791:             return Err(MembershipError::IntegrityMismatch);
 792:         }
 793:         let encoded_update_raw = hex_decode(encoded_update_hex)?;
 794:         let encoded_update = String::from_utf8(encoded_update_raw).map_err(|_| {
 795:             MembershipError::InvalidFormat("encoded update payload is not utf8".to_string())
 796:         })?;
 797:         let signed_update = parse_signed_update_envelope(&encoded_update)?;
 798:         entries.push(MembershipLogEntry {
 799:             index,
 800:             previous_hash,
 801:             entry_hash,
 802:             signed_update,
 803:         });
 804:     }
 805: 
 806:     verify_membership_log_chain(&entries)?;
 807:     Ok(entries)
 808: }
 809: 
 810: pub fn replay_membership_snapshot_and_log(
 811:     snapshot: &MembershipState,
 812:     entries: &[MembershipLogEntry],
 813:     now_unix: u64,
 814: ) -> Result<MembershipState, MembershipError> {
 815:     let snapshot_root = snapshot.state_root_hex()?;
 816:     let mut state = snapshot.clone();
 817:     let mut replay_cache = MembershipReplayCache {
 818:         seen_update_ids: HashSet::new(),
 819:         max_epoch: snapshot.epoch,
 820:     };
 821:     let mut replay_started = false;
 822:     for entry in entries {
 823:         if !replay_started {
 824:             let record = &entry.signed_update.record;
 825:             if record.epoch_prev < snapshot.epoch {
 826:                 continue;
 827:             }
 828:             if record.epoch_prev != snapshot.epoch || record.prev_state_root != snapshot_root {
 829:                 return Err(MembershipError::PrevStateRootMismatch);
 830:             }
 831:             replay_started = true;
 832:         }
 833:         state = apply_signed_update(&state, &entry.signed_update, now_unix, &mut replay_cache)?;
 834:     }
 835:     Ok(state)
 836: }
 837: 
 838: pub fn write_membership_audit_log(
 839:     path: impl AsRef<Path>,
 840:     entries: &[MembershipLogEntry],
 841: ) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
 842:     let path = path.as_ref();
 843:     if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
 844:         fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
 845:     }
 846:     let mut out = String::new();
 847:     for entry in entries {
 848:         out.push_str(&format!(
 849:             "index={} previous_hash={} entry_hash={} update_id={} operation={} target={}\n",
 850:             entry.index,
 851:             entry.previous_hash,
 852:             entry.entry_hash,
 853:             entry.signed_update.record.update_id,
 854:             entry.signed_update.record.operation.operation_name(),
 855:             entry.signed_update.record.target
 856:         ));
 857:     }
 858:     fs::write(path, out).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))
 859: }
 860: 
 861: fn persist_membership_log(
 862:     path: &Path,
 863:     entries: &[MembershipLogEntry],
 864: ) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
 865:     verify_membership_log_chain(entries)?;
 866:     let mut out = format!("version={MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION}\n");
 867:     for entry in entries {
 868:         let update_envelope = entry.signed_update.canonical_envelope()?;
 869:         let update_hex = hex_encode(update_envelope.as_bytes());
 870:         out.push_str(&format!(
 871:             "entry={}|{}|{}|{}\n",
 872:             entry.index, entry.previous_hash, entry.entry_hash, update_hex
 873:         ));
 874:     }
 875:     atomic_write(path, out.as_bytes(), 0o600)
 876: }
 877: 
 878: fn verify_membership_log_chain(entries: &[MembershipLogEntry]) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
 879:     for (position, entry) in entries.iter().enumerate() {
 880:         if entry.index != position as u64 {
 881:             return Err(MembershipError::IntegrityMismatch);
 882:         }
 883:         let expected_previous = if position == 0 {
 884:             "genesis".to_string()
 885:         } else {
 886:             entries[position - 1].entry_hash.clone()
 887:         };
 888:         if entry.previous_hash != expected_previous {
 889:             return Err(MembershipError::IntegrityMismatch);
 890:         }
 891:         let update_envelope = entry.signed_update.canonical_envelope()?;
 892:         let update_hex = hex_encode(update_envelope.as_bytes());
 893:         let expected_hash = sha256_hex(
 894:             format!("{}|{}|{}", entry.index, entry.previous_hash, update_hex).as_bytes(),
 895:         );
 896:         if expected_hash != entry.entry_hash {
 897:             return Err(MembershipError::IntegrityMismatch);
 898:         }
 899:     }
 900:     Ok(())
 901: }
 902: 
 903: fn verify_membership_signatures(
 904:     state: &MembershipState,
 905:     signed_update: &SignedMembershipUpdate,
 906:     payload: &[u8],
 907: ) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
 908:     let active_approvers = state.active_approvers();
 909:     let mut signer_ids = BTreeSet::new();
 910:     for signature in &signed_update.approver_signatures {
 911:         if !signer_ids.insert(signature.approver_id.clone()) {
 912:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
 913:                 "duplicate signer id in update signatures".to_string(),
 914:             ));
 915:         }
 916:     }
 917:     if signer_ids.len() < usize::from(state.quorum_threshold) {
 918:         return Err(MembershipError::ThresholdNotMet);
 919:     }
 920: 
 921:     let mut owner_signed = false;
 922:     for signature in &signed_update.approver_signatures {
 923:         let approver = active_approvers
 924:             .get(&signature.approver_id)
 925:             .ok_or_else(|| MembershipError::SignerNotAuthorized(signature.approver_id.clone()))?;
 926:         if approver.role == MembershipApproverRole::Owner {
 927:             owner_signed = true;
 928:         }
 929:         let verifying_key =
 930:             VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(&approver.approver_pubkey_hex)?)
 931:                 .map_err(|_| MembershipError::SignatureInvalid)?;
 932:         let signature_bytes = decode_hex_to_fixed::<64>(&signature.signature_hex)?;
 933:         let signature_obj = Signature::from_bytes(&signature_bytes);
 934:         verifying_key
 935:             .verify(payload, &signature_obj)
 936:             .map_err(|_| MembershipError::SignatureInvalid)?;
 937:     }
 938: 
 939:     if signed_update.record.operation.requires_owner_signer() && !owner_signed {
 940:         return Err(MembershipError::OwnerSignatureRequired);
 941:     }
 942: 
 943:     Ok(())
 944: }
 945: 
 946: fn reduce_membership_state(
 947:     state: &MembershipState,
 948:     operation: &MembershipOperation,
 949: ) -> Result<MembershipState, MembershipError> {
 950:     let mut next = state.clone();
 951:     match operation {
 952:         MembershipOperation::AddNode(node) => {
 953:             if next
 954:                 .nodes
 955:                 .iter()
 956:                 .any(|candidate| candidate.node_id == node.node_id)
 957:             {
 958:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidTransition(
 959:                     "cannot add node that already exists",
 960:                 ));
 961:             }
 962:             decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(&node.node_pubkey_hex)?;
 963:             next.nodes.push(node.clone());
 964:         }
 965:         MembershipOperation::RemoveNode { node_id } => {
 966:             let before = next.nodes.len();
 967:             next.nodes.retain(|candidate| candidate.node_id != *node_id);
 968:             if next.nodes.len() == before {
 969:                 return Err(MembershipError::NotFound(format!("node {node_id}")));
 970:             }
 971:         }
 972:         MembershipOperation::RevokeNode { node_id } => {
 973:             let node = next
 974:                 .nodes
 975:                 .iter_mut()
 976:                 .find(|candidate| candidate.node_id == *node_id)
 977:                 .ok_or_else(|| MembershipError::NotFound(format!("node {node_id}")))?;
 978:             if node.status == MembershipNodeStatus::Revoked {
 979:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidTransition("node already revoked"));
 980:             }
 981:             node.status = MembershipNodeStatus::Revoked;
 982:             node.updated_at_unix = unix_now();
 983:         }
 984:         MembershipOperation::RestoreNode { node_id } => {
 985:             let node = next
 986:                 .nodes
 987:                 .iter_mut()
 988:                 .find(|candidate| candidate.node_id == *node_id)
 989:                 .ok_or_else(|| MembershipError::NotFound(format!("node {node_id}")))?;
 990:             if node.status == MembershipNodeStatus::Active {
 991:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidTransition("node already active"));
 992:             }
 993:             node.status = MembershipNodeStatus::Active;
 994:             node.updated_at_unix = unix_now();
 995:         }
 996:         MembershipOperation::RotateNodeKey {
 997:             node_id,
 998:             new_pubkey_hex,
 999:         } => {
1000:             decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(new_pubkey_hex)?;
1001:             let node = next
1002:                 .nodes
1003:                 .iter_mut()
1004:                 .find(|candidate| candidate.node_id == *node_id)
1005:                 .ok_or_else(|| MembershipError::NotFound(format!("node {node_id}")))?;
1006:             node.node_pubkey_hex = new_pubkey_hex.clone();
1007:             node.updated_at_unix = unix_now();
1008:         }
1009:         MembershipOperation::RotateApprover(approver) => {
1010:             decode_hex_to_fixed::<32>(&approver.approver_pubkey_hex)?;
1011:             if let Some(existing) = next
1012:                 .approver_set
1013:                 .iter_mut()
1014:                 .find(|candidate| candidate.approver_id == approver.approver_id)
1015:             {
1016:                 *existing = approver.clone();
1017:             } else {
1018:                 next.approver_set.push(approver.clone());
1019:             }
1020:         }
1021:         MembershipOperation::SetQuorum { threshold } => {
1022:             if *threshold == 0 {
1023:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidTransition(
1024:                     "quorum threshold must be at least one",
1025:                 ));
1026:             }
1027:             next.quorum_threshold = *threshold;
1028:         }
1029:     }
1030:     Ok(next)
1031: }
1032: 
1033: fn parse_membership_state_payload(payload: &str) -> Result<MembershipState, MembershipError> {
1034:     let fields = parse_key_values(payload)?;
1035:     let version = parse_u8_field(&fields, "version")?;
1036:     if version != MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION {
1037:         return Err(MembershipError::UnsupportedVersion(version));
1038:     }
1039:     let network_id = required_field(&fields, "network_id")?.to_string();
1040:     let epoch = parse_u64_field(&fields, "epoch")?;
1041:     let quorum_threshold = parse_u8_field(&fields, "quorum_threshold")?;
1042:     let metadata_hash_raw = required_field(&fields, "metadata_hash")?.to_string();
1043:     let metadata_hash = if metadata_hash_raw.is_empty() {
1044:         None
1045:     } else {
1046:         Some(metadata_hash_raw)
1047:     };
1048:     let node_count = parse_usize_field(&fields, "node_count")?;
1049:     let mut nodes = Vec::with_capacity(node_count);
1050:     for index in 0..node_count {
1051:         let node = MembershipNode {
1052:             node_id: required_field(&fields, &format!("node.{index}.node_id"))?.to_string(),
1053:             node_pubkey_hex: required_field(&fields, &format!("node.{index}.node_pubkey_hex"))?
1054:                 .to_string(),
1055:             owner: required_field(&fields, &format!("node.{index}.owner"))?.to_string(),
1056:             status: MembershipNodeStatus::parse(required_field(
1057:                 &fields,
1058:                 &format!("node.{index}.status"),
1059:             )?)?,
1060:             roles: split_csv(required_field(&fields, &format!("node.{index}.roles"))?),
1061:             joined_at_unix: parse_u64_field(&fields, &format!("node.{index}.joined_at_unix"))?,
1062:             updated_at_unix: parse_u64_field(&fields, &format!("node.{index}.updated_at_unix"))?,
1063:         };
1064:         nodes.push(node);
1065:     }
1066:     let approver_count = parse_usize_field(&fields, "approver_count")?;
1067:     let mut approver_set = Vec::with_capacity(approver_count);
1068:     for index in 0..approver_count {
1069:         let approver = MembershipApprover {
1070:             approver_id: required_field(&fields, &format!("approver.{index}.approver_id"))?
1071:                 .to_string(),
1072:             approver_pubkey_hex: required_field(
1073:                 &fields,
1074:                 &format!("approver.{index}.approver_pubkey_hex"),
1075:             )?
1076:             .to_string(),
1077:             role: MembershipApproverRole::parse(required_field(
1078:                 &fields,
1079:                 &format!("approver.{index}.role"),
1080:             )?)?,
1081:             status: MembershipApproverStatus::parse(required_field(
1082:                 &fields,
1083:                 &format!("approver.{index}.status"),
1084:             )?)?,
1085:             created_at_unix: parse_u64_field(
1086:                 &fields,
1087:                 &format!("approver.{index}.created_at_unix"),
1088:             )?,
1089:         };
1090:         approver_set.push(approver);
1091:     }
1092: 
1093:     Ok(MembershipState {
1094:         schema_version: version,
1095:         network_id,
1096:         epoch,
1097:         nodes,
1098:         approver_set,
1099:         quorum_threshold,
1100:         metadata_hash,
1101:     })
1102: }
1103: 
1104: fn parse_signed_update_envelope(value: &str) -> Result<SignedMembershipUpdate, MembershipError> {
1105:     let fields = parse_key_values(value)?;
1106:     let payload_hex = required_field(&fields, "payload_hex")?;
1107:     let payload_raw = hex_decode(payload_hex)?;
1108:     let payload = String::from_utf8(payload_raw)
1109:         .map_err(|_| MembershipError::InvalidFormat("update payload is not utf8".to_string()))?;
1110:     let record = parse_membership_update_payload(&payload)?;
1111: 
1112:     let sig_count = parse_usize_field(&fields, "sig_count")?;
1113:     let mut signatures = Vec::with_capacity(sig_count);
1114:     for index in 0..sig_count {
1115:         signatures.push(MembershipSignature {
1116:             approver_id: required_field(&fields, &format!("sig.{index}.approver_id"))?.to_string(),
1117:             signature_hex: required_field(&fields, &format!("sig.{index}.signature_hex"))?
1118:                 .to_string(),
1119:         });
1120:     }
1121:     Ok(SignedMembershipUpdate {
1122:         record,
1123:         approver_signatures: signatures,
1124:     })
1125: }
1126: 
1127: fn parse_membership_update_payload(
1128:     payload: &str,
1129: ) -> Result<MembershipUpdateRecord, MembershipError> {
1130:     let fields = parse_key_values(payload)?;
1131:     let version = parse_u8_field(&fields, "version")?;
1132:     if version != MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION {
1133:         return Err(MembershipError::UnsupportedVersion(version));
1134:     }
1135:     let operation_name = required_field(&fields, "operation")?;
1136:     let operation = match operation_name {
1137:         "add_node" => MembershipOperation::AddNode(MembershipNode {
1138:             node_id: required_field(&fields, "op.node_id")?.to_string(),
1139:             node_pubkey_hex: required_field(&fields, "op.node_pubkey_hex")?.to_string(),
1140:             owner: required_field(&fields, "op.owner")?.to_string(),
1141:             status: MembershipNodeStatus::parse(required_field(&fields, "op.status")?)?,
1142:             roles: split_csv(required_field(&fields, "op.roles")?),
1143:             joined_at_unix: parse_u64_field(&fields, "op.joined_at_unix")?,
1144:             updated_at_unix: parse_u64_field(&fields, "op.updated_at_unix")?,
1145:         }),
1146:         "remove_node" => MembershipOperation::RemoveNode {
1147:             node_id: required_field(&fields, "op.node_id")?.to_string(),
1148:         },
1149:         "revoke_node" => MembershipOperation::RevokeNode {
1150:             node_id: required_field(&fields, "op.node_id")?.to_string(),
1151:         },
1152:         "restore_node" => MembershipOperation::RestoreNode {
1153:             node_id: required_field(&fields, "op.node_id")?.to_string(),
1154:         },
1155:         "rotate_node_key" => MembershipOperation::RotateNodeKey {
1156:             node_id: required_field(&fields, "op.node_id")?.to_string(),
1157:             new_pubkey_hex: required_field(&fields, "op.new_pubkey_hex")?.to_string(),
1158:         },
1159:         "rotate_approver" => MembershipOperation::RotateApprover(MembershipApprover {
1160:             approver_id: required_field(&fields, "op.approver_id")?.to_string(),
1161:             approver_pubkey_hex: required_field(&fields, "op.approver_pubkey_hex")?.to_string(),
1162:             role: MembershipApproverRole::parse(required_field(&fields, "op.role")?)?,
1163:             status: MembershipApproverStatus::parse(required_field(&fields, "op.status")?)?,
1164:             created_at_unix: parse_u64_field(&fields, "op.created_at_unix")?,
1165:         }),
1166:         "set_quorum" => MembershipOperation::SetQuorum {
1167:             threshold: parse_u8_field(&fields, "op.threshold")?,
1168:         },
1169:         _ => {
1170:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1171:                 "unknown update operation {operation_name}"
1172:             )));
1173:         }
1174:     };
1175: 
1176:     let policy_context_raw = required_field(&fields, "policy_context")?.to_string();
1177:     Ok(MembershipUpdateRecord {
1178:         network_id: required_field(&fields, "network_id")?.to_string(),
1179:         update_id: required_field(&fields, "update_id")?.to_string(),
1180:         operation,
1181:         target: required_field(&fields, "target")?.to_string(),
1182:         prev_state_root: required_field(&fields, "prev_state_root")?.to_string(),
1183:         new_state_root: required_field(&fields, "new_state_root")?.to_string(),
1184:         epoch_prev: parse_u64_field(&fields, "epoch_prev")?,
1185:         epoch_new: parse_u64_field(&fields, "epoch_new")?,
1186:         created_at_unix: parse_u64_field(&fields, "created_at_unix")?,
1187:         expires_at_unix: parse_u64_field(&fields, "expires_at_unix")?,
1188:         reason_code: required_field(&fields, "reason_code")?.to_string(),
1189:         policy_context: if policy_context_raw.is_empty() {
1190:             None
1191:         } else {
1192:             Some(policy_context_raw)
1193:         },
1194:     })
1195: }
1196: 
1197: fn atomic_write(path: &Path, body: &[u8], _mode: u32) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
1198:     if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
1199:         if parent.exists() {
1200:             let metadata =
1201:                 fs::symlink_metadata(parent).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1202:             if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() || !metadata.file_type().is_dir() {
1203:                 return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1204:                     "membership parent path {} must be a real directory",
1205:                     parent.display()
1206:                 )));
1207:             }
1208:         }
1209:         fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1210:         #[cfg(unix)]
1211:         {
1212:             fs::set_permissions(parent, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700))
1213:                 .map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1214:         }
1215:     }
1216:     if path.exists() {
1217:         let metadata =
1218:             fs::symlink_metadata(path).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1219:         if metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
1220:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1221:                 "membership target {} must not be a symlink",
1222:                 path.display()
1223:             )));
1224:         }
1225:     }
1226:     let temp_path = path.with_extension(format!(
1227:         "tmp.{}.{}",
1228:         std::process::id(),
1229:         SystemTime::now()
1230:             .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
1231:             .map(|duration| duration.as_nanos())
1232:             .unwrap_or(0)
1233:     ));
1234:     let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new();
1235:     options.write(true).create_new(true);
1236:     #[cfg(unix)]
1237:     {
1238:         use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
1239:         options.mode(_mode);
1240:     }
1241:     let mut temp = options
1242:         .open(&temp_path)
1243:         .map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1244:     if let Err(err) = temp.write_all(body) {
1245:         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp_path);
1246:         return Err(MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()));
1247:     }
1248:     if let Err(err) = temp.sync_all() {
1249:         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp_path);
1250:         return Err(MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()));
1251:     }
1252:     if let Err(err) = fs::rename(&temp_path, path) {
1253:         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp_path);
1254:         return Err(MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()));
1255:     }
1256:     #[cfg(unix)]
1257:     if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
1258:         let parent_dir =
1259:             fs::File::open(parent).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1260:         parent_dir
1261:             .sync_all()
1262:             .map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1263:     }
1264:     validate_membership_file_security(path, "membership state file")?;
1265:     Ok(())
1266: }
1267: 
1268: fn validate_membership_file_security(path: &Path, label: &str) -> Result<(), MembershipError> {
1269:     #[cfg(unix)]
1270:     {
1271:         let link_metadata =
1272:             fs::symlink_metadata(path).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1273:         if link_metadata.file_type().is_symlink() || !link_metadata.file_type().is_file() {
1274:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1275:                 "{label} path must be a regular file and must not be a symlink"
1276:             )));
1277:         }
1278:         let metadata = fs::metadata(path).map_err(|err| MembershipError::Io(err.to_string()))?;
1279:         let mode = metadata.mode() & 0o777;
1280:         if mode & 0o077 != 0 {
1281:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1282:                 "{label} permissions are too broad: {mode:o}"
1283:             )));
1284:         }
1285:         let owner_uid = metadata.uid();
1286:         let expected_uid = Uid::effective().as_raw();
1287:         if owner_uid != expected_uid {
1288:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1289:                 "{label} owner uid mismatch: expected {expected_uid}, got {owner_uid}"
1290:             )));
1291:         }
1292:     }
1293:     #[cfg(not(unix))]
1294:     {
1295:         let _ = (path, label);
1296:     }
1297:     Ok(())
1298: }
1299: 
1300: fn required_field<'a>(
1301:     fields: &'a HashMap<String, String>,
1302:     key: &str,
1303: ) -> Result<&'a str, MembershipError> {
1304:     fields
1305:         .get(key)
1306:         .map(String::as_str)
1307:         .ok_or_else(|| MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!("missing field {key}")))
1308: }
1309: 
1310: fn parse_key_values(value: &str) -> Result<HashMap<String, String>, MembershipError> {
1311:     let mut fields = HashMap::new();
1312:     for line in value.lines() {
1313:         if line.trim().is_empty() {
1314:             continue;
1315:         }
1316:         let Some((key, field_value)) = line.split_once('=') else {
1317:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
1318:                 "line missing key/value separator".to_string(),
1319:             ));
1320:         };
1321:         if fields
1322:             .insert(key.to_string(), field_value.to_string())
1323:             .is_some()
1324:         {
1325:             return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1326:                 "duplicate field {key}"
1327:             )));
1328:         }
1329:     }
1330:     Ok(fields)
1331: }
1332: 
1333: fn parse_u8_field(fields: &HashMap<String, String>, key: &str) -> Result<u8, MembershipError> {
1334:     required_field(fields, key)?
1335:         .parse::<u8>()
1336:         .map_err(|_| MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!("invalid u8 field {key}")))
1337: }
1338: 
1339: fn parse_u64_field(fields: &HashMap<String, String>, key: &str) -> Result<u64, MembershipError> {
1340:     required_field(fields, key)?
1341:         .parse::<u64>()
1342:         .map_err(|_| MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!("invalid u64 field {key}")))
1343: }
1344: 
1345: fn parse_usize_field(
1346:     fields: &HashMap<String, String>,
1347:     key: &str,
1348: ) -> Result<usize, MembershipError> {
1349:     required_field(fields, key)?
1350:         .parse::<usize>()
1351:         .map_err(|_| MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!("invalid usize field {key}")))
1352: }
1353: 
1354: fn split_csv(value: &str) -> Vec<String> {
1355:     value
1356:         .split(',')
1357:         .map(str::trim)
1358:         .filter(|entry| !entry.is_empty())
1359:         .map(ToString::to_string)
1360:         .collect()
1361: }
1362: 
1363: fn hex_encode(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
1364:     let mut out = String::with_capacity(bytes.len() * 2);
1365:     for byte in bytes {
1366:         out.push_str(&format!("{byte:02x}"));
1367:     }
1368:     out
1369: }
1370: 
1371: fn hex_decode(encoded: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MembershipError> {
1372:     let trimmed = encoded.trim();
1373:     if (trimmed.len() & 1) != 0 {
1374:         return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
1375:             "hex value has odd length".to_string(),
1376:         ));
1377:     }
1378:     let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(trimmed.len() / 2);
1379:     let raw = trimmed.as_bytes();
1380:     let mut index = 0usize;
1381:     while index < raw.len() {
1382:         let hi = decode_hex_nibble(raw[index])?;
1383:         let lo = decode_hex_nibble(raw[index + 1])?;
1384:         out.push((hi << 4) | lo);
1385:         index += 2;
1386:     }
1387:     Ok(out)
1388: }
1389: 
1390: fn decode_hex_to_fixed<const N: usize>(encoded: &str) -> Result<[u8; N], MembershipError> {
1391:     let mut bytes = [0u8; N];
1392:     let decoded = hex_decode(encoded)?;
1393:     if decoded.len() != N {
1394:         return Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(format!(
1395:             "hex value has invalid length: expected {}, got {}",
1396:             N,
1397:             decoded.len()
1398:         )));
1399:     }
1400:     bytes.copy_from_slice(decoded.as_slice());
1401:     Ok(bytes)
1402: }
1403: 
1404: fn decode_hex_nibble(value: u8) -> Result<u8, MembershipError> {
1405:     match value {
1406:         b'0'..=b'9' => Ok(value - b'0'),
1407:         b'a'..=b'f' => Ok(value - b'a' + 10),
1408:         b'A'..=b'F' => Ok(value - b'A' + 10),
1409:         _ => Err(MembershipError::InvalidFormat(
1410:             "invalid hex character".to_string(),
1411:         )),
1412:     }
1413: }
1414: 
1415: fn sha256_hex(input: &[u8]) -> String {
1416:     let digest = Sha256::digest(input);
1417:     hex_encode(digest.as_slice())
1418: }
1419: 
1420: fn unix_now() -> u64 {
1421:     SystemTime::now()
1422:         .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
1423:         .map(|duration| duration.as_secs())
1424:         .unwrap_or(0)
1425: }

1445:             role,

1447:             created_at_unix: 100,
1448:         }
1449:     }

1455:             owner: "owner@example.local".to_string(),
1456:             status: MembershipNodeStatus::Active,
1457:             roles: vec!["tag:servers".to_string()],
1458:             joined_at_unix: 100,
1459:             updated_at_unix: 100,

1472:                 approver("guardian-2", 3, MembershipApproverRole::Guardian),
1473:             ],
1474:             quorum_threshold: 2,
1475:             metadata_hash: None,
1476:         }

1480:     fn canonical_state_and_root_are_deterministic() {
1481:         let mut first = base_state();
1482:         first.nodes.push(active_node("node-b", 7));
1483:         first
1484:             .nodes
1485:             .sort_by(|left, right| right.node_id.cmp(&left.node_id));
1486: 
1487:         let mut second = base_state();
1488:         second.nodes.push(active_node("node-b", 7));
1489: 
1490:         let first_payload = first.canonical_payload().expect("payload should build");
1491:         let second_payload = second.canonical_payload().expect("payload should build");
1492:         assert_eq!(first_payload, second_payload);
1493:         assert_eq!(
1494:             first.state_root_hex().expect("root should build"),
1495:             second.state_root_hex().expect("root should build")
1496:         );
1497:     }

1657:     fn owner_signature_required_for_rotate_approver() {
1658:         let state = base_state();
1659:         let replacement = MembershipApprover {
1660:             approver_id: "guardian-3".to_string(),
1661:             approver_pubkey_hex: hex_encode(

1663:             ),

1668:

1670:         candidate.approver_set.push(replacement.clone());
1671:         candidate.epoch += 1;
1672:         let record = MembershipUpdateRecord {
1673:             network_id: state.network_id.clone(),
1674:             update_id: "update-rotate-approver".to_string(),
1675:             operation: MembershipOperation::RotateApprover(replacement),
1676:             target: "guardian-3".to_string(),
1677:             prev_state_root: state.state_root_hex().expect("root"),
1678:             new_state_root: candidate.state_root_hex().expect("root"),
1679:             epoch_prev: state.epoch,
1680:             epoch_new: state.epoch + 1,
1681:             created_at_unix: 140,
1682:             expires_at_unix: 640,
1683:             reason_code: "rotate".to_string(),
1684:             policy_context: None,
1685:         };
1686:         let guardian_one = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[2; 32]);
1687:         let guardian_two = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[3; 32]);
1688:         let signed = SignedMembershipUpdate {
1689:             record: record.clone(),
1690:             approver_signatures: vec![
1691:                 sign_update_record(&record, "guardian-1", &guardian_one).expect("sign"),
1692:                 sign_update_record(&record, "guardian-2", &guardian_two).expect("sign"),
1693:             ],
1694:         };
1695: 
1696:         let err = apply_signed_update(&state, &signed, 150, &mut MembershipReplayCache::default())
1697:             .expect_err("owner signature should be required");
1698:         assert_eq!(err, MembershipError::OwnerSignatureRequired);
1699:     }
1700: 
1701:     #[test]
1702:     fn replay_cache_not_updated_on_failed_update() {
1703:         let state = base_state();
1704:         let new_node = active_node("node-b", 12);
1705: 
1706:         let mut candidate = state.clone();
1707:         candidate.nodes.push(new_node.clone());
1708:         candidate.epoch += 1;
1709:         let base_record = MembershipUpdateRecord {
1710:             network_id: state.network_id.clone(),
1711:             update_id: "update-retry".to_string(),
1712:             operation: MembershipOperation::AddNode(new_node.clone()),
1713:             target: "node-b".to_string(),
1714:             prev_state_root: state.state_root_hex().expect("root"),
1715:             new_state_root: candidate.state_root_hex().expect("root"),
1716:             epoch_prev: state.epoch,
1717:             epoch_new: state.epoch + 1,
1718:             created_at_unix: 120,
1719:             expires_at_unix: 600,
1720:             reason_code: "join".to_string(),
1721:             policy_context: None,
1722:         };
1723: 
1724:         let owner_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1; 32]);
1725:         let guardian_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[2; 32]);
1726: 
1727:         let mut bad_record = base_record.clone();
1728:         bad_record.new_state_root = "deadbeef".to_string();
1729:         let bad_signed = SignedMembershipUpdate {
1730:             record: bad_record.clone(),
1731:             approver_signatures: vec![
1732:                 sign_update_record(&bad_record, "owner-1", &owner_key).expect("sign"),
1733:                 sign_update_record(&bad_record, "guardian-1", &guardian_key).expect("sign"),
1734:             ],
1735:         };
1736: 
1737:         let mut cache = MembershipReplayCache::default();
1738:         let err = apply_signed_update(&state, &bad_signed, 130, &mut cache)
1739:             .expect_err("bad root should fail");
1740:         assert_eq!(err, MembershipError::NewStateRootMismatch);
1741: 
1742:         let good_signed = SignedMembershipUpdate {
1743:             record: base_record.clone(),
1744:             approver_signatures: vec![
1745:                 sign_update_record(&base_record, "owner-1", &owner_key).expect("sign"),
1746:                 sign_update_record(&base_record, "guardian-1", &guardian_key).expect("sign"),
1747:             ],
1748:         };
1749: 
1750:         let applied = apply_signed_update(&state, &good_signed, 131, &mut cache)
1751:             .expect("valid update should still apply after failed attempt");
1752:         assert_eq!(applied.epoch, state.epoch + 1);
1753:     }

1756:     fn loading_empty_membership_log_is_supported() {
1757:         let unique = format!(
1758:             "membership-empty-log-{}",
1759:             std::time::SystemTime::now()
1760:                 .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
1761:                 .expect("clock should be valid")
1762:                 .as_nanos()
1763:         );

1765:         std::fs::create_dir_all(&temp_dir).expect("temp dir should be created");
1766:         let log = temp_dir.join("membership.log");
1767:         std::fs::write(&log, format!("version={MEMBERSHIP_SCHEMA_VERSION}\n"))
1768:             .expect("empty log should be written");
1769: 
1770:         #[cfg(unix)]
1771:         {
1772:             use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
1773:             let mut perms = std::fs::metadata(&log).unwrap().permissions();
1774:             perms.set_mode(0o600);
1775:             std::fs::set_permissions(&log, perms).unwrap();
1776:         }
1777: 
1778:         let entries = load_membership_log(&log).expect("empty log should load");
1779:         assert!(entries.is_empty());
1780: 
1781:         let _ = std::fs::remove_file(log);
1782:         let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(temp_dir);
1783:     }

1786:     fn revoke_and_restore_update_timestamp() {
1787:         let state = base_state();
1788:         let original_updated = state
1789:             .nodes
1790:             .iter()
1791:             .find(|node| node.node_id == "node-a")
1792:             .expect("node should exist")
1793:             .updated_at_unix;

1800:         )
1801:         .expect("revoke should succeed");

1804:             .iter()
1805:             .find(|node| node.node_id == "node-a")
1806:             .expect("node should exist");

1809:

1813:                 node_id: "node-a".to_string(),

1815:         )
1816:         .expect("restore should succeed");
1817:         let restored_node = restored
1818:             .nodes
1819:             .iter()
1820:             .find(|node| node.node_id == "node-a")
1821:             .expect("node should exist");
1822:         assert_eq!(restored_node.status, MembershipNodeStatus::Active);
1823:         assert!(restored_node.updated_at_unix >= revoked_node.updated_at_unix);

1826:     #[test]
1827:     fn snapshot_and_log_roundtrip_integrity() {
1828:         let unique = format!(
1829:             "membership-store-{}",
1830:             std::time::SystemTime::now()
1831:                 .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
1832:                 .expect("clock should be valid")
1833:                 .as_nanos()
1834:         );
1835:         let temp_dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(unique);
1836:         std::fs::create_dir_all(&temp_dir).expect("temp dir should be created");
1837:         let snapshot = temp_dir.join("membership.snapshot");
1838:         let log = temp_dir.join("membership.log");
1839:         let audit = temp_dir.join("membership.audit.log");
1840: 
1841:         let state = base_state();
1842:         persist_membership_snapshot(&snapshot, &state).expect("snapshot should persist");
1843:         let loaded_snapshot = load_membership_snapshot(&snapshot).expect("snapshot should load");
1844:         assert_eq!(
1845:             loaded_snapshot.state_root_hex().expect("snapshot root"),
1846:             state.state_root_hex().expect("original root")
1847:         );
1848: 
1849:         let new_node = active_node("node-b", 17);
1850:         let mut candidate = state.clone();
1851:         candidate.nodes.push(new_node.clone());
1852:         candidate.epoch += 1;
1853:         let record = MembershipUpdateRecord {
1854:             network_id: state.network_id.clone(),
1855:             update_id: "update-log-1".to_string(),
1856:             operation: MembershipOperation::AddNode(new_node),
1857:             target: "node-b".to_string(),
1858:             prev_state_root: state.state_root_hex().expect("root"),
1859:             new_state_root: candidate.state_root_hex().expect("root"),
1860:             epoch_prev: state.epoch,
1861:             epoch_new: state.epoch + 1,
1862:             created_at_unix: 130,
1863:             expires_at_unix: 700,
1864:             reason_code: "join".to_string(),
1865:             policy_context: None,
1866:         };
1867:         let owner_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[1; 32]);
1868:         let guardian_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[2; 32]);
1869:         let signed = SignedMembershipUpdate {
1870:             record: record.clone(),
1871:             approver_signatures: vec![
1872:                 sign_update_record(&record, "owner-1", &owner_key).expect("sign"),
1873:                 sign_update_record(&record, "guardian-1", &guardian_key).expect("sign"),
1874:             ],
1875:         };
1876: 
1877:         append_membership_log_entry(&log, &signed).expect("log should append");
1878:         let entries = load_membership_log(&log).expect("log should load");
1879:         write_membership_audit_log(&audit, &entries).expect("audit should write");
1880:         assert_eq!(entries.len(), 1);
1881: 
1882:         let replayed =
1883:             replay_membership_snapshot_and_log(&loaded_snapshot, &entries, 140).expect("replay");
1884:         assert!(replayed.nodes.iter().any(|node| node.node_id == "node-b"));
1885:         let replayed_from_checkpoint = replay_membership_snapshot_and_log(&replayed, &entries, 141)
1886:             .expect("checkpoint replay should ignore historical entries");
1887:         assert_eq!(
1888:             replayed_from_checkpoint
1889:                 .state_root_hex()
1890:                 .expect("checkpoint replay root"),
1891:             replayed.state_root_hex().expect("replayed root")
1892:         );
1893: 
1894:         let mut tampered = std::fs::read_to_string(&log).expect("log should read");
1895:         tampered = tampered.replace("entry=0|", "entry=1|");
1896:         std::fs::write(&log, tampered).expect("tampered log should write");
1897:         let bad = load_membership_log(&log);
1898:         assert!(bad.is_err());
1899:     }
1900: }

===== /crates/rustynet-control/src/operations.rs =====

   1: #![forbid(unsafe_code)]
   2: 
   3: use std::collections::BTreeMap;
   4: use std::fmt;
   5: use std::path::Path;
   6: use std::sync::Mutex;
   7: 
   8: use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
   9: 
  10: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  11: pub enum IngestionPath {
  12:     Mdm,
  13:     EnvVar,
  14:     CliArg,
  15:     ApiPayload,
  16:     UiForm,
  17:     LogField,
  18: }
  19: 
  20: pub fn redact_fields(
  21:     _path: IngestionPath,
  22:     input: &BTreeMap<String, String>,
  23: ) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
  24:     let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
  25:     for (key, value) in input {
  26:         if is_sensitive_key(key) || looks_sensitive_value(value) {
  27:             out.insert(key.clone(), "REDACTED".to_string());
  28:         } else {
  29:             out.insert(key.clone(), value.clone());
  30:         }
  31:     }
  32:     out
  33: }
  34: 
  35: fn is_sensitive_key(key: &str) -> bool {
  36:     let lowered = key.to_ascii_lowercase();
  37:     [
  38:         "token",
  39:         "secret",
  40:         "password",
  41:         "passphrase",
  42:         "credential",
  43:         "private_key",
  44:         "nonce",
  45:     ]
  46:     .iter()
  47:     .any(|needle| lowered.contains(needle))
  48: }
  49: 
  50: fn looks_sensitive_value(value: &str) -> bool {
  51:     let lowered = value.to_ascii_lowercase();
  52:     lowered.contains("bearer ")
  53:         || lowered.starts_with("sk_")
  54:         || lowered.starts_with("vault://")
  55:         || lowered.contains("-----begin")
  56: }
  57: 
  58: #[derive(Debug, Default)]
  59: pub struct StructuredLogger {
  60:     lines: Mutex<Vec<String>>,
  61: }
  62: 
  63: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
  64: pub enum OperationsError {
  65:     Internal,
  66:     Io,
  67:     IntegrityMismatch,
  68:     InvalidFormat,
  69: }
  70: 
  71: impl fmt::Display for OperationsError {
  72:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
  73:         match self {
  74:             OperationsError::Internal => f.write_str("internal operations error"),
  75:             OperationsError::Io => f.write_str("i/o error"),
  76:             OperationsError::IntegrityMismatch => f.write_str("integrity mismatch"),
  77:             OperationsError::InvalidFormat => f.write_str("invalid format"),
  78:         }
  79:     }
  80: }
  81: 
  82: impl std::error::Error for OperationsError {}
  83: 
  84: impl StructuredLogger {
  85:     pub fn log(
  86:         &self,
  87:         path: IngestionPath,
  88:         fields: &BTreeMap<String, String>,
  89:     ) -> Result<(), OperationsError> {
  90:         let redacted = redact_fields(path, fields);
  91:         let mut encoded = String::from("{");
  92:         let mut first = true;
  93:         for (key, value) in &redacted {
  94:             if !first {
  95:                 encoded.push(',');
  96:             }
  97:             first = false;
  98:             encoded.push('"');
  99:             encoded.push_str(key);
 100:             encoded.push_str("\":\"");
 101:             encoded.push_str(value);
 102:             encoded.push('"');
 103:         }
 104:         encoded.push('}');
 105: 
 106:         let mut guard = self.lines.lock().map_err(|_| OperationsError::Internal)?;
 107:         guard.push(encoded);
 108:         Ok(())
 109:     }
 110: 
 111:     pub fn lines(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>, OperationsError> {
 112:         let guard = self.lines.lock().map_err(|_| OperationsError::Internal)?;
 113:         Ok(guard.clone())
 114:     }
 115: }
 116: 
 117: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 118: pub struct HealthSnapshot {
 119:     pub component: String,
 120:     pub healthy: bool,
 121:     pub detail: String,
 122:     pub timestamp_unix: u64,
 123: }
